# V7 Lot 75 — Arena II Implementation Plan (Part 1: L75-01 … L75-08, L75-13)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Finish the V7 arena behind `?ui=next` before the default flip. Part 1 does five things:
- it fixes the hint and tutorial copy;
- it renders the desktop and landscape-compact layouts;
- it compacts the portrait bottom band, so the plate never covers a seat on a small phone;
- it pins every FX endpoint to an arena element in all three modes;
- it hosts the tutorial (coach, callouts, tour, Look gate, every commit path gated) and the first-game hints on the arena.

**Architecture:**
- One layout mode per render, `seatsModel.layout.mode`. It comes from the visible box through the L73 rule (`viewportModeFor`), and `<main data-layout-mode>` exposes it.
  - The six regions stay direct children of `<main>` in `ARENA_REGIONS` order in every mode. A mode changes only their classes (CSS grid areas) and `data-arena-row`, so no region remounts on a rotation (Appendix A.3).
- Desktop:
  - the `ticker` region hosts a collapsible log panel;
  - the card, Shop and seat sheets resolve `placement="auto"` to centered dialogs;
  - seats are drawn at their per-mode box.
- Landscape-compact: a height-bound hand size helper in `ui/card/` sets the hand size.
- Portrait: one pure geometry helper (`bottom-band.ts`) decides when the bottom band compacts.
- The tutorial and the hints are arena hooks (`useArenaTutorial`, `useArenaHints`) plus two hosts that portal to `document.body`, on the new `Z_INDEX.coach` and `Z_INDEX.hint` layers.
  - The tutorial gate is composed into the commit helper's `allow`, beside the reconnect lock.
  - The hint causes ride the helper's `onSent`. `view-model/` stays pure.
- The old `TableScreen` stays the default until L75-09 (Part 2). Part 1 deletes nothing from the old table.

**Tech Stack:** React 19 · TypeScript (strict) · Vitest 4 · @testing-library/react + user-event on happy-dom (`renderUi`, `apps/client/src/test/render.tsx`) · motion · the L72 `ui/` design system. No new dependency.

**Spec:** `docs/technical_spec_v7.md` is normative: §5.4, §6.1, §6.3, §6.4, §6.6.3–§6.6.5, §6.7, §6.8, §6.9, §6.12, §7.7, §8.1–§8.3, §10, §10.1, §10.2, §10.4, §11, §12, §14.3. Appendix A is `docs/technical_spec_v7_parity.md`. Tracker: `docs/backlog_v7.md`, Lot 75. **Part 2** (L75-09 … L75-12: the flip, deleting the old table, the gate) is planned on the real code once Part 1 is merged into this branch. One branch (`claude/v7-lot-75`), one PR into `dev` at the end of Part 2 (L75-12).

> Generated 2026-11-03 by the same method as Lot 74:
> - the controller mapped the code (`map-layout-fx.md`, `map-tutorial-hints.md`, HEAD `c6941bec`) and wrote binding rulings R1–R16 (`controller-rulings-p1.md`);
> - the contract (Appendix A) was fixed;
> - three domain planners wrote the tasks: A copy and hints, B layouts and FX, C tutorial;
> - a consistency pass and a critic followed.
>
> Each task ends with **Decisions**: choices the executor cannot derive from the spec. They bind unless the designer overrides them in review (Appendix B). When a brief and the spec disagree, the spec wins; when a brief and the code disagree on a fact, the code wins. The owning task states each such correction.

## Global Constraints

- **Client-only (§1, §15 row 75; golden rule 7).**
  - No server, engine or protocol change. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays 45, and no Classic rule or value changes. A task that finds a real need stops and asks.
  - The only `packages/shared` edits are L75-01's copy edits (spec v7 §10, §10.2, §10.4). They touch the tutorial `script.ts` / `tour.ts` copy, which never goes on the wire, and add one `Fixed some bugs.` item to `release-notes.ts`.
- **Opt-in only (§4.4; R9).**
  - The arena still renders only behind `?ui=next`. **L75-04** extends the opt-in to tutorial rooms: `resolveTableUi` stops forcing `classic` for `playKind === 'tutorial'`. That is the only Part 1 `App.tsx` edit, a comment line.
  - The default stays the classic table until L75-09. The L75-05 brief's `App.tsx` file entry and its "Stop ignoring tutorial rooms" sentence move to L75-04.
- **Import rule (L74-01; `frontend.md` › Arena).**
  - `screens/arena/` imports only from `ui/`, `view-model/`, `fx/`, `net/` (types), `help/`, `feedback/` and `screens/status-labels`, `screens/illegal-action-copy` and `screens/how-to-play-dialog`.
  - It never imports `screens/table/` or `design/`. The one exception is the `TableScreenProps` type that `ArenaScreenProps` extends.
  - A pure helper still living in `screens/table/` or `design/` moves, with its tests, in the task that first needs it. L75-06 drops `help/hint-overlay.tsx`'s two `design/` imports, so the arena reaches no `design/` file through `help/`.
- **Old table (R14).** Part 1 deletes nothing from the old table; deletions are Part 2.
  - `screens/table.tsx`, `screens/table/**` and `design/**` are edited only where a test moves out of a source-text read. The task names each such case.
  - Shared modules the old table also uses may change: `help/hint-overlay.tsx`, `help/hint-copy.ts`, `fx/play-flyout.ts`, `fx/use-table-fx-triggers.ts`, `ui/fx-anchors.ts`. The old table must keep compiling, and its tests stay green or are classified in `docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md`. Nothing ships before the L75-09 flip in the same PR.
- **Lot 76 is off-limits (R14).** Lot 76 runs in parallel in its own session. Part 1 never edits:
  - `screens/home*` or `screens/lobby*`;
  - the lobby kit picker (`screens/lobby-kit-picker*`);
  - the What's new dialog (`screens/whats-new-dialog.tsx`, L76-05). L75-01 edits only the item-count pin in `screens/whats-new-dialog.test.ts` (R10);
  - How to play (`screens/how-to-play-*`, L76-06);
  - Feedback (`feedback/**`, L76-07).
  The shared docs (`decisions.md`, `frontend.md`, `backlog_v7.md`, `release-notes.ts`) are reconciled by the Part 2 lot-end merge of `origin/dev`.
- **No rule logic in components (§4.3; golden rule 8).**
  - Legality, targets, disabled reasons and drop zones come only from the view-model.
  - The tutorial gate is client presentation over `tutorialSendAllowed` (`view-model/tutorial-coach-copy.ts`). The server stays authoritative (`intersect-tutorial-legal.ts`).
- **Commit rule (§6.6.4; R8).**
  - Every `ArenaIntent` leaves through `createArenaCommit`. The commit's `allow` is `(intent) => !reconnecting && tutorial.allows(intent)`: the tutorial gate composes with the L74-25 reconnect lock and never replaces it.
  - Every latch, sheet close and targeting end keys on the commit's boolean, so a vetoed send leaves its control live. Leave, forfeit, chat and the tutorial Skip stay App-level calls.
  - Selecting, lifting, opening or closing a sheet, hovering or long-pressing sends nothing.
- **No hidden information (§6.3).** Render only what the view-model exposes. `hidden-kit` reads `SeatModel.kitHidden`: an unspied or a base-Spied seat (Q66).
- **Tests (§12.2; AGENTS.md §6; `docs/agent/testing.md`).**
  - Component tests are `*.test.tsx` that open with `// @vitest-environment happy-dom` and render through `renderUi`. Pure logic is tested in node `*.test.ts`. They query by role, name, visible text and `data-*` anchors.
  - Never a new source-text (`readFileSync`) test. When a Part 1 change makes an existing source-text assertion obsolete, the task rewrites it as a behavior test and records the row in `docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md` (§12.1).
  - No new test file shares a base name with another `.ts` / `.tsx` test, in its directory or against any existing test file (P4). For example, `help/hint-overlay.test.ts` stays, so its behavior twin is `help/hint-overlay.render.test.tsx`; `screens/table/tutorial-callout.test.ts` exists, so L75-04's callout test is `ui/coach/tutorial-callout.render.test.tsx`.
  - happy-dom has no layout: geometry goes to pure helpers, and pixels go to the browser checks below.
  - Tests never call `Date.now()`. Use fake timers with `advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime`.
  - No test is skipped, disabled or weakened.
- **Strict TypeScript.** `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes` (spread optional props conditionally), `noPropertyAccessFromIndexSignature`. Never widen a type or cast to silence them. Unions come from `as const` arrays; named exports only.
- **Design (DESIGN.md).**
  - Colors via `bg-v7-*` / `text-v7-*` classes or `ui/palette.ts`, never `var()` inline. Tap targets ≥ 44 px; no functional text under 11 px; no emoji or ✕ characters.
  - Overlays use `Sheet` / `Dialog` / `Toast`, plus the named portal layers in `ui/layers.ts`: `dragGhost`, `banner`, and the new `hint` and `coach` (R7).
  - Reduced motion via `usePrefersReducedMotion()`, with `data-motion` on animated roots.
  - **A task that adds or changes a `ui/` file or a token updates `DESIGN.md` in the same commit (§5.8).**
- **Copy.** English only. Reuse V2 strings verbatim (`view-model/table-copy.ts`, `view-model/chrome-labels.ts`, `screens/illegal-action-copy.ts`, `ui/ui-copy.ts`). Every new player-facing string is listed in the task's Decisions and in Appendix B › New strings.
- **What's new (§10.4; R10).**
  - Only **L75-01** touches release notes in Part 1. At task time it checks whether `lot-72-v7` is on `origin/main`. If it is not, the task appends one `{ before, after: 'Fixed some bugs.' }` item and updates the count pins in `release-notes.test.ts` and `whats-new-dialog.test.ts`. If it is, the task stops and asks.
  - The opt-in arena changes are listed at the L75-09 flip.
- **Designer questions (R16).** Every designer-only choice ships a default marked **Designer question**. On 2026-11-03 the designer answered four of them; every other question ships its default (Appendix B). Each answer lands in the task that owns that surface:
  - Q87-1 dock reason lines and Q87-2 "Not your turn" twice → L75-13: not answered, the defaults ship (no change);
  - Q87-3 turn label at 375 px → L75-02: **Designer 2026-11-03: keep the truncation** (no change);
  - Q87-4 hand scroll cue → L75-13: **Designer 2026-11-03: add an edge fade** (right edge when the hand overflows, left edge once scrolled away from the start);
  - Q87-5 ground scanlines → L75-02: **Designer 2026-11-03: keep them**, with a scoped detector ignore;
  - the desktop log panel (open question 5, L75-02): **Designer 2026-11-03: keep it expanded at 320 px.**
- **Docs per task (§12.5; AGENTS.md §9, §12).**
  - Each task appends its bullet to `docs/agent/frontend.md` › `## Arena (V7, ?ui=next)` in task order, and fixes any line it supersedes.
  - `decisions.md` entries are appended at the end in the `## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] …` format, each closing with its own `---`. A past entry is never edited; a correction supersedes it. The **"Bas plus court" entry is written by the controller in the plan commit**, from Domain B's proposed text (R5, S8); L75-13 cites it and appends its measured numbers in a new dated entry.
  - A `YYYY-MM-DD` in task text stands for the commit day (`date +%F`).
- **Backlog (R15).** The task row flips `To do` → `Done` in the task's commit. After each task, the Lot 75 Progress cell and the Total read:

  | After | Lot 75 cell | Total (from `dev`'s 72) |
  |---|---|---|
  | L75-01 | 1 | 73 |
  | L75-02 | 2 | 74 |
  | L75-03 | 3 | 75 |
  | L75-13 | 4 | 76 |
  | L75-08 | 5 | 77 |
  | L75-04 | 6 | 78 |
  | L75-05 | 7 | 79 |
  | L75-06 | 8 | 80 |
  | L75-07 | 9 | 81 |

  The Lot 75 cell is exact. If a parallel lot moved the Total first, add one to whatever it holds.
- **Commits (AGENTS.md §10; R15).**
  - Exactly one Conventional Commit per task. The subject is ≤ 50 chars (hard cap 72) and carries the task id, for example `feat(arena): render the desktop layout (L75-02)`.
  - Stage files by name, never `git add -A`.
  - **No AI attribution and no Co-Authored-By trailer**: AGENTS.md overrides any harness reminder.
  - `pnpm verify` is green before each commit.
- **Browser checks (Lot 74 L74-12 / L74-17 precedent; open-question ruling 13; S7).**
  - A browser clause in a task's Acceptance is run by a dedicated controller subagent after the task review, on dedicated dev servers on free ports, never on another session's ports or processes. Part 1 batches them into two checks.
  - **Check 1** runs after L75-08 and covers L75-02, L75-03, L75-13 and L75-08 (plus the Mirror auto-resolve re-check). It records in `frontend.md` in one commit, `docs(arena): record browser check 1 (L75-08)`, whose body lists L75-02, L75-03, L75-13 and L75-08.
  - **Check 2** runs after L75-05 and covers the full tutorial at 375×812. It records in one commit, `docs(arena): record browser check 2 (L75-05)`.
  - Neither is a task's own commit: each task flips to `Done` with its tests green. A defect a check finds gets its own `fix(arena): … (L75-NN)` commit. Anything the check cannot reach goes to the workspace `gate-items.md` for L75-12.
- **Test counts.** Each `it.each` row counts as one test, as Vitest reports it. A count that differs only through that expansion is noted in the report, never "fixed".
- **Tooling.** Run every command through `pnpm` (Node is pinned via `devEngines`). Run a single test file with `pnpm vitest run <path>`. Fetch current Vitest / happy-dom / React docs through Context7 before writing framework-specific config (AGENTS.md §8).

## Conventions (shared by every task)

- **Files:** kebab-case names under `apps/client/src/screens/arena/` (Appendix A.1). `.tsx` exports components only; constants, types and pure helpers sit in sibling `.ts` files.
- **Default test viewport is portrait (R2).**
  - From L75-02 on, the client happy-dom window defaults to **390×844**. The setting is `environmentOptions.happyDOM` in the client project of `vitest.config.ts`, and `docs/agent/testing.md` records it. The planner measured it at `c6941bec`: 287 / 287 client files and 2471 / 2471 tests stay green, so there is no `stubArenaViewport` fallback (K10).
  - A test that does not stub a viewport is therefore a portrait phone with an unmeasured (0×0) arena, which is grid.
  - **Desktop and landscape tests always stub their viewport explicitly** with `stubArenaLayout(viewport, arena, options?)` (Appendix A.10). That is the `use-arena-layout-box.test.tsx` pattern: a fake `visualViewport`, a fake `ResizeObserver`, and a `getBoundingClientRect` spy with optional per-element rects.
  - The mode under test is read from `<main data-layout-mode>`.
- **Inputs:**
  - Build every view with `makeArenaView` / `makeArenaProps`, or the `view-model/test-fixtures.ts` builders they wrap.
  - A tutorial view is `makeTutorialArenaView(index, overrides?)` (L75-04, A.10). Tests override only what they assert on.
  - Intents are asserted on the `makeArenaProps` spies, through the props a fake connection would receive. Below `ArenaScreen`, assert on the `vi.fn()` passed as `commit`.
- **"Nothing sent"** is always `expectNoArenaIntent(props)`. "Exactly one intent" checks that spy's call count and arguments.
- **First-game hints appear in Classic arena renders from L75-06 (S4).** On the POV's turn with fresh hint storage, and off turn while an opponent's kit is hidden, an `ArenaScreen` render shows a hint card: an `aside` named by the hint title, with 'Hide coach', 'Got it' and 'Skip all' buttons, portaled to `document.body`.
  - Tests in L75-02 … L75-05 that render `ArenaScreen` never query document-wide by a hint title word ('Your turn', 'Draw', 'Shop', 'Elimination reward', and the other `HINT_COPY` titles), and never count every button or landmark in `document`. Scope those queries to a region (`[data-arena-region]`) or a dialog. A component rendered alone, without `ArenaScreen`, mounts no hint.
  - L75-06 still runs the whole client suite and scopes any query it finds.
  - Tests that assert a hint call `resetHelpStorage()` in `beforeEach`: hint storage persists across the tests of one file.
- **Sheets:**
  - From L75-02, the card, Shop and seat sheets pass `placement="auto"`: bottom in portrait and landscape-compact, centered on desktop (§6.4).
  - Every other arena sheet keeps `placement="bottom"`. Shared dialogs keep `Dialog`'s own `'auto'` (§7.7).
  - On desktop the log is a panel, not a sheet (A.4).
- **Tutorial highlights** are one attribute, `data-tutorial-highlight="<id>"`, on the mapped element (A.8.6). "Marks exactly its element" means `document.querySelectorAll('[data-tutorial-highlight]')` is exactly the mapped set. Hint anchors stay `data-hint-anchor`, and the two never share code.
- **Order** is meaningful and comes from the model (seat order, badge order, action order). Components never sort.
- **Docs bullets** in the frontend.md Arena section start with the file and the task id, for example: `` - `screens/arena/log-panel.tsx` (L75-02, spec v7 §6.4): … ``.

## Review Focus

These are the five input classes or failure modes that Part 1's briefs and acceptance lines do not test, and that a player is most likely to meet. Each one has a mandatory test in the owning task.

1. **A resize or rotation crosses a mode boundary mid-interaction.**
   - A rotation or a desktop window resize crosses the boundary. The phone keyboard in the chat sheet drops the visible height under 500 px too, but under open-question ruling 7 the layout mode **holds while a text field has focus** and recomputes on blur, with the draft kept (K12; L75-02's `useArenaLayoutBox` hold, and the same hold in `ui/`'s `useViewportMode()` so the coach and Carousel agree, U1).
   - Expected: an open reducer sheet stays open and re-places (card sheet `data-sheet-placement` bottom ↔ center); the owner-local menu, leave confirm and Unspy picker stay open (no region remounts, A.3); a chat draft keeps its focus and the portrait mode while focused, then the arena re-lays out on blur and the draft stays.
   - Live targeting survives: the bar stays, and a valid seat tap sends exactly once.
   - A drag that is lifted when the mode changes, or when the portrait bottom band flips (L75-13), is cancelled: it springs back and sends nothing, because its drop rects were measured for the old layout.
   - **Owner: L75-03.** Test: `stubArenaLayout` at 390×844, then `setViewport(1440, 900)` and `setViewport(390, 420)`, each with a card sheet open, then the menu, the leave confirm and the Unspy picker, then targeting, then a lifted and a pressed drag; `expectNoArenaIntent` after the release. The keyboard case types in the chat field, then `setViewport(390, 420)`: the mode stays `portrait` and the field keeps focus; after blur it is `landscape-compact` and the draft stays. The coach's half (same message key and hidden state across a crossing) is **L75-04**'s test.
2. **The tutorial veto latch.**
   - A send that `allow` vetoes leaves every control live and re-pressable, and the scripted path then sends exactly once:
     - the card sheet stays open with Use enabled;
     - the dock's Draw latch does not latch;
     - the Shop stays open after a vetoed Buy;
     - targeting stays live after a vetoed seat tap;
     - a vetoed drop springs back;
     - the card-sheet **Deactivate confirm stays open** (carry-forward fix).
   - The follow-coach copy shows on the veto and clears after the next allowed send.
   - **Owner: L75-05.** Test: per path, veto (a wrong card or path at the index), then assert the control is still enabled and `expectNoArenaIntent`, then the scripted action, then the spy is called once.
3. **The coach goes under a sheet.**
   - Over an open read-only sheet and over an expanded blocking sheet, the coach stays visible and pressable: its host's `zIndex` is `Z_INDEX.coach`, above every sheet layer, and it sits outside every `[inert]` subtree.
   - Pressing × hides the coach without closing the sheet and without moving focus out of it. Got it advances the tour with a sheet open.
   - **Owner: L75-04.** Test: tutorial index 1 with a card sheet open, then `Hide coach`: the dialog is still open, the coach is hidden, and `document.activeElement` stays inside the dialog.
4. **The compact band flaps at the threshold.**
   - Compacting the bottom band makes the arena taller. A naive trigger then sees room, un-compacts, sees the overlap again, and loops.
   - `bottomBandFor` must be stable under its own effect: while `current === 'compact'`, it judges the arena height the full band would leave (measured − `bottomBandSavingsPx(width)`).
   - **Hysteresis (S1).** `bottomBandSavingsPx` can under-estimate the saving by about 44 px: an upgraded-shield pill can add a fourth pill row to the full strip at 360 px. So once the band is compact, it returns to full only when the full band's arena would clear the plate with at least `BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX` = 48 px to spare. Full → compact keeps the plain threshold.
   - **Owner: L75-13.** Tests: pure — at 360×740 with 8 seats, `compact` stays `compact` when the arena grows by exactly the savings, `full` stays `full` 1 px above the threshold, and `compact` returns to `full` only at the savings plus 48 px; and no flip-flop for a full-band arena oscillating within 48 px of the threshold, with the modelled savings exact or 44 px short. Component — after a `setArena(…)` that grows the arena by the savings, `<main data-bottom-band>` stays `compact` across two more ResizeObserver callbacks, and it returns to `full` only with the 48 px of slack.
5. **FX anchors while the desktop log panel is collapsed.**
   - The collapsed panel's list unmounts (L75-02), but another `action-log-panel` can stay mounted at zero size (a depth-hidden log sheet). `queryActionLogAnchor()` must then return the visible toggle (`data-zone="log-collapsed"`), never the zero-size element.
   - When the panel is expanded, the endpoint is the panel's visible scrollport (`data-zone="action-log-panel"`), never its taller content.
   - **Owner: L75-08.** Test: desktop `stubArenaLayout` with a `rectFor` that gives the toggle a box and a mounted stand-in `action-log-panel` 0×0, with the panel collapsed. A point-gain chip from the log is measured from the toggle; after expanding, it is measured from the panel scrollport.

## Execution order

Sequential, as R13 sets: **L75-01 → L75-02 → L75-03 → L75-13 → L75-08 → L75-04 → L75-05 → L75-06 → L75-07**. Each task starts from the previous task's commit.

- **L75-01** (copy) lands first: L75-06 and L75-07 build on its corrected `HINT_COPY`.
- **L75-02** sets the portrait test default and `stubArenaLayout` that every later task uses. It adds `data-layout-mode`, `data-arena-row` and the log panel.
- **L75-03** adds landscape-compact and its hand helper, and owns Review Focus 1. **L75-13** then compacts the portrait band, reusing L75-03's `compact` / `cardWidthPx` props.
- **L75-08** pins the FX endpoints once all three layouts exist.
- **L75-04** adds `Z_INDEX.hint` / `Z_INDEX.coach`, the tutorial hook and the routing (R9), and composes the commit's `allow` with the lock half of `tutorial.allows` (K4). **L75-05** then fills the script half and gates every commit path.
- **L75-06** hosts the hints on `Z_INDEX.hint` and the commit's `onSent`. **L75-07** adds the per-card thief body last.
- `ArenaScreenInner`'s final hook order and opener order are written once in Appendix A.8.2 (S3) and A.9.2 (S2). A task consumes only from tasks earlier in this order and from shipped code (P1).

After L75-07, Part 2 (L75-09 … L75-12) is planned on the real code. Nothing ships until L75-12.

**Decisions:**
- **Designer question, every string above.** Defaults as in Step 3. The `hidden-kit` and Opponent wording reuse Lot 76's How to play sentence ("…only with an upgraded Spy or Spy Thief, or once you are eliminated. A basic Spy shows only your resources.", §10.1), in the third person, so the two screens agree. `reward` now says "one of their cards", because rules spec §6 lets the pick include unused specials ("a card from their hand" did not).
- **Designer question: `your-turn`.** 'It is your turn you have to take **one** action.' is a run-on sentence, but it is not in the §10.2 list, so it is unchanged. Default: unchanged (never invent a copy change).
- **Designer question: the What's new line** `Some tips and tutorial steps had typos or outdated Spy and reward text.` / `Fixed some bugs.` (§10.4: one short Before sentence).

### Task L75-02: Render the arena desktop layout

**Brief correction (R1):** the brief's "§6.4: width ≥ 1024 px and height ≥ 700 px" is stale. The mode is `viewportModeFor` (`ui/viewport-mode.ts`, spec §6.4 as amended): visible height < 500 → `landscape-compact`; otherwise width > height → `desktop`; otherwise `portrait`. 1024×600 is desktop; 768×1024 is portrait.

```ts
// arena-regions.ts — additive
export const ARENA_ROWS = ['top', 'ticker', 'arena', 'strip', 'hand', 'dock', 'side'] as const;
export type ArenaRow = (typeof ARENA_ROWS)[number];
export function arenaRowOf(region: ArenaRegion, mode: LayoutMode): ArenaRow;
export function arenaRowAttributes(region: ArenaRegion, mode: LayoutMode): { readonly 'data-arena-row': ArenaRow };
/** `<main>`'s grid for `mode`; every region is one named area (its region id). */
export interface ArenaGridStyle { readonly gridTemplateAreas: string; readonly gridTemplateColumns: string; readonly gridTemplateRows: string }
export function arenaGridStyle(mode: LayoutMode): ArenaGridStyle;
export function arenaAreaStyle(region: ArenaRegion): { readonly gridArea: ArenaRegion };

// log-panel.tsx (A.4.1 without `tutorialHighlight?`: L75-04 adds it, and L75-02 does not pre-add it, S6)
export interface LogPanelProps {
  expanded: boolean; view: PlayingStateView; model: LogModel;
  onToggle: () => void; onOpenCard: (source: CardSheetSource) => void;
}
export function LogPanel(props: LogPanelProps): ReactElement;

// log-rounds.tsx
export interface LogRoundsProps {
  view: PlayingStateView; model: LogModel; onOpenCard: (source: CardSheetSource) => void;
  /** 'sheet-body' keeps `-top-3` (the Sheet body's 12 px padding, G27); 'panel' sticks at `top-0`. */
  inset: 'sheet-body' | 'panel';
}
export function LogRounds(props: LogRoundsProps): ReactElement;

// use-log-follow.ts
/** While `active`: open on the newest line, follow within 48 px, refresh `lastTop` on every stick. */
export function useLogFollow(active: boolean, content: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>, scrollerOf: (content: HTMLElement) => HTMLElement | null): void;

// seat-geometry.ts — additive (A.4.3)
export function seatRenderBox(mode: LayoutMode): Size;           // SEAT_BOX[mode]
export function seatRect(center: SeatPlacement, mode?: LayoutMode): Rect;   // mode defaults to 'portrait'
// SEAT_RENDER_BOX stays, === seatRenderBox('portrait').

// opponent-seat.tsx — OpponentSeatProps gains `mode?: LayoutMode` (default 'portrait'); root `data-seat-size={mode}`.

// ui/use-visual-viewport-box.ts — additive (U1 ruling; the one definition of "a text field has focus")
/** A `<textarea>`, a text-entry `<input>` (not button, checkbox, color, file, hidden, image, radio, range, reset or submit), or a contenteditable element. */
export function isTextEntryElement(element: Element | null): boolean;
/** True while `document.activeElement` is a text-entry element (`focusin` / `focusout`). */
export function useTextEntryFocused(): boolean;
// useViewportMode(): ViewportMode keeps its last mode while useTextEntryFocused(); useVisualViewportBox() is unchanged (live).

// test-view.ts (A.10, verbatim)
export interface ArenaLayoutStubOptions { readonly rectFor?: (element: Element) => DOMRect | undefined }
export interface ArenaLayoutStub { setViewport(width: number, height: number): void; setArena(width: number, height: number): void; restore(): void }
export function stubArenaLayout(viewport: Size, arena: Size, options?: ArenaLayoutStubOptions): ArenaLayoutStub;
export const ARENA_TEST_BOXES: {
  readonly phone360: LayoutBox; readonly phone375: LayoutBox; readonly phone390: LayoutBox; readonly duplicator360: LayoutBox;
  readonly tablet: LayoutBox; readonly landscape: LayoutBox; readonly landscapeSmall: LayoutBox; readonly desktop: LayoutBox;
};
```

```ts
/** Arena rows per layout mode: technical spec v7 §6.1, §6.4; Lot 75 plan Appendix A.3 (L75-02). */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { ARENA_REGIONS, ARENA_ROWS, arenaGridStyle, arenaRowAttributes, arenaRowOf } from './arena-regions';

const MODES = ['portrait', 'landscape-compact', 'desktop'] as const;

describe('arena rows (A.3)', () => {
  it('puts each region in its row for every mode', () => {
    expect(ARENA_ROWS).toEqual(['top', 'ticker', 'arena', 'strip', 'hand', 'dock', 'side']);
    expect(ARENA_REGIONS.map((region) => MODES.map((mode) => arenaRowOf(region, mode)))).toEqual([
      ['top', 'top', 'top'],
      ['ticker', 'top', 'side'],
      ['arena', 'arena', 'arena'],
      ['strip', 'hand', 'strip'],
      ['hand', 'hand', 'hand'],
      ['dock', 'hand', 'hand'],
    ]);
  });

  it('spreads the row as data-arena-row', () => {
    expect(arenaRowAttributes('dock', 'desktop')).toEqual({ 'data-arena-row': 'hand' });

    for (const mode of MODES) {
      const names = new Set(arenaGridStyle(mode).gridTemplateAreas.split(/[\s"]+/).filter((name) => name !== ''));
      expect([...names].sort()).toEqual([...ARENA_REGIONS].sort());
    }
  });
});
```

```tsx
function EditingProbe() {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const { viewport } = useArenaLayoutBox(ref);
  return (
    <div ref={ref}>
      <output data-testid="box">{`${viewport.width}x${viewport.height}`}</output>
      <input aria-label="Message" />
      <input aria-label="Ready" type="checkbox" />
    </div>
  );
}
function setVisible(width: number, height: number): void {
  act(() => { fake.width = width; fake.height = height; fake.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize')); });
}

describe('useArenaLayoutBox keyboard hold (ruling 7)', () => {
  it('holds the viewport while a text field has focus, and follows it on blur', async () => {
    const { user } = renderUi(<EditingProbe />);
    const field = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Message' });
    await user.click(field);
    setVisible(390, 420);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('box').textContent).toBe('390x844');
    act(() => { field.blur(); });
    expect(screen.getByTestId('box').textContent).toBe('390x420');
  });

  it('a focused checkbox holds nothing: only text entry raises a keyboard', async () => {
    const { user } = renderUi(<EditingProbe />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Ready' }));
    setVisible(844, 390);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('box').textContent).toBe('844x390');
  });
});
```

```tsx
function HoldProbe() {
  const mode = useViewportMode();
  const { width, height } = useVisualViewportBox();
  return (
    <div>
      <output data-testid="mode">{mode}</output>
      <output data-testid="box">{`${width}x${height}`}</output>
      <input aria-label="Message" />
    </div>
  );
}

describe('useViewportMode keyboard hold (L75-02, ruling 7, U1)', () => {
  it('holds its mode while a text field has focus and follows on blur; the box stays live', async () => {
    const { user } = renderUi(<HoldProbe />);
    const field = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Message' });
    await user.click(field);
    resize(390, 420); // the keyboard opens: the visible box shrinks under 500 px
    expect([screen.getByTestId('mode').textContent, screen.getByTestId('box').textContent]).toEqual(['portrait', '390x420']);
    act(() => { field.blur(); });
    expect(screen.getByTestId('mode').textContent).toBe('landscape-compact');

});
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/log-panel.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * The desktop log panel: technical spec v7 §6.4, §6.9; Appendix A `table-action-log-panel`; plan
 * Appendix A.4.1 (L75-02). Also the follow rule's call-site test the L74-26 review left open (M3):
 * every stick refreshes `lastTop`, so a scroll event the reader did not make never unsticks the log.
 */
import type { ActionLogEntryView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { fireEvent, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { log } from '../../view-model/log';
import { LogPanel } from './log-panel';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, makeArenaView, stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub } from './test-view';

const drawBy = (actorPlayerId: string, turnSequence: number): ActionLogEntryView => ({ kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId, action: 'draw', turnSequence });
const ATTACK: ActionLogEntryView = { kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p1', action: 'playCard', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false, targetPlayerId: 'me', turnSequence: 3 };
/** Three seats: turns 0–2 are round 1, turn 3 round 2. */
const LOG = [drawBy('me', 0), drawBy('p1', 1), drawBy('p2', 2), ATTACK];

let layout: ArenaLayoutStub | null = null;
afterEach(() => { layout?.restore(); layout = null; });

function setup(expanded: boolean, actionLog: readonly ActionLogEntryView[] = LOG) {
  const view = makeArenaView({ overrides: { actionLog } });
  const onToggle = vi.fn<() => void>();
  const onOpenCard = vi.fn();
  return { onToggle, onOpenCard, ...renderUi(<LogPanel expanded={expanded} view={view} model={log(view)} onToggle={onToggle} onOpenCard={onOpenCard} />) };
}
const toggle = (): HTMLElement => screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Action log' });
const port = (): HTMLElement | null => document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="action-log-panel"]');

/** happy-dom has no layout: give the scrollport a geometry and clamp writes as a browser does. */
function stubScroller(element: HTMLElement, box: { scrollHeight: number; clientHeight: number; scrollTop: number }): void {
  Object.defineProperties(element, {
    scrollHeight: { configurable: true, get: () => box.scrollHeight },
    clientHeight: { configurable: true, get: () => box.clientHeight },
    scrollTop: {
      configurable: true,
      get: () => box.scrollTop,
      set: (value: number) => { box.scrollTop = Math.min(value, box.scrollHeight - box.clientHeight); },
    },
  });
}

describe('LogPanel (§6.4, §6.9)', () => {
  it('expanded: a 44 px toggle that controls a scrollport of rounds under sticky top-0 headers', () => {
    setup(true);
    expect([toggle().getAttribute('data-zone'), toggle().getAttribute('aria-expanded'), toggle().getAttribute('aria-controls')])
      .toEqual(['log-collapsed', 'true', port()?.id]);
    const rounds = [...(port()?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-log-round]') ?? [])];
    expect(rounds.map((round) => [round.querySelector('h3')?.textContent, round.querySelectorAll('[data-log-line]').length]))
      .toEqual([['Round 1', 3], ['Round 2', 1]]);
    const header = rounds[0]?.querySelector('h3');
    expect([header?.classList.contains('top-0'), header?.classList.contains('-top-3')]).toEqual([true, false]);
  });

  it('collapsed: only the toggle renders, so no zero-size list is left for the FX (Review Focus 5)', () => {
    setup(false);
    expect([toggle().getAttribute('aria-expanded'), port(), document.querySelector('[data-log-round]')]).toEqual(['false', null, null]);
  });

  it('the toggle calls onToggle and a card name opens its read-only sheet', async () => {
    const { user, onToggle, onOpenCard } = setup(true);
    await user.click(toggle());
    expect(onToggle).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await user.click(within(port() ?? document.body).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack' }));
    expect(onOpenCard.mock.calls).toEqual([[{ kind: 'log', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false }]]);
  });

  it('reads "No actions yet" while the log is empty', () => {
    setup(true, []);
    expect(within(port() ?? document.body).getByText('No actions yet')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('keeps following new lines through a scroll event the reader did not make (L74-26 review M3)', () => {
    layout = stubArenaLayout(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop.viewport, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop.arena);
    setup(true);
    const scroller = port();
    if (scroller === null) throw new Error('no scrollport');
    const box = { scrollHeight: 900, clientHeight: 300, scrollTop: 0 };
    stubScroller(scroller, box);
    const grow = (): void => { layout?.setArena(1000, 600); }; // fires every fake ResizeObserver: the follow re-sticks
    grow();
    expect(box.scrollTop).toBe(600);
    box.scrollHeight = 1100; // new lines landed; Chrome fires a scroll with scrollTop unchanged
    fireEvent.scroll(scroller);
    grow();
    expect(box.scrollTop).toBe(800);
    box.scrollTop = 300; // the reader scrolls up
    fireEvent.scroll(scroller);
    box.scrollHeight = 1300;
    grow();
    expect(box.scrollTop).toBe(300);
  });
});
```

That is 5 tests.

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-desktop.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * The arena desktop layout and the layout-mode root: technical spec v7 §6.1, §6.4, §6.9, §7.7;
 * controller rulings R1 (thresholds), R2 (the portrait test window), R3 (desktop); plan Appendix
 * A.2–A.4 and A.10 (L75-02). It also pins `stubArenaLayout`: a node test cannot render a mode flip.
 */
import type { ActionLogEntryView, PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { UserEvent } from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { SEAT_BOX, type LayoutBox } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { seats } from '../../view-model/seats';
import { makeCard, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ARENA_REGIONS } from './arena-regions';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView, stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub } from './test-view';

const P1_DRAWS: ActionLogEntryView = { kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p1', action: 'draw', turnSequence: 1 };
const deskView = (seatCount = 4): PlayingStateView =>
  makeArenaView({ seatCount, overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: [makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' })] }), actionLog: [P1_DRAWS] } });

let layout: ArenaLayoutStub | null = null;
afterEach(() => { layout?.restore(); layout = null; });

function setupAt(box: LayoutBox, view: PlayingStateView = deskView()) {
  layout?.restore(); // a second setup in one test replaces the first stub, never stacks on it
  const stub = stubArenaLayout(box.viewport, box.arena);
  layout = stub;
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view });
  return { stub, props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />) };
}
function one(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (found === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return found;
}
const main = (): HTMLElement => one('main[data-arena="next"]');
const region = (name: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
const rows = (): (string | null)[] => ARENA_REGIONS.map((name) => region(name).getAttribute('data-arena-row'));
const logToggle = (): HTMLElement => within(region('ticker')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Action log' });

describe('desktop layout (§6.4, R3)', () => {
  it.each([
    { label: 'the ellipse', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop, kind: 'arc' },
    { label: 'the grid seats() falls back to', box: { viewport: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop.viewport, arena: { width: 1000, height: 120 } }, kind: 'grid' },
  ])('1440×900 renders desktop: 7 seats on $label at the desktop box, the log column, the dock in the hand row', ({ box, kind }) => {
    const view = deskView(8);
    const { props } = setupAt(box, view);
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('desktop');
    const opponents = one('[data-zone="opponents"]');
    expect([opponents.getAttribute('data-seat-layout'), seats(view, box).layout.kind]).toEqual([kind, kind]);
    const drawn = [...opponents.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="opponent-seat"]')];
    expect(drawn.map((seat) => [seat.getAttribute('data-seat-size'), seat.style.width, seat.style.height]))
      .toEqual(Array.from({ length: 7 }, () => ['desktop', `${String(SEAT_BOX.desktop.width)}px`, `${String(SEAT_BOX.desktop.height)}px`]));
    expect(Array.from(main().querySelectorAll('[data-arena-region]'), (node) => node.getAttribute('data-arena-region'))).toEqual([...ARENA_REGIONS]);
    expect(rows()).toEqual(['top', 'side', 'arena', 'strip', 'hand', 'hand']);
    expect([logToggle().getAttribute('data-zone'), logToggle().getAttribute('aria-expanded')]).toEqual(['log-collapsed', 'true']);
    expect(one('[data-zone="action-log-panel"] [data-log-line="0"]').textContent).toBe('p1 draws');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('the log panel toggles closed and open, and its toggle stays rendered; nothing is sent', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop);
    await user.click(logToggle());
    expect([logToggle().getAttribute('aria-expanded'), document.querySelector('[data-zone="action-log-panel"]')]).toEqual(['false', null]);
    await user.click(logToggle());
    expect(logToggle().getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true');
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="action-log-panel"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('Menu → Action log expands a collapsed panel on desktop and opens no sheet', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop);
    await user.click(logToggle());
    await user.click(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Menu' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Menu' })).getByRole('button', { name: 'Action log' }));
    expect([logToggle().getAttribute('aria-expanded'), screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Action log' })]).toEqual(['true', null]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it.each<{ label: string; open: (user: UserEvent) => Promise<void>; dialog: string; desktop: 'center' | 'bottom' }>([
    { label: 'the card sheet', open: (user) => user.click(within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage' })), dialog: 'Basic attack', desktop: 'center' },
    { label: 'the seat sheet', open: (user) => user.click(within(region('arena')).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' })), dialog: 'p1', desktop: 'center' },
    { label: 'the Shop', open: (user) => user.click(within(region('dock')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Shop' })), dialog: 'Shop', desktop: 'center' },
    { label: 'the chat sheet', open: (user) => user.click(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Chat/ })), dialog: 'Chat', desktop: 'bottom' },
  ])('$label is a $desktop sheet on desktop and a bottom sheet on a portrait phone (§6.4, §7.7)', async ({ open, dialog, desktop }) => {
    const first = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop);
    await open(first.user);
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: dialog }).getAttribute('data-sheet-placement')).toBe(desktop);
    first.unmount();
    const phone = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390);
    await open(phone.user);
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: dialog }).getAttribute('data-sheet-placement')).toBe('bottom');
    expectNoArenaIntent(phone.props);
  });

  it('a log sheet opened in portrait stays open when the window turns desktop, beside the expanded panel (A.4.1)', async () => {
    const { user, stub } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390);
    await user.click(within(region('ticker')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Action log/ }));
    stub.setViewport(1440, 900);
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('desktop');
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Action log' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(logToggle().getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true');
  });

  it('a 768×1024 tablet renders portrait: one column with the ticker row and seats at the portrait box (R1)', () => {
    setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.tablet);
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('portrait');
    expect(rows()).toEqual(['top', 'ticker', 'arena', 'strip', 'hand', 'dock']);
    expect(within(region('ticker')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Action log/ }).getAttribute('aria-haspopup')).toBe('dialog');
    expect(one('[data-zone="opponent-seat"]').getAttribute('data-seat-size')).toBe('portrait');
  });
});

describe('layout mode root (R1, R2, A.2)', () => {
  it.each([
    [1440, 900, 'desktop'], [1024, 600, 'desktop'],
    [768, 1024, 'portrait'], [360, 740, 'portrait'], [375, 812, 'portrait'], [390, 844, 'portrait'],
    [844, 390, 'landscape-compact'], [667, 375, 'landscape-compact'], [740, 360, 'landscape-compact'],
  ] as const)('a %i×%i visible box renders %s', (width, height, mode) => {
    setupAt({ viewport: { width, height }, arena: { width: Math.min(width, 1000), height: 200 } });
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe(mode);
  });

  it('stubArenaLayout flips data-layout-mode live, and every region keeps its element (A.3, A.10)', () => {
    const { stub } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390);
    const before = ARENA_REGIONS.map(region);
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('portrait');
    stub.setViewport(1440, 900);
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('desktop');
    stub.setViewport(844, 390);
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('landscape-compact');
    expect(ARENA_REGIONS.map(region).every((element, index) => element === before[index])).toBe(true);
  });

  it('without a stub the window is a 390×844 portrait phone with an unmeasured arena (R2)', () => {
    renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...makeArenaProps()} />);
    expect([window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight]).toEqual([390, 844]);
    expect([main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode'), one('[data-zone="opponents"]').getAttribute('data-seat-layout')]).toEqual(['portrait', 'grid']);
  });
});
```

That is 21 tests as Vitest counts them (2 + 1 + 1 + 4 + 1 + 1 in the desktop block, 9 + 1 + 1 in the root block).

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail.** `pnpm vitest run apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-desktop.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/log-panel.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-regions.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/seat-geometry.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/opponent-seat.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/use-arena-layout-box.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/test-view.test.ts apps/client/src/ui/use-visual-viewport-box.test.tsx`. Expected:
  - `log-panel.test.tsx` fails to resolve `./log-panel`, a new module;
  - `arena-desktop` and `arena-regions` fail at run time with a `TypeError`, not at resolve: `./test-view` and `./arena-regions` exist, so `stubArenaLayout`, `ARENA_TEST_BOXES`, `arenaRowOf` and the other new named exports import as `undefined` (the one `expect(ARENA_ROWS)` fails on that `undefined`);

```
## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] V7 arena desktop: one grid per mode, the log column, centred sheets (L75-02)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.1, §6.4, §6.9, §7.7; controller rulings R1, R2, R3 and open-question
rulings 5 and 7 (Lot 75 plan, Appendix A.2–A.4).

- **Thresholds.** The mode is `viewportModeFor` (§6.4 as amended). The L75-02 brief's "width ≥ 1024
  and height ≥ 700" was stale: 1024×600 is desktop, and 768×1024 is portrait.
- **One grid, no wrappers.** `<main>` switches its grid template per mode, and every region keeps its
  element, so owner-local sheets, drafts and latches survive a rotation.
- **Desktop log.** The panel replaces the ticker (§6.4). It opens expanded, 320 px wide, with a 44 px
  rail when collapsed. The collapsed list unmounts, so the toggle is then the only log endpoint.
  **Designer 2026-11-03:** expanded on mount and 320 px, kept.
- **Centred sheets.** The card, Shop and seat sheets resolve `placement="auto"`. The Sheet primitive
  already had it, so no `ui/` change. On desktop the Shop keeps the Sheet's 28rem width cap.
- **Seat box per mode.** Desktop seats are drawn at 120×136, with a tile portrait (72×96) and a 40 px
  resource column. The portrait anatomy is unchanged.
- **Keyboard hold (ruling 7).** While a text `input`, a `textarea` or a contenteditable element has
  focus, `useArenaLayoutBox` keeps the viewport box it had when focus arrived. The mode, and every
  size read from the viewport, holds until blur. U1 (controller): `ui/`'s `useViewportMode()` holds
  its mode the same way, through the shared `useTextEntryFocused()` (`ui/use-visual-viewport-box.ts`),
  so the coach, Carousel and the hint hosts agree with the arena. `useVisualViewportBox()` stays
  live, so a sheet or toast still sits above the keyboard. `Sheet` reads that live box for
  `placement="auto"`, so its placement follows the keyboard, not the held mode. That is harmless:
  `auto` is `bottom` in both portrait and landscape-compact, and the two differ only on desktop.
- **Test window (R2).** The client happy-dom window defaults to 390×844. At `c6941bec`, all 287
  client test files stayed green with it, so no per-file stub was needed.
- **Ground scanlines (Q87-5, designer 2026-11-03).** The Lot 72 ground keeps its 1 px scanlines
  every 3 px. The detector's `repeating-stripes-gradient` finding on them is ignored, scoped to
  `ui/tokens.css` (`.impeccable/config.json`), as the two halo rules are. Spec v7 §12.4 asks for the
  designer's approval before any other ignore, and this is it. Nothing else is ignored.
- **Turn label (Q87-3, designer 2026-11-03).** The turn label keeps truncating at 375 px, with its
  `title`. No change.

---
```

- Component tests with a 1440×900 layout box, mode `desktop`: `1440×900 renders desktop: 7 seats on $label …` (the `data-layout-mode` assertion).
- Every opponent seat in the ellipse container (grid when `seats()` says so): the same `it.each` (`arc` and `grid` rows, both against `seats(view, box).layout.kind`).
- The log panel toggles open and closed, keeping a rendered log element when collapsed: `the log panel toggles closed and open, and its toggle stays rendered; nothing is sent`, and `log-panel.test.tsx` › `collapsed: only the toggle renders …`.
- The dock sits in the hand row: `rows()` equals `[…, 'hand', 'hand']` in the desktop test.
- A 768×1024 box renders portrait: `a 768×1024 tablet renders portrait …` and the R1 `it.each`.
- Browser check at 1440×900 with 2, 4 and 8 seats: browser check (Check 1).
- The ground scanlines' detector ignore is in `.impeccable/config.json` with a reason that cites the designer (Q87-5): Step 3's parse check prints 29; Check 1's in-page scan confirms the finding is gone.
- The mode holds while a text field has focus, in the arena and in `ui/` (ruling 7, U1; not in the brief's Acceptance, ruled by the controller): `use-arena-layout-box.test.tsx` › `holds the viewport while a text field has focus, and follows it on blur`, and `ui/use-visual-viewport-box.test.tsx` › `holds its mode while a text field has focus and follows on blur; the box stays live`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Brief correction (R1):** the stale thresholds (above).
- **R2, K10:** the global 390×844 default. The planner measured it at `c6941bec` with a scratch config that only adds the option: 287 / 287 client files and 2471 / 2471 tests green. So the `stubArenaViewport` fallback is not built, and Step 3's whole-suite run re-checks the measurement.
- **Ruling 7** lives in `useArenaLayoutBox` and holds the whole viewport box, not only the mode: the hand size (L75-03) and the band savings (L75-13) hold with it. Text-entry inputs only.
- **U1 (controller ruling).** `isTextEntryElement` and `useTextEntryFocused()` live in `ui/use-visual-viewport-box.ts`, and `useViewportMode()` holds its last mode through them (render-phase held state), so `CoachPanel`, `Carousel` and the hint hosts read the same mode as the arena while a field has focus. `useVisualViewportBox()` stays live: sheets and toasts must follow the keyboard. **Sheet placement follows the live box**, not the held mode (`Sheet` resolves `placement="auto"` from `useVisualViewportBox()`, `ui/primitives/sheet.tsx:196, :232`). It can differ from the held mode only on desktop (a held `desktop` is `center`; a live box that left desktop is `bottom`); `auto` is `bottom` in both portrait and landscape-compact, so there it cannot differ. This is `ui/` work with its own test and a `DESIGN.md` line, so it is staged in Step 6.
- **Grid by inline style** (`arenaGridStyle`), because Tailwind cannot spell quoted area templates. `arenaGridStyle`, `arenaAreaStyle`, `log-rounds.tsx` and `use-log-follow.ts` are accepted additions to contract A.1 / A.3 (K11). The desktop grid has three columns (hand, dock, log) and the top bar spans all three (K12).
- **The desktop log list unmounts when collapsed** (contract A.4.1 now states it; L75-08's visible-first lookup covers any other zero-size `action-log-panel`): the toggle is then the only log endpoint, and expanding again reopens on the newest line.
- **Designer 2026-11-03:** the desktop log panel opens expanded, 320 px wide, with a 44 px rail (open-question ruling 5). Kept.
- **Designer question:** the desktop Shop is a centred dialog at the Sheet's 28rem cap (V2's Shop dialog was 48rem). Default: 28rem.
- **Designer question:** the desktop seat anatomy, a tile portrait (72×96) with a 40 px resource column in the 120×136 box (starting values, §14.3). Default: as planned.
- **Designer 2026-11-03, Q87-3:** the turn label keeps truncating at 375 px, with its `title`. No change.
- **Designer 2026-11-03, Q87-5:** the ground scanlines stay, and `repeating-stripes-gradient` on `ui/tokens.css` is ignored in `.impeccable/config.json` (Step 3). The spec §12.4 pre-approval covered only the two halo rules; this is the designer's approval for the third.
- **The Sell zone is not moved** on desktop: `sellZoneRect`'s signature is a Lot 74 export, and the call-site change would rewrite `hand-drag-layer.test.tsx`'s Sell test. Gate item.
- **New strings:** none. The toggle reuses `ACTION_LOG_OPEN_LABEL` ('Action log'), and the panel reuses 'No actions yet' and 'Round N'.

```ts
export const HAND_CARD_MIN_W_PX = 64;
export const HAND_CARD_MAX_W_PX = 120;
/** Card height / width (`ui/card/card.tsx`: height = --card-w × 1.5). Additive; L75-13 reads it. */

/** Landscape-compact: a hand card a quarter of the visible height tall (§5.4, §6.4), clamped to [MIN, MAX]. */
```

```ts
/** The landscape-compact hand size rule: technical spec v7 §5.4, §6.4; DESIGN.md › Card › Size contract (L75-03). */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { HAND_CARD_ASPECT, HAND_CARD_MAX_W_PX, HAND_CARD_MIN_W_PX, heightBoundHandCardWidthPx } from './hand-card-size';

describe('heightBoundHandCardWidthPx (§5.4)', () => {
  it('makes a landscape hand card a quarter of the visible height tall', () => {
    expect([heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(390), heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(480)]).toEqual([65, 80]);
    expect(heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(480) * HAND_CARD_ASPECT).toBe(120);
  });

  it('clamps to the Card hand size, 64 to 120 px', () => {
    expect([HAND_CARD_MIN_W_PX, HAND_CARD_MAX_W_PX, HAND_CARD_ASPECT]).toEqual([64, 120, 1.5]);
    expect([heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(375), heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(0), heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(1000)]).toEqual([64, 64, 120]);
  });

  it('never grows as the visible height shrinks', () => {
    for (let height = 361; height < 500; height += 1) {
      expect(heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(height)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(height - 1));
    }
  });
});
```

```tsx
function renderCompact(view: PlayingStateView) {
  const onOpenCard = vi.fn();
  const result = renderUi(<SelfStrip model={selfStrip(view, LIVE)} compact onOpenKit={vi.fn()} onOpenCard={onOpenCard} />);
  return { ...result, onOpenCard };

describe('SelfStrip compact (L75-03, L75-13)', () => {
  it('is one row: the thumb kit portrait and four small pills whose captions are spoken, not shown', () => {
    const { container } = renderCompact(viewWith({ lives: 9, shield: 4, points: 12, upgradePoints: 1 }));
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-self-strip]')?.getAttribute('data-self-strip')).toBe('compact');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Inspect your kit' }).getAttribute('data-kit-size')).toBe('thumb');
    const resources = container.querySelector('[data-zone="resources"]');
    if (resources === null) throw new Error('no resources zone');
    expect(resources.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('resources');
    const pills = Array.from(resources.querySelectorAll('[data-resource-kind]'));
    expect(pills.map((pill) => pill.getAttribute('data-resource-kind'))).toEqual(['life', 'shield', 'point', 'upgradePoint']);
    expect(pills.map((pill) => [visibleText(pill), spokenTexts(pill), pill.getAttribute('data-size')])).toEqual([
      ['9', ['Lives 9'], 'sm'], ['4', ['Shield 4'], 'sm'], ['12', ['Points 12'], 'sm'], ['1', ['Upgrade points 1'], 'sm'],
    ]);
  });

  it('keeps the flow badges and the in-play thumbs, and a thumb still opens its sheet', async () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ overrides: {
      self: makeSelf({ activePersistentEffects: [POISON] }),
      players: [makePlayer('me', { isYou: true, blockTurnsRemaining: 2 }), makePlayer('p1'), makePlayer('p2')],
    } });
    const { user, onOpenCard } = renderCompact(view);
    expect(screen.getByText('Block · 2 left')).toBeTruthy();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Poison, 3 card lives' }));
    expect(onOpenCard.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({ kind: 'in-play' });
  });
});
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-landscape.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * The arena landscape-compact layout: technical spec v7 §5.4, §6.1, §6.4, §14.3 (tutorial and hint
 * anchors on equivalent elements); controller ruling R4; plan Appendix A.3, A.5 (L75-03).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { heightBoundHandCardWidthPx } from '../../ui/card/hand-card-size';
import { SEAT_BOX, seatOrder, type LayoutBox } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { makeCard, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ARENA_REGIONS } from './arena-regions';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView, stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub } from './test-view';

let layout: ArenaLayoutStub | null = null;
afterEach(() => { layout?.restore(); layout = null; });
function setupAt(box: LayoutBox, view: PlayingStateView) {
  layout = stubArenaLayout(box.viewport, box.arena);
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view });
  return { props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />) };
}
function one(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (found === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return found;
}
const main = (): HTMLElement => one('main[data-arena="next"]');
const region = (name: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
const rows = (): (string | null)[] => ARENA_REGIONS.map((name) => region(name).getAttribute('data-arena-row'));

describe('landscape-compact (§6.4, R4)', () => {
  it.each([
    { label: '844×390', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscape },
    { label: '667×375', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscapeSmall },
  ])('$label: 8 seats in the band in turn order; the compact strip, the hand and the dock share one row', ({ box }) => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8 });
    const { props } = setupAt(box, view);
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('landscape-compact');
    const band = one('[data-zone="opponents"]');
    expect(band.getAttribute('data-seat-layout')).toBe('band');
    const drawn = [...band.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="opponent-seat"]')];
    expect(drawn.map((seat) => seat.getAttribute('data-player-id'))).toEqual(seatOrder(view));
    expect(drawn.every((seat) => seat.getAttribute('data-seat-size') === 'landscape-compact'
      && seat.style.height === `${String(SEAT_BOX['landscape-compact'].height)}px`)).toBe(true);
    expect(rows()).toEqual(['top', 'top', 'arena', 'hand', 'hand', 'hand']);
    expect(one('[data-self-strip]').getAttribute('data-self-strip')).toBe('compact');
    expect(one('[data-hand-fan]').style.getPropertyValue('--hand-card-w')).toBe(`${String(heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(box.viewport.height))}px`);
    expect(within(region('ticker')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Action log/ }).getAttribute('aria-haspopup')).toBe('dialog');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a 360×740 phone stays portrait, with the full strip and the ticker row', () => {
    setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, makeArenaView());
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe('portrait');
    expect(rows()).toEqual(['top', 'ticker', 'arena', 'strip', 'hand', 'dock']);
    expect(one('[data-self-strip]').getAttribute('data-self-strip')).toBe('full');
    expect(one('[data-zone="opponents"]').getAttribute('data-seat-layout')).not.toBe('band');
  });

  it.each([
    { mode: 'portrait', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390, strip: 'full' },
    { mode: 'landscape-compact', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscape, strip: 'compact' },
    { mode: 'desktop', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop, strip: 'full' },
  ])('keeps every first-game hint anchor and the tutorial anchors on arena elements in $mode (§14.3, S5)', ({ mode, box, strip }) => {
    setupAt(box, makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: [makeCard('spy')] }) } }));
    expect(main().getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe(mode);
    const anchors = new Set(Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-hint-anchor]'), (node) => node.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')));
    expect([...anchors]).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['your-turn', 'incoming', 'resources', 'hand', 'specials', 'draw', 'shop', 'hidden-kit']));
    // The elements L75-04's tour marks (S5): one each, in its region, in every mode.
    for (const selector of [
      '[data-arena-region="ticker"] [data-zone="log-collapsed"]',
      '[data-arena-region="self-strip"] [data-zone="resources"]',
      '[data-arena-region="self-strip"] [data-zone="kit-portrait"]',
      '[data-arena-region="top-bar"] [data-top-bar="turn"]',
      '[data-arena-region="top-bar"] [data-turn-clock]',
    ]) {
      expect(document.querySelectorAll(selector)).toHaveLength(1);
    }
    expect(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Forfeit' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(one('[data-self-strip]').getAttribute('data-self-strip')).toBe(strip);
  });
});
```

That is 6 tests as Vitest counts them. The last one also pins the anchors L75-04 relies on (S5): the log element in the `ticker` region (the `LogTicker` button, or the desktop `LogPanel` toggle), the resources row and the `KitPortrait` root in the `self-strip` region (the compact strip too), the top bar's turn card, clock and flag, and `data-self-strip`.

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-mode-crossing.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * A layout mode crossing mid-interaction: plan Review Focus 1; technical spec v7 §6.4, §6.6.3,
 * §6.7; Appendix A.3 (no region remounts) and A.5.3 (a mode change cancels a drag); controller
 * open-question ruling 7 (the mode holds while a text field has focus) (L75-03). The coach's half
 * (its message key and hidden state across a crossing) is L75-04's.
 */
import type { PlayingStateView, PublicPlayerView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, fireEvent, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { UserEvent } from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi, type RenderUiOptions } from '../../test/render';
import { makeCard, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView, stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub } from './test-view';

const PRIMARY = { pointerId: 1, isPrimary: true, button: 0 } as const;
const HAND = [makeCard('spy', { instanceId: 'spy-1' }), makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' })];
function withSeat(view: PlayingStateView, playerId: string, patch: Partial<PublicPlayerView>): PlayingStateView {
  return { ...view, players: view.players.map((player) => (player.id === playerId ? { ...player, ...patch } : player)) };
}
/** Me, p1, p2; 10 points; Spy and Basic attack in hand; p1 spies on me, so Unspy opens. */
const crossView = (): PlayingStateView => withSeat(makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: HAND }) } }), 'p1', { spyingOnYou: true });

let layout: ArenaLayoutStub | null = null;
afterEach(() => { layout?.restore(); layout = null; });
function setup(options: RenderUiOptions = {}) {
  const stub = stubArenaLayout(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390.viewport, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390.arena);
  layout = stub;
  const onPlayCard = vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onPlayCard']>();
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view: crossView(), onPlayCard });
  return { stub, onPlayCard, props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, options) };
}
const mode = (): string | null | undefined => document.querySelector('main[data-arena="next"]')?.getAttribute('data-layout-mode');
function region(name: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
  if (found === null) throw new Error(`no ${name} region`);
  return found;
}
const ghost = (): Element | null => document.querySelector('[data-zone="hand-drag-ghost"]');
/** A rotation to desktop, then a short window (landscape-compact), each checked. */
function cross(stub: ArenaLayoutStub, check: (next: 'desktop' | 'landscape-compact') => void): void {
  stub.setViewport(1440, 900);
  expect(mode()).toBe('desktop');
  check('desktop');
  stub.setViewport(390, 420);
  expect(mode()).toBe('landscape-compact');
  check('landscape-compact');
}

describe('a mode crossing mid-interaction (Review Focus 1)', () => {
  it('an open card sheet stays open and re-places: bottom, centred on desktop, bottom again', async () => {
    const { user, props, stub } = setup();
    await user.click(within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }));
    const placement = (): string | null => screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' }).getAttribute('data-sheet-placement');
    expect(placement()).toBe('bottom');
    cross(stub, (next) => { expect(placement()).toBe(next === 'desktop' ? 'center' : 'bottom'); });
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it.each<{ label: string; open: (user: UserEvent) => Promise<void>; dialog: string }>([
    { label: 'the menu', open: (user) => user.click(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Menu' })), dialog: 'Menu' },
    { label: 'the leave confirm', open: (user) => user.click(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Forfeit' })), dialog: 'Leave the game?' },
    { label: 'the Unspy picker', open: (user) => user.click(within(region('dock')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Unspy, minus 10 pts' })), dialog: 'Unspy' },
  ])('$label (owner-local) stays open across both crossings: no region remounts (A.3)', async ({ open, dialog }) => {
    const { user, props, stub } = setup();
    await open(user);
    cross(stub, () => { expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: dialog })).toBeTruthy(); });
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('live targeting survives both crossings, and a valid seat tap then sends exactly once', async () => {
    const { user, onPlayCard, stub } = setup();
    await user.click(within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).getByRole('button', { name: /^Use/ }));
    cross(stub, () => { expect(screen.queryByText('Choose a target')).not.toBeNull(); });
    await user.click(within(region('arena')).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1, target' }));
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['spy-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p1' }]]);
  });

  it('a lifted drag is cancelled by a mode change and sends nothing, though its release lands on a valid seat (A.5.3)', () => {
    const { props, stub } = setup({ reducedMotion: true });
    fireEvent.pointerDown(within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage' }), { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 100, clientY: 700 });
    fireEvent.pointerMove(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 103, clientY: 688 });
    expect(ghost()).not.toBeNull();
    stub.setViewport(1440, 900);
    expect(ghost()).toBeNull();
    // Every element reports the arena box here, so without the cancel this release would play the card on p1.
    fireEvent.pointerUp(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 50, clientY: 50 });
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a press that has not lifted yet is dropped by a mode change: the next move lifts nothing', () => {
    const { props, stub } = setup({ reducedMotion: true });
    fireEvent.pointerDown(within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage' }), { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 100, clientY: 700 });
    stub.setViewport(390, 420);
    fireEvent.pointerMove(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 103, clientY: 688 });
    fireEvent.pointerUp(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 50, clientY: 50 });
    expect(ghost()).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a chat draft holds portrait and keeps its focus under the keyboard; on blur the arena re-lays out and the draft stays (ruling 7)', async () => {
    const { user, props, stub } = setup();
    await user.click(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Chat/ }));
    const field = (): HTMLInputElement => within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Chat' })).getByRole<HTMLInputElement>('textbox', { name: 'Message' });
    await user.type(field(), 'gl hf');
    stub.setViewport(390, 420);
    expect([mode(), document.activeElement === field(), field().value]).toEqual(['portrait', true, 'gl hf']);
    act(() => { field().blur(); });
    expect([mode(), field().value]).toEqual(['landscape-compact', 'gl hf']);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});
```

That is 8 tests (1 + 3 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1).

```
## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] V7 arena landscape-compact: the band, the hand size rule, the compact strip (L75-03)

Source: technical spec v7 §5.4, §6.1, §6.4; controller ruling R4 and open-question rulings 6 and 7
(Lot 75 plan, Appendix A.3, A.5); plan Review Focus 1.

- **Rows.** The top bar and the ticker share the top row, the seats sit in one band in turn order,
  and the compact self strip, the hand and the dock share the bottom row. **Designer question**
  (open question 6): this composition? Default: yes.
- **Hand size.** A landscape hand card is a quarter of the visible height tall, clamped to 64–120 px
  (`heightBoundHandCardWidthPx`): 65 px at 390 px, the 64 px floor at 375 px.
- **Compact strip.** One row: the thumb kit portrait and four small pills. Their captions are spoken
  and in `title`, but not shown, because four captioned pills do not fit one row at 360 px. The flow
  badges and the thumbs keep their own lines, so the in-play cards cost the full and the compact
  strip the same height (L75-13 relies on it). **Designer question:** captions off in the compact
  strip (L43-01 keeps them on the full strip)? Default: off.
- **Seat box.** `SEAT_BOX['landscape-compact']` goes from 76×84 to 76×92 (§14.3 tuning). The band now
  draws the portrait anatomy, which had been clipped 4 px at the arena top.
- **Drag.** A mode change cancels a pressed or lifted drag: its drop rects were measured for the
  old layout.

---
```

**Browser check (controller, after review).** It runs in batched **Check 1** after L75-08. On a landscape phone at 844×390 and 667×375, with 8 seats:
- no page scroll; the band holds all 7 seats; the plate is clear of the band (record the arena height against `ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscape` and `landscapeSmall`);
- hand faces are uncropped with readable chips, and the hand scrolls inside itself;
- the compact strip fits one row; the dock is usable.

Repeat with in-play cards (the thumbs line grows the bottom row) and with the chat keyboard up (the mode holds). If the 667×375 arena leaves the plate on the band, the fix commit adds the A.5.2 `TopBar` `compact?`. The result is recorded in Check 1's one commit, `docs(arena): record browser check 1 (L75-08)` (S7).

**Acceptance map:**
- A unit test covers the height-bound hand size helper: `hand-card-size.test.ts` (3 tests).
- Component tests at 844×390 and 667×375: mode `landscape-compact`, seats in the band container in turn order, hand and dock in one row: `$label: 8 seats in the band in turn order; …` (both rows).
- A 360×740 box stays portrait: `a 360×740 phone stays portrait, with the full strip and the ticker row`.
- Browser check on a landscape phone with 8 seats: browser check (Check 1).
- Review Focus 1 (mandatory): `arena-mode-crossing.test.tsx`, all 8 tests (card sheet, owner-local ×3, targeting, lifted drag, pressed drag, keyboard). The keyboard case follows open-question ruling 7: the mode holds while the field has focus and flips on blur, the draft kept (K12).
- The anchors L75-04 relies on, in every mode (S5): `keeps every first-game hint anchor and the tutorial anchors on arena elements in $mode (§14.3, S5)`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Hand size:** a quarter of the visible height. **Designer question:** is 65 px at 390 px tall right? Default: yes.
- **Compact strip captions hidden.** **Designer question** (above); default off.
- **One strip tree (L75-13 reuse).** The compact strip is the full strip's JSX with different classes, kit size and pill captions, not a second branch. L75-13 flips `compact` on a portrait phone while the player may be holding focus on the kit portrait or watching a resource flash, and a second branch would remount both.
- **Landscape composition** (open question 6). **Designer question**; default as A.3.
- **`SEAT_BOX['landscape-compact']` tuned to 76×92** (A.4.3 allows it; decisions line; K11). `seatRenderBox('landscape-compact')` therefore returns 76×92 from here on, and every domain's tests read `SEAT_BOX[mode]` or `seatRenderBox(mode)`, never a literal.
- **A `HAND_CARD_ASPECT` export** beyond A.5.1 (additive, accepted by K11), which L75-13 reads.
- **No `TopBar` / `LogTicker` `compact?`** unless Check 1 shows the 375 px landscape arena too short (A.5.2 allows it then).
- **New strings:** none.

```ts
// bottom-band.ts
export type BottomBand = 'full' | 'compact';
export const COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX: number;   // 52
export const PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX: number;       // 122 = 74 landing + 4 gap + 44 badge row (open-question ruling 2)
export const BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX: number;       // 48: the slack a compact band needs before it returns to full (S1)
export function bottomBandSavingsPx(viewportWidth: number): number;   // integer, rounded up
export function plateRect(mode: LayoutMode, arena: Size, height: number): Rect;
export function plateOverlapPx(layout: SeatLayout, arena: Size, plateHeight: number): number;
export function bottomBandFor(view: PlayingStateView, box: LayoutBox, current: BottomBand): BottomBand;
// arena-screen.tsx: <main data-bottom-band={band}>

// hand-scroll-cue.ts (Q87-4, designer 2026-11-03)
export type ScrollExtent = Pick<HTMLElement, 'scrollWidth' | 'clientWidth' | 'scrollLeft'>;
/** The edges of a horizontal scroller that have content hidden past them: 'left' once scrolled away from the start, 'right' while more lies beyond the end. */
export type HiddenEdges = 'none' | 'left' | 'right' | 'both';
export function hiddenEdges(region: ScrollExtent): HiddenEdges;
export function useHiddenEdges(region: HTMLElement | null): HiddenEdges;
// hand-fan.tsx: <span aria-hidden data-scroll-cue="start" | "end"> over the left / right edge of the hand scroller
```

The hand's fade is the admin tables' cue (`screens/admin/admin-scroll-cue.ts` and `SCROLL_CUE_CLASS` in `screens/admin/admin-ui.tsx`, L80-12), on both edges. That cue is private to `screens/admin/`, not a `ui/` piece: the arena may not import `screens/admin/` (import rule), and moving it to `ui/` would rewrite the admin tables and their tests for no gain here. So the hand fan carries its own copy of the same pattern.
