Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/bottom-band.test.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The compact bottom band geometry: designer 2026-11-03 "Bas plus court" (decisions.md); controller
 * ruling R5; plan Appendix A.6 and Review Focus 4; technical spec v7 §6.1, §6.4 (L75-13).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { layoutSeats, PLATE_BOX, SEAT_BOX, type LayoutBox } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import {
  BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX, bottomBandFor, bottomBandSavingsPx, COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX, PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX, plateOverlapPx, plateRect,
  type BottomBand,
} from './bottom-band';
import { SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX } from './seat-geometry';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const EIGHT = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8 });
const withArena = (box: LayoutBox, height: number): LayoutBox => ({ viewport: box.viewport, arena: { width: box.arena.width, height } });
const overlapAt = (view: PlayingStateView, box: LayoutBox, plate: number): number => plateOverlapPx(layoutSeats(view, box), box.arena, plate);
/** Two grid rows (an 8 px gutter over each 92 px row) over the 122 px footprint: from here the 8-seat full band clears the plate. */
const THRESHOLD = 2 * (SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX + SEAT_BOX.portrait.height) + PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX;

describe('plate geometry (A.6)', () => {
  it('draws the plate rect bottom-anchored and growing upward, as CentralPlate does', () => {
    expect(PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX).toBe(122);
    expect(plateRect('portrait', { width: 360, height: 214 }, PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX)).toEqual({ left: 120, top: 92, width: 120, height: 122 });
  });

  it('reproduces the Lot 74 gate with the empty 96 px plate: 82 px at 360×740 and 14 px at 375×812', () => {
    expect([overlapAt(EIGHT, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, PLATE_BOX.portrait.height), overlapAt(EIGHT, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone375, PLATE_BOX.portrait.height)]).toEqual([82, 14]);
  });

  it('counts no overlap for seats beside the plate (3 seats at 360×740)', () => {
    expect(overlapAt(makeArenaView(), ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX)).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('bottomBandSavingsPx (A.6)', () => {
  it('gives back the strip difference plus the hand height the 52 px cards save, rounded up', () => {
    expect(COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX).toBe(52);
    expect([bottomBandSavingsPx(360), bottomBandSavingsPx(375), bottomBandSavingsPx(768)]).toEqual([124, 128, 201]);
  });
});

describe('bottomBandFor (R5, Review Focus 4)', () => {
  it('is full outside portrait and before the arena is measured (0×0)', () => {
    for (const current of ['full', 'compact'] as const) {
      expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscape, current)).toBe('full');
      expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop, current)).toBe('full');
      expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, { viewport: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360.viewport, arena: { width: 0, height: 0 } }, current)).toBe('full');
    }
  });

  it('at 360×740 with 8 seats: compact under the threshold and still compact when the arena grows by the savings; full 1 px above it (Review Focus 4)', () => {
    const phone = ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360;
    const savings = bottomBandSavingsPx(phone.viewport.width);
    expect(THRESHOLD).toBe(322);
    expect(BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX).toBe(48);
    expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, withArena(phone, THRESHOLD - 1), 'full')).toBe('compact');
    expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, withArena(phone, THRESHOLD - 1 + savings), 'compact')).toBe('compact');
    expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, withArena(phone, THRESHOLD + 1), 'full')).toBe('full');
    // Compact returns to full only when the full band's arena would clear the plate by BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX (S1).
    expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, withArena(phone, THRESHOLD + savings + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX - 1), 'compact')).toBe('compact');
    expect(bottomBandFor(EIGHT, withArena(phone, THRESHOLD + savings + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX), 'compact')).toBe('full');
  });

  it('never flip-flops within BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX of the threshold, even when the strip saves 44 px more than the model (S1, Review Focus 4)', () => {
    const phone = ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360;
    const savings = bottomBandSavingsPx(phone.viewport.width);
    // `full` is the arena the full band leaves. A compact band adds the modelled savings plus what the model
    // misses: an upgraded-shield pill can add a fourth pill row to the full strip at 360 px, about 44 px.
    for (let full = THRESHOLD - BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX; full <= THRESHOLD + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX; full += 1) {
      for (const missed of [0, 44]) {
        let band: BottomBand = 'full';
        const seen: BottomBand[] = [];
        for (let pass = 0; pass < 4; pass += 1) {
          band = bottomBandFor(EIGHT, withArena(phone, band === 'compact' ? full + savings + missed : full), band);
          seen.push(band);
        }
        expect(seen.every((next) => next === seen[0])).toBe(true);
      }
    }
  });

  it('stays compact on an arena shorter than one seat row: the overlap only grows as the arena shrinks', () => {
    // A 2-seat Duplicator on a 360×640 phone: the full band leaves an arena of about 40 px (`duplicator360`'s 140 px on a phone
    // 100 px shorter). That is short of one 92 px seat, so `layoutSeats` falls back to its grid, whose rows start at the arena
    // top whatever its height.
    const view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 2, overrides: { self: makeSelf({ kitId: 'duplicator' }) } });
    const full: LayoutBox = { viewport: { width: 360, height: 640 }, arena: { width: 360, height: 40 } };
    expect(bottomBandFor(view, full, 'full')).toBe('compact');
    const compact = withArena(full, full.arena.height + bottomBandSavingsPx(full.viewport.width));
    expect(compact.arena.height).toBe(164);
    // The judged arena is 164 − (124 + 48) = −8 px: the seat sits wholly below the arena, still covered by the plate.
    expect(bottomBandFor(view, compact, 'compact')).toBe('compact');
  });

  it('a measured arena squeezed to 0 px tall is compact, never full for good: only a 0-wide arena is unmeasured', () => {
    const squeezed = withArena(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, 0);
    expect([bottomBandFor(EIGHT, squeezed, 'full'), bottomBandFor(EIGHT, squeezed, 'compact')]).toEqual(['compact', 'compact']);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: '8 seated at 360×740', view: EIGHT, box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360 },
    { label: '8 walk-in seats at 360×740', view: makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, pov: 'walk-in' }), box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360 },
    { label: '8 seated at 375×812', view: EIGHT, box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone375 },
    { label: '8 walk-in seats at 375×812', view: makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, pov: 'walk-in' }), box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone375 },
    { label: "a 2-seat Duplicator's two-row dock at 360×740", view: makeArenaView({ seatCount: 2, overrides: { self: makeSelf({ kitId: 'duplicator' }) } }), box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.duplicator360 },
  ])('$label: the full band covers a seat, the compact band clears the plate and stays compact (R5 acceptance)', ({ view, box }) => {
    expect(bottomBandFor(view, box, 'full')).toBe('compact');
    const compact = withArena(box, box.arena.height + bottomBandSavingsPx(box.viewport.width));
    expect(overlapAt(view, compact, PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX)).toBe(0);
    expect(bottomBandFor(view, compact, 'compact')).toBe('compact');
  });
});
```

That is 14 tests as Vitest counts them (3 + 1 + 5 + 5 `it.each` rows). The flip-flop test is S1's: without the hysteresis, a full-band arena 1–44 px under the threshold with the 44 px the model misses goes compact, then full, then compact. The two tests above fail for an overlap that is clipped to the plate's own extent (it reads 0 once the first grid row, which starts 8 px under the arena top, lies below the arena's bottom edge, that is for an arena of 8 px or less) and for a guard that treats `arena.height === 0` as unmeasured: the 2-seat case then flaps (40 px compact, 164 px full, 40 px again) and the squeezed arena stays full.

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-bottom-band.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * The compact bottom band on the arena: designer 2026-11-03 "Bas plus court" (decisions.md);
 * controller ruling R5; plan Appendix A.6 and Review Focus 4; technical spec v7 §6.1, §6.4 (L75-13).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { fireEvent, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi, type RenderUiOptions } from '../../test/render';
import { heightBoundHandCardWidthPx } from '../../ui/card/hand-card-size';
import { SEAT_BOX, type LayoutBox } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { makeCard, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import { BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX, bottomBandSavingsPx, COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX, PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX } from './bottom-band';
import { SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX } from './seat-geometry';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView, stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub } from './test-view';

const eight = (): PlayingStateView => makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: [makeCard('spy')] }) } });
let layout: ArenaLayoutStub | null = null;
afterEach(() => { layout?.restore(); layout = null; });
function setupAt(box: LayoutBox, view: PlayingStateView, options: RenderUiOptions = {}) {
  const stub = stubArenaLayout(box.viewport, box.arena);
  layout = stub;
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view });
  return { stub, props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, options) };
}
function one(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (found === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return found;
}
const PRIMARY = { pointerId: 1, isPrimary: true, button: 0 } as const;
const band = (): string | null => one('main[data-arena="next"]').getAttribute('data-bottom-band');
const strip = (): string | null => one('[data-self-strip]').getAttribute('data-self-strip');
const handWidth = (): string => one('[data-hand-fan]').style.getPropertyValue('--hand-card-w');

describe('the compact bottom band (R5)', () => {
  it('compacts at 360×740 with 8 seats: one-row strip, 52 px hand cards, the dock keeps its controls, every anchor stays', () => {
    const { props } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, eight());
    expect([band(), strip(), handWidth()]).toEqual(['compact', 'compact', `${String(COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX)}px`]);
    const dock = one('[data-arena-region="dock"]');
    expect([
      within(dock).getByRole('button', { name: /^Draw, plus / }),
      within(dock).getByRole('button', { name: 'Shop' }),
      within(dock).getByRole('button', { name: /^Unspy/ }),
    ]).toHaveLength(3);
    for (const selector of ['[data-zone="resources"][data-hint-anchor="resources"]', '[data-zone="private-zone"] [data-zone="kit-portrait"]', '[data-zone="hand"][data-hint-anchor="hand"]', '[data-zone="specials"][data-hint-anchor="specials"]']) {
      expect(document.querySelector(selector)).not.toBeNull();
    }
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: '4 seats at 360×740', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, seatCount: 4 },
    { label: '8 seats on a 768×1024 tablet', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.tablet, seatCount: 8 },
  ])('keeps the full band for $label', ({ box, seatCount }) => {
    setupAt(box, makeArenaView({ seatCount }));
    expect([band(), strip(), handWidth()]).toEqual(['full', 'full', '']);
  });

  it('stays compact when the arena grows by exactly the savings, across two more ResizeObserver callbacks (Review Focus 4)', () => {
    const { viewport, arena } = ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360;
    const { stub } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, eight());
    expect(band()).toBe('compact');
    const grown = arena.height + bottomBandSavingsPx(viewport.width);
    stub.setArena(arena.width, grown);
    expect(band()).toBe('compact');
    stub.setArena(arena.width, grown);
    stub.setArena(arena.width, grown);
    expect(band()).toBe('compact');
  });

  it('returns to the full band once the full band would clear the plate by BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX (S1)', () => {
    const { viewport, arena } = ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360;
    const { stub } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, eight());
    // The full band's arena clears two grid rows (8 px gutter + 92 px each) over the 122 px footprint.
    const threshold = 2 * (SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX + SEAT_BOX.portrait.height) + PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX;
    stub.setArena(arena.width, threshold + bottomBandSavingsPx(viewport.width) + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX - 1);
    expect(band()).toBe('compact');
    stub.setArena(arena.width, threshold + bottomBandSavingsPx(viewport.width) + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX);
    expect([band(), strip(), handWidth()]).toEqual(['full', 'full', '']);
  });

  it('is never compact in landscape-compact or on desktop', () => {
    const { stub } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, eight());
    stub.setViewport(844, 390);
    expect([band(), strip(), handWidth()]).toEqual(['full', 'compact', `${String(heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(390))}px`]);
    stub.setViewport(1440, 900);
    expect([band(), strip(), handWidth()]).toEqual(['full', 'full', '']);
  });

  it('cancels a lifted drag when the band flips under it, and sends nothing, though its release lands on a valid seat (A.5.3)', () => {
    // The arena height, the strip and the hand card width all change with the band, so the drop rects measured at lift are stale.
    const view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: [makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' })] }) } });
    const { viewport, arena } = ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360;
    const { stub, props } = setupAt(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360, view, { reducedMotion: true });
    expect(band()).toBe('compact');
    fireEvent.pointerDown(within(one('[data-arena-region="hand"]')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage' }), { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 100, clientY: 700 });
    fireEvent.pointerMove(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 103, clientY: 688 });
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="hand-drag-ghost"]')).not.toBeNull();
    const threshold = 2 * (SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX + SEAT_BOX.portrait.height) + PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX;
    stub.setArena(arena.width, threshold + bottomBandSavingsPx(viewport.width) + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX);
    expect(band()).toBe('full');
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="hand-drag-ghost"]')).toBeNull();
    // Every element reports the arena box here, so without the cancel this release would play the card on a seat.
    fireEvent.pointerUp(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 50, clientY: 50 });
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});
```

That is 7 tests as Vitest counts them (1 + 2 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1). The last one is the band's twin of `arena-mode-crossing.test.tsx`'s mode-change drag test (A.5.3).

Append to `apps/client/src/screens/arena/hand-fan.test.tsx` (it already imports `fireEvent`, `renderUi`, `makeCard`; the `fan` and `viewWith` helpers are in the file). The scroller's box is stubbed after the first render and a `scroll` event makes the hook read it again, as `screens/admin/admin-ui.test.tsx` does for the table cue:

```tsx
describe('hand scroll fades (Q87-4, designer 2026-11-03; L75-13)', () => {
  const SEVEN = ['basic-attack', 'strong-attack', 'super-attack', 'spy', 'shield', 'tax', 'thief'] as const;
  /** The hand's scroller; happy-dom has no layout, so a test gives it the box a browser would report. */
  function stubScroller(box: { scrollWidth: number; clientWidth: number; scrollLeft: number }): HTMLElement {
    const region = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-hand-fan] [data-scroll-axis="x"]');
    if (region === null) throw new Error('no hand scroller');
    Object.defineProperties(region, {
      scrollWidth: { configurable: true, get: () => box.scrollWidth },
      clientWidth: { configurable: true, get: () => box.clientWidth },
      scrollLeft: { configurable: true, get: () => box.scrollLeft },
    });
    return region;
  }
  const fades = (): [boolean, boolean] => [
    document.querySelector('[data-hand-fan] [data-scroll-cue="start"]') !== null,
    document.querySelector('[data-hand-fan] [data-scroll-cue="end"]') !== null,
  ];

  it('with 7 cards that overflow: the right fade shows and the left does not; scrolled away from the start both show; at the end only the left stays', () => {
    renderUi(fan(viewWith({ hand: SEVEN.map((id) => makeCard(id)) })));
    expect(fades()).toEqual([false, false]);
    const box = { scrollWidth: 900, clientWidth: 360, scrollLeft: 0 };
    const region = stubScroller(box);
    fireEvent.scroll(region);
    expect(fades()).toEqual([false, true]);
    box.scrollLeft = 200;
    fireEvent.scroll(region);
    expect(fades()).toEqual([true, true]);
    box.scrollLeft = 540; // scrollWidth − clientWidth: the end
    fireEvent.scroll(region);
    expect(fades()).toEqual([true, false]);
    // Decorative: not announced, and a tap or drag reaches the card under it.
    const left = document.querySelector('[data-scroll-cue="start"]');
    expect([left?.getAttribute('aria-hidden'), left?.className.includes('pointer-events-none')]).toEqual(['true', true]);
  });

  it('with no overflow: neither fade shows', () => {
    renderUi(fan(viewWith({ hand: SEVEN.slice(0, 3).map((id) => makeCard(id)) })));
    const region = stubScroller({ scrollWidth: 360, clientWidth: 360, scrollLeft: 0 });
    fireEvent.scroll(region);
    expect(fades()).toEqual([false, false]);
  });
});
```

That is 2 tests. The fades are static, so reduced motion changes nothing and needs no test of its own.

  - **`bottom-band.ts`** (pure, no React; doc cites R5, the "Bas plus court" decisions entry, §6.1, §6.4).

    - Module-local constants, each with its source:
      - `FULL_STRIP_PX = 160`: the full strip at 360 and 375 px with no thumb and no badge (tile portrait plus three pill rows; L74-26 G4, measured at 375);
      - `COMPACT_STRIP_PX = 61`: `py-1` 8 plus the 53 px thumb-portrait row;
      - `HAND_VIEWPORT_SHARE = 0.19`: the Card `hand` clamp's `19vw`.
    - `bottomBandSavingsPx(w)` is `Math.ceil((FULL_STRIP_PX - COMPACT_STRIP_PX) + HAND_CARD_ASPECT * (clamp(HAND_CARD_MIN_W_PX, w * HAND_VIEWPORT_SHARE, HAND_CARD_MAX_W_PX) - COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX))`. Rounding up keeps it integral and never under the real saving (see Decisions).
    - `plateRect(mode, arena, height)`: `center = plateCenterFor(mode, arena)` and `box = PLATE_BOX[mode]`. The bottom is `center.centerY + box.height / 2` (CentralPlate's `bottom` style); return `{ left: center.centerX - box.width / 2, top: bottom - height, width: box.width, height }`.
    - `plateOverlapPx(layout, arena, h)`: for each `seatRect(center, layout.mode)` over `seatCenters(layout, arena)` that meets `plateRect(layout.mode, arena, h)` horizontally, count `Math.max(0, seatRect.bottom − plateRect.top)`, and return the max. 0 when no seat meets the plate. This is monotone in the arena's height: the plate is pinned to the arena's bottom edge, so a seat drawn below the arena is covered too, and the overlap never falls as the arena shrinks. Do not clip it to the plate's own extent: the grid places its rows from the arena top whatever the arena height (`seat-geometry.ts`), and `layoutSeats` falls back to that grid for an arena shorter than a seat, so a clipped overlap reads 0 for an arena of 8 px or less and the band would flap.
    - `bottomBandFor(view, box, current)`:
      - `'full'` unless `layoutModeFor(box.viewport) === 'portrait'`, and `'full'` while `box.arena.width === 0` (unmeasured: the arena is 0×0 before the first measure, and a measured region keeps its width). A measured width with any height, even 0, is judged;
      - otherwise it judges `judged = { viewport: box.viewport, arena: { width: box.arena.width, height: box.arena.height - (current === 'compact' ? bottomBandSavingsPx(box.viewport.width) + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX : 0) } }`, and the result is `plateOverlapPx(layoutSeats(view, judged), judged.arena, PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX) > 0 ? 'compact' : 'full'`.
      - So full → compact keeps the plain threshold, and compact → full needs the full band's arena to clear the plate with `BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX` to spare (S1).
  - **`hand-scroll-cue.ts`** (Q87-4; the admin tables' cue, `screens/admin/admin-scroll-cue.ts`, on both edges). Pure, then the hook:
    - `type ScrollExtent = Pick<HTMLElement, 'scrollWidth' | 'clientWidth' | 'scrollLeft'>`, `type HiddenEdges = 'none' | 'left' | 'right' | 'both'`, and a module constant `EDGE_SLACK_PX = 1` ("browsers round a fractional scroll position, so one pixel short of an edge counts as the edge", as the admin cue says).
    - `hiddenEdges(region)`: `left` is `region.scrollLeft > EDGE_SLACK_PX`; `right` is `region.scrollWidth - region.clientWidth - region.scrollLeft > EDGE_SLACK_PX`. A region that does not overflow (and happy-dom's zeros) has neither. Return `'both' | 'left' | 'right' | 'none'` from the two booleans.
    - `useHiddenEdges(region: HTMLElement | null): HiddenEdges`: `useSyncExternalStore` over `useCallback`ed `subscribe` and `read`, exactly as the admin hook: `subscribe` adds a passive `scroll` listener to the region and a `ResizeObserver` on the region and on its `firstElementChild` (new cards, a resize), and removes both; `read` is `region === null ? 'none' : hiddenEdges(region)`. The snapshot is a string, so it is stable. Doc comment cites spec v7 §6.1 item 4 and the admin cue (L80-12).
  - **`hand-fan.tsx`** (Q87-4, designer 2026-11-03).
    - `const [region, setRegion] = useState<HTMLDivElement | null>(null); const hidden = useHiddenEdges(region);` in `HandFan`, and `ref={setRegion}` on its `ScrollArea` (the primitive forwards `ref`; the region is state so the hook sees it mount, as in `AdminDataTable`).
    - The root gains `relative` (its classes become `@container relative h-full w-full …`), so the fades position against it, not the arena.
    - Two sibling `<span>`s after the `ScrollArea`, each only while its edge is hidden (`hidden === 'left' || hidden === 'both'`, and `'right'`): `aria-hidden="true"`, `data-scroll-cue="start"` or `"end"`, and a module constant `HAND_CUE_CLASS = 'pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 w-10 from-v7-bg-0 to-transparent'` plus `left-0 bg-linear-to-r` (start) or `right-0 bg-linear-to-l` (end). The admin cue is `bg-linear-to-l from-v7-panel to-transparent`, `w-10`; the hand sits on the ground, so it fades from `--bg-0` (`from-v7-bg-0`, a token in `ui/tokens.css`).
    - No transition and no `data-motion`: the fades appear and disappear with the scroll position, so they are the same under reduced motion. They take no clicks, so a tap, a long press or a drag reaches the card. A selected or focused card (`z-10`) rises above a fade, which keeps it readable.

  - **`use-hand-drag.ts`** (a drag across a band flip). `HandDragInput.band?: BottomBand` (a type import from `./bottom-band`). L75-03's cancel effect compared `input.mode` with a `previousMode` ref; it now compares the key `` `${input.mode ?? ''}:${input.band ?? ''}` `` with a `previousLayout` ref, and calls `onPointerCancel()` when the key changes. With both absent, nothing changes. Update the hook's doc comment: "the drop rects belong to one layout: a mode or band change cancels the gesture".

```
## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] "Bas plus court": the compact band's numbers (L75-13)

Source: the plan-commit entry "Bas plus court" (designer 2026-11-03, R5), which this one only
quantifies; plan Appendix A.6 and Review Focus 4; the Lot 74 gate (G1, G2, G4).

- **Trigger.** Portrait only. The band compacts when a seat would meet the plate's 122 px footprint
  (open-question ruling 2) above the full band. The model reproduces the gate's measurements
  (82 px at 360×740, 14 px at 375×812 with the empty 96 px plate).
- **Sizes.** Compact hand cards are 52 px wide. The compact strip is 61 px against the full
  strip's 160 px at 360–375 px. The savings are `ceil(99 + 1.5 × (clamp(64, 19vw, 120) − 52))`:
  124 px at 360 and 128 px at 375. An 8-seat table at 360×740 gets a 338 px arena, 16 px over the
  322 px it needs.
- **No flap (S1).** Flow badges and thumbs cost both strips the same, and the savings are rounded up.
  An upgraded-shield pill can add a fourth pill row to the full strip at 360 px, so the model can
  miss about 44 px of saving. A compact band therefore returns to full only when the full band's
  arena would clear the plate with `BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX` = 48 px to spare; full → compact keeps the
  plain threshold. From about 385 px the full strip drops to two pill rows, so the constant
  over-estimates and the band only returns to full later. Check 1 re-measures the arena heights,
  and this note is updated if they differ (open-question ruling 3).
- **Monotone overlap.** A seat counts as covered by `max(0, seat bottom − plate top)`, not by its
  intersection with the plate, because the plate is pinned to the arena's bottom edge and the grid
  places its rows from the arena top. With the intersection, an arena of 8 px or less read 0, and a
  short compact arena (a 2-seat Duplicator at 360×640: 40 px full, 164 px compact, judged −8 px)
  flapped between the bands. The unmeasured guard is a 0-wide arena only.
- **Designer question:** at 52 px the longest card names fall to the 10 px floor and may wrap
  mid-word (DESIGN.md › Card › Name). Accept, or hide names at that size? Default: accept.
- **Hand scroll cue (Q87-4, designer 2026-11-03: fade added).** A hand that overflows shows a
  static fade on its right edge, and on its left edge once scrolled away from the start. It
  reuses the admin tables' pattern (`screens/admin/admin-scroll-cue.ts`, L80-12) in
  `hand-scroll-cue.ts`, because that code is private to `screens/admin/`; the fade is `--bg-0`
  into nothing, 40 px wide, and static under reduced motion.
```

```
it never flaps. A band flip also cancels a lifted hand drag, and an
overflowing hand fades at its edges (Q87-4, designer 2026-11-03).
```

**Browser check (controller, after review).** It runs in batched **Check 1** after L75-08, at 360×740 and 375×812:
- 8 seats seated and walk-in, and a 2-seat Duplicator dock: no seat is covered by the plate, with an empty plate and with one incoming badge row. Record the 170 px plate (chips plus badges, or three or more incoming) as a gate item.
- Measure the full and compact arena heights against `ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone360`, `phone375` and `duplicator360`, and the full and compact strip heights (160 and 61). If they differ, the fix commit updates the boxes and constants and amends the numbers note.
- No flapping: watch the band across a turn change, an upgraded shield (the full strip may take a fourth pill row at 360) and a flow badge appearing.
- At 390×844 with 8 seats, the band compacts (footprint 122).
- The hand's edge fades (Q87-4): with enough cards to overflow at 360×740 (7 cards), only the right edge fades; scrolled away from the start, both do; at the end, only the left. The fade reads as subtle over the ground's bottom halo, and a tap or drag on a card under it still works.

The result is recorded in Check 1's one commit, `docs(arena): record browser check 1 (L75-08)` (S7). The L75-12 gate re-checks all of it.

**Acceptance map:**
- At 360×740 and 375×812 with 8 seats, and with a 2-seat Duplicator dock, no seat is covered by the central plate, checked by a pure geometry test: `bottom-band.test.ts` › `$label: the full band covers a seat, the compact band clears the plate …` (5 rows).
- … and by the Lot 75 gate: browser check (Check 1, then L75-12).
- The layout choice is recorded in `decisions.md` before coding: the plan-commit "Bas plus court" entry (R5, S8); this task appends the numbers note as a new dated entry.
- Review Focus 4 (mandatory): pure `at 360×740 with 8 seats: compact under the threshold …` and `never flip-flops within BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX of the threshold, … (S1, Review Focus 4)`; component `stays compact when the arena grows by exactly the savings, across two more ResizeObserver callbacks (Review Focus 4)` and `returns to the full band once the full band would clear the plate by BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX (S1)`.
- The hand scroll cue (Q87-4, designer 2026-11-03: fade added): `hand-fan.test.tsx` › `hand scroll fades …` (2 tests: with 7 cards that overflow the right fade shows and the left does not, scrolled away from the start both show; with no overflow neither), then the browser check (Check 1).
- A lifted drag does not survive a band flip: `cancels a lifted drag when the band flips under it, and sends nothing …` (`arena-bottom-band.test.tsx`).
- No flap on an arena shorter than one seat row: `stays compact on an arena shorter than one seat row …` and `a measured arena squeezed to 0 px tall is compact, never full for good …`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **The strip saving is a measured constant** (`FULL_STRIP_PX` 160 minus `COMPACT_STRIP_PX` 61). The full strip's height depends on pill wrapping (font metrics), which a pure helper cannot read. It is exact at 360–375 px with no extra pill row, and an over-estimate wider, which only delays a return to full.
- **Flow badges and thumbs keep their own lines in the compact strip,** so they never change the savings.
- **Hysteresis (S1).** `BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX = 48`: a compact band returns to full only when the full band's arena would clear the plate with 48 px to spare, which covers the about 44 px the model misses when an upgraded-shield pill adds a fourth pill row at 360 px. Full → compact keeps the plain threshold. The judging-the-full-band rule alone did not cover that miss.
- **The overlap is monotone in arena height.** `plateOverlapPx` counts `max(0, seatRect.bottom − plateRect.top)` for each seat that meets the plate horizontally, and the unmeasured guard is `box.arena.width === 0` only. The plate sits at the arena's bottom edge and the grid places its rows from the arena top, so an overlap clipped to the plate reads 0 for an arena of 8 px or less (and the band flaps for any arena shorter than one seat row, such as the 2-seat Duplicator at 360×640: 40 px full, 164 px compact), and a measured 0 px tall arena would stay `full` for good. Every value this task pins (82 and 14 px with the empty plate, 0 at 322 px, the five acceptance rows, the flip-flop sweep) is unchanged by it.
- **`bottomBandSavingsPx` rounds up**, which keeps the RF4 arithmetic integral.
- **A band flip cancels a lifted drag.** L75-03 cancels a drag on a mode change because its drop rects were measured at lift. A flip of the portrait band changes the arena height, the strip and the hand card width under the lifted card in the same way, so `useHandDrag` keys the cancel on `${mode}:${band}` (L75-13 owns the change, in `use-hand-drag.ts`). The alternative, a gate item, would leave a card droppable on a stale zone.
- **Q87-4, Designer 2026-11-03: fade added.** `HandFan` shows a static fade on the scroller's right edge while it overflows, and on its left edge once it is scrolled away from the start (`hand-scroll-cue.ts`, the admin tables' cue on both edges). It sits in `screens/arena/`, not `ui/`: the admin cue is private to `screens/admin/`, which the arena may not import. DESIGN.md › Layout gets the line anyway. It is static, so reduced motion needs no variant.
- **Q87-1, Q87-2:** not answered, so the defaults ship: no change to the dock (R16).
- **Designer question:** 52 px names at the 10 px floor (numbers note).
- **New strings:** none.

```ts
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * FX measuring on rendered anchors: L51-09, L51-13, L51-14; controller ruling R6 (L75-08). Card
 * ghosts land on the plate, the log endpoint is the first visible one, and collapsed chrome is no
 * FX endpoint. happy-dom has no layout: each anchor is a detached element with a stubbed rect.
 */
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { DECK_CARD_FLYOUT_HEIGHT, DECK_CARD_FLYOUT_WIDTH, tokenFlyoutPovSelector, tokenFlyoutResourceSelector, tokenFlyoutSeatSelector } from '../ui/fx-anchors';
import { measureDeckCardFlyout, measureFeltCenterRect, measurePlayCardGhost, measureTargetingCue, queryActionLogAnchor, queryFirstVisible, queryPlayerFxAnchor } from './play-flyout';
import { tokenFlyoutUsesCardChrome, type DomRectLite } from './table-fx-types';

const anchors: HTMLElement[] = [];
function anchor(attributes: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, rect: DOMRect, parent: HTMLElement = document.body): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.createElement('div');
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(attributes)) element.setAttribute(name, value);
  parent.append(element);
  anchors.push(element);
  vi.spyOn(element, 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue(rect);
  return element;
}
const centre = (rect: DomRectLite | null | undefined): readonly number[] =>
  rect === null || rect === undefined ? [] : [rect.left + rect.width / 2, rect.top + rect.height / 2];
/** The plate (landing frame inside), p2's seat with its portrait, and the log ticker. */
function plateSeatAndLog(): HTMLElement {
  const plate = anchor({ 'data-zone': 'pending' }, new DOMRect(100, 300, 120, 122));
  anchor({ 'data-plate-landing': '' }, new DOMRect(134, 310, 52, 74), plate);
  const seat = anchor({ 'data-zone': 'opponent-seat', 'data-player-id': 'p2' }, new DOMRect(10, 10, 76, 92));
  anchor({ 'data-zone': 'opponent-portrait' }, new DOMRect(14, 24, 40, 53), seat);
  anchor({ 'data-zone': 'log-collapsed' }, new DOMRect(0, 60, 400, 44));
  return plate;
}

afterEach(() => {
  for (const element of anchors.splice(0)) element.remove();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('measureTokenFlyout playerId (L51-09 / L51-13)', () => {
  it('scopes opponent resources to the seat and POV to the dock', () => {
    // unchanged body: the five selector expectations of the current test
  });

  it('sizes buy/sell card ghosts toward the felt center (L51-13)', () => {
    expect([DECK_CARD_FLYOUT_WIDTH, DECK_CARD_FLYOUT_HEIGHT]).toEqual([48, 72]);
    const plate = plateSeatAndLog();
    const buy = measureDeckCardFlyout('p2', 'verso.png', 'buy');
    expect([centre(buy?.from), centre(buy?.to)]).toEqual([[160, 347], [34, 50.5]]);
    expect([buy?.from.width, buy?.from.height, buy?.to.width, buy?.to.height]).toEqual([48, 72, 48, 72]);
    const sell = measureDeckCardFlyout('p2', 'verso.png', 'sell');
    expect([centre(sell?.from), centre(sell?.to)]).toEqual([[34, 50.5], [160, 347]]);
    plate.remove();
    // With no plate and no felt the log alone is never a ghost target (L51-13).
    expect(measureDeckCardFlyout('p2', 'verso.png', 'buy')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('token chip chrome is asCard-only — width must not promote resource icons (L51-14)', () => {
    expect(tokenFlyoutUsesCardChrome({ asCard: true })).toBe(true);
    expect(tokenFlyoutUsesCardChrome({})).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('arena FX endpoints (R6, L75-08)', () => {
  it('aims card ghosts at the plate landing, then the plate, then the felt, never the hand band', () => {
    const felt = anchor({ 'data-zone': 'felt' }, new DOMRect(0, 0, 400, 500));
    const plate = anchor({ 'data-zone': 'pending' }, new DOMRect(100, 300, 120, 122), felt);
    const landing = anchor({ 'data-plate-landing': '' }, new DOMRect(134, 310, 52, 74), plate);
    anchor({ 'data-zone': 'card-band' }, new DOMRect(0, 600, 400, 130));
    anchor({ 'data-instance-id': 'atk-1' }, new DOMRect(30, 620, 64, 96));
    expect(centre(measureFeltCenterRect())).toEqual([160, 347]);
    expect(centre(measurePlayCardGhost('me', 'art.png', 'atk-1')?.to)).toEqual([160, 347]);
    vi.spyOn(landing, 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue(new DOMRect(0, 0, 0, 0));
    expect(centre(measureFeltCenterRect())).toEqual([160, 361]);
    plate.remove();
    expect(centre(measureFeltCenterRect())).toEqual([200, 250]);
    felt.remove();

  });

  it('the log endpoint is the first visible of the panel and the toggle (Review Focus 5)', () => {

    const toggle = anchor({ 'data-zone': 'log-collapsed' }, new DOMRect(1396, 100, 44, 44));

    expect(queryActionLogAnchor()).toBe(panel);
    expect(queryFirstVisible(['[data-zone="nowhere"]'])).toBeNull();
  });

  it('collapsed chrome is no FX endpoint: a player with no seat has no anchor and no cue (R6)', () => {
    anchor({ 'data-zone': 'opponents-collapsed' }, new DOMRect(0, 0, 120, 44));
    anchor({ 'data-zone': 'incoming-collapsed' }, new DOMRect(0, 50, 44, 44));
    expect([queryPlayerFxAnchor('p9'), measureTargetingCue('p9', 'me')]).toEqual([null, null]);
  });
});
```

```ts
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Token flyout overlay chrome — L51-14 and §8.3, rewritten from a source-text read as an overlay
 * behavior test (technical spec v7 §12.1; classification row for this file; L75-08).
 */
import { createElement } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../test/render';
import { TableFxContext, type TableFxContextValue } from './table-fx-hooks';
import { TableFxOverlay } from './table-fx-overlay';
import type { TableFxEvent } from './table-fx-types';

function tokenEvent(id: string, asCard: boolean): Extract<TableFxEvent, { kind: 'tokenFlyout' }> {
  return {
    id, kind: 'tokenFlyout', artUrl: `${id}.png`,
    from: { left: 0, top: 0, width: 64, height: 64 }, to: { left: 200, top: 400, width: 40, height: 40 },
    ...(asCard ? { asCard: true } : {}), expiresAt: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
  };
}
function overlay(events: readonly TableFxEvent[], reducedMotion: boolean) {
  const value: TableFxContextValue = { enqueue: vi.fn(), events };
  return renderUi(createElement(TableFxContext.Provider, { value }, createElement(TableFxOverlay)), { reducedMotion });
}
const drawn = (): (string | null)[] => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-zone="table-fx"] [data-fx]'), (node) => node.getAttribute('data-fx'));

describe('measureTokenFlyout playerId (L51-09 / L51-13)', () => {
  it('token chip chrome is asCard-only — width must not promote resource icons (L51-14) (table-fx-overlay)', () => {
    overlay([tokenEvent('chip', false), tokenEvent('ghost', true)], false);
    expect(drawn()).toEqual(['token-flyout', 'card-flyout']);
  });

  it('draws no chip and no card ghost under reduced motion (§8.3)', () => {
    overlay([tokenEvent('chip', false), tokenEvent('ghost', true)], true);
    expect(drawn()).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * The FX anchor contract on the arena: technical spec v7 §6.12, §8.1, §8.3; controller ruling R6;
 * plan Appendix A.7.1 and Review Focus 5; Appendix A `table-fx-layer`, `table-fx-token-chips`,
 * `table-fx-card-ghosts`, `table-fx-threat`, `table-fx-resolution-elim-reward`, `table-banners`
 * (L75-08). happy-dom has no layout: `rectFor` gives each anchor its own box, so every measure
 * names the element it read; any other element reports the arena box.
 */
import type { PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import {
  measureBuyCardFlyout, measureBuySpecialFlyout, measureDirectedTokenFlyout, measureFeltCenterRect, measurePlayCardGhost,
  measureTargetingCue, measureTokenFlyout, queryActionLogAnchor, queryPendingFlashAnchor, queryPlayerFxAnchor,
} from '../../fx/play-flyout';
import type { DomRectLite } from '../../fx/table-fx-types';
import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { makeCard, makePending, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView, stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub } from './test-view';

const SEAT_P1 = '[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="p1"]';
const BOX = {
  p1Portrait: new DOMRect(14, 24, 40, 53), p1Life: new DOMRect(58, 26, 28, 16), p1Seat: new DOMRect(10, 10, 76, 92),
  ownKit: new DOMRect(20, 560, 40, 53), ownPoint: new DOMRect(120, 570, 44, 28), landing: new DOMRect(170, 380, 52, 74),
  atk: new DOMRect(30, 650, 64, 96), hand: new DOMRect(16, 650, 200, 96), specials: new DOMRect(240, 650, 64, 96),
  ticker: new DOMRect(8, 96, 374, 44), toggle: new DOMRect(1396, 100, 44, 44), logPanel: new DOMRect(1110, 160, 320, 640),
} as const;
/** Descendant selectors first: an element takes the first box whose selector it matches. */
const ANCHOR_BOXES: readonly (readonly [string, DOMRect])[] = [
  [`${SEAT_P1} [data-zone="opponent-portrait"]`, BOX.p1Portrait],
  [`${SEAT_P1} [data-resource-kind="life"]`, BOX.p1Life],
  [SEAT_P1, BOX.p1Seat],
  ['[data-zone="private-zone"] [data-zone="kit-portrait"]', BOX.ownKit],
  ['[data-zone="resources"] [data-resource-kind="point"]', BOX.ownPoint],
  ['[data-plate-landing]', BOX.landing],
  ['[data-instance-id="atk-1"]', BOX.atk],
  ['[data-zone="hand"]', BOX.hand],
  ['[data-zone="specials"]', BOX.specials],
  ['[data-arena-region="ticker"] [data-zone="log-collapsed"][aria-haspopup]', BOX.ticker],
  ['[data-zone="log-collapsed"]', BOX.toggle],
  ['[data-zone="action-log-panel"]', BOX.logPanel],
];
const rectFor = (element: Element): DOMRect | undefined =>
  element.hasAttribute('data-stand-in') ? new DOMRect(0, 0, 0, 0) : ANCHOR_BOXES.find(([selector]) => element.matches(selector))?.[1];
/** Index 0 fans a chip 5 px left, scaled 0.4 on arrival (`fannedChip`, fx/play-flyout.ts). */
const FAN = { from: -5, to: -2 } as const;
function expectCentredOn(rect: DomRectLite | null | undefined, box: DOMRect, dx = 0): void {
  if (rect === null || rect === undefined) throw new Error('no rect measured');
  expect(rect.left + rect.width / 2).toBeCloseTo(box.left + box.width / 2 + dx, 6);
  expect(rect.top + rect.height / 2).toBeCloseTo(box.top + box.height / 2, 6);
}
const FX_VIEW: PlayingStateView = makeArenaView({ overrides: {
  self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: [makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' })], specialCards: [makeCard('mega-attack', { instanceId: 'mega-1' })] }),
  pendingEffects: [makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'basic-attack' })],
} });

let layout: ArenaLayoutStub | null = null;
afterEach(() => { layout?.restore(); layout = null; });
function one(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (found === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return found;
}
const region = (name: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);

describe('FX anchor contract (R6, A.7.1)', () => {
  it.each([
    { mode: 'portrait', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390, log: BOX.ticker, logZone: 'log-collapsed' },
    { mode: 'landscape-compact', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscape, log: BOX.ticker, logZone: 'log-collapsed' },
    { mode: 'desktop', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop, log: BOX.logPanel, logZone: 'action-log-panel' },
  ])('resolves every FX endpoint to a rendered arena element in $mode', ({ mode, box, log, logZone }) => {
    layout = stubArenaLayout(box.viewport, box.arena, { rectFor });
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: FX_VIEW });
    renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    expect(one('main[data-arena="next"]').getAttribute('data-layout-mode')).toBe(mode);
    // Seat: the token-chip anchor, the targeting cue and the elimination beat read it.
    expect(queryPlayerFxAnchor('p1')).toBe(one(SEAT_P1));
    expect(measureTargetingCue('p1', 'me')?.from).toEqual({ left: 10, top: 10, width: 76, height: 92 });
    // POV, its values and its kit portrait; the seat's value and portrait (a Thief steal).
    expect(queryPlayerFxAnchor('me')).toBe(region('self-strip'));
    expectCentredOn(measureDirectedTokenFlyout('life', 'log', { playerId: 'p1' })?.to, BOX.p1Life, FAN.to);
    const gain = measureTokenFlyout('point', 'gain');
    expectCentredOn(gain?.from, log, FAN.from);
    expectCentredOn(gain?.to, BOX.ownPoint, FAN.to);
    const steal = measureDirectedTokenFlyout('point', { playerId: 'p1' }, { playerId: 'me' });
    expectCentredOn(steal?.from, BOX.p1Portrait, FAN.from);
    expectCentredOn(steal?.to, BOX.ownKit, FAN.to);
    // Card ghosts: the hand instance to the plate (48 × 72), buys to the hand and specials groups.
    const ghost = measurePlayCardGhost('me', 'art.png', 'atk-1');
    expectCentredOn(ghost?.from, BOX.atk);
    expectCentredOn(ghost?.to, BOX.landing);
    expect([ghost?.to.width, ghost?.to.height]).toEqual([48, 72]);
    expectCentredOn(measureFeltCenterRect(), BOX.landing);
    expectCentredOn(measureBuyCardFlyout('spy')?.to, BOX.hand);
    expectCentredOn(measureBuySpecialFlyout()?.to, BOX.specials);
    // Resolution flash, log, reward pulse (dock), last fallback (felt).
    expect(queryPendingFlashAnchor('e1')?.getAttribute('data-pending-id')).toBe('e1');
    expect(queryActionLogAnchor()?.getAttribute('data-zone')).toBe(logZone);
    expect(one('[data-zone="dock"]')).toBe(region('dock'));
    expect(one('[data-zone="felt"]')).toBe(region('arena'));
    // The banner host reads no anchor: it sits on the visual viewport box in every mode.
    const banners = one('[data-zone="table-banners"]');
    expect([banners.style.width, banners.style.height]).toEqual([`${String(box.viewport.width)}px`, `${String(box.viewport.height)}px`]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('measures a log chip from the toggle while the desktop panel is collapsed, and from its scrollport once expanded (Review Focus 5)', async () => {
    layout = stubArenaLayout(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop.viewport, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop.arena, { rectFor });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...makeArenaProps({ view: FX_VIEW })} />);
    const toggle = within(region('ticker')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Action log' });
    await user.click(toggle);
    // A mounted, zero-size log list (as a depth-hidden log sheet leaves) must not swallow the toggle.
    const standIn = document.createElement('div');
    standIn.setAttribute('data-zone', 'action-log-panel');
    standIn.setAttribute('data-stand-in', '');
    document.body.append(standIn);
    expect(queryActionLogAnchor()).toBe(toggle);
    expectCentredOn(measureTokenFlyout('point', 'gain')?.from, BOX.toggle, FAN.from);
    await user.click(toggle);
    expect(queryActionLogAnchor()).toBe(one('[data-arena-region="ticker"] [data-zone="action-log-panel"]'));
    expectCentredOn(measureTokenFlyout('point', 'gain')?.from, BOX.logPanel, FAN.from);
    standIn.remove();
  });
});
```

That is 4 tests as Vitest counts them (3 `it.each` rows + 1).

```
## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] V7 FX anchors: the plate is the felt centre, the log is visible-first, collapsed chrome retires (L75-08)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.1, §6.4, §6.9, §6.12; controller ruling R6 and open-question rulings 4
and 12 (Lot 75 plan, Appendix A.7); `docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md` (fx rows).

- **Plate target.** `measureFeltCenterRect` returns a 48×72 rect on the plate's landing frame
  (`[data-plate-landing]`, else `[data-zone="pending"]`), else on the felt, else null. The V2
  midpoint of the plate and the card band put arena ghosts between the plate and the self strip.
- **Visible-first log.** `queryActionLogAnchor` is `queryFirstVisible([panel, collapsed])`: a log
  list that is mounted but zero-size (a collapsed panel, a depth-hidden log sheet) never wins.
- **Retired:**
  - `queryOpponentsCollapsedAnchor` and the collapsed tails of the seat and resource lookups;
  - `measureIncomingCollapsedCue` and its second targeting cue;
  - `OPPONENTS_COLLAPSED_ZONE`, `INCOMING_PENDING_ZONE` and `INCOMING_COLLAPSED_ZONE`;
  - `queryIncomingAnchor` now names the plate.

  They are superseded by §6.4 (landscape-compact replaces the V6 short-viewport collapse), §6.1
  (every region always renders), §6.9 (an always-rendered log endpoint) and §6.12 (FX on arena
  anchors). No arena element carried them, so no arena FX is lost. Under `?ui=classic` the old
  table loses only the fallbacks for chrome it collapses, until L75-10 deletes it.
- **Tests.** `play-flyout.test.ts` › 'aims flyouts at collapsed chrome …' (L53-07) retires, and its
  owner moves from L75-10 to L75-08. The L51-13 source read and the overlay source read become
  behavior tests.
```

**Browser check (controller, after review).** This is the batched **Check 1**, after this task. One dedicated subagent runs it on its own dev servers, at 390×844, 844×390 and 1440×900, and it covers L75-02, L75-03, L75-13 and L75-08.

The FX scenarios, each in all three modes:
- an incoming attack: the threat outline, the seat targeting cue and the "You are being attacked" banner;
- a mutual cancel: the resolution flash on the plate badge or the arrow label;
- Mirror, with the Lot 74 carry-forward re-check: a first Mirror must not auto-resolve to its preselected target after Minimize then Menu;
- an elimination and its reward: the elimination beat on the seat and the reward pulse on the dock;
- a Sentence: its countdown banners;
- an own Gambler wipe: its banner;
- a buy, a sell and a play: the ghosts on the plate;
- a Thief steal: seat-to-seat chips;
- on desktop, a point gain with the log panel collapsed and then expanded;
- under reduced motion: fades only.

The check also re-measures the `ARENA_TEST_BOXES` arena heights and runs the L75-02, L75-03 and L75-13 paragraphs. It is recorded under "Lot 75 Part 1 verified" in `frontend.md`, in one commit: `docs(arena): record browser check 1 (L75-08)`, whose body lists L75-02, L75-03, L75-13 and L75-08 (S7). That commit is not any task's own commit. A defect gets its own `fix(arena): … (L75-NN)` commit. Anything it cannot reach goes to `gate-items.md` for L75-12.

**Acceptance map:**
- An anchor-contract behavior test renders the arena in the three modes and resolves every endpoint the `fx/` measure helpers query (seat, seat resource, self resource, kit portrait, hand instance, plate, log, dock, pending id): `resolves every FX endpoint to a rendered arena element in $mode` (3 rows).
- The `play-flyout.test.ts` product assertions get arena behavior tests:
  - L51-09 seat/dock scoping: the kept selector test, plus the seat and self resource assertions in the anchor test.
  - L51-13 ghost sizing: 'sizes buy/sell card ghosts toward the felt center (L51-13)' (rewritten) and the anchor test's 48×72 plate ghost.
  - L51-14 asCard-only chrome: the kept logic test and the overlay behavior test.
- Browser scenarios (incoming attack, mutual cancel, Mirror, elimination + reward, Sentence, own Gambler wipe): browser check (Check 1).
- Review Focus 5 (mandatory): `measures a log chip from the toggle while the desktop panel is collapsed, and from its scrollport once expanded (Review Focus 5)`, plus `play-flyout.test.ts` › 'the log endpoint is the first visible …'.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Brief correction (R6, open-question ruling 4):** the collapsed fallbacks retire instead of becoming endpoints.
- **The Q63 seed** is already in L74-17: nothing moves.
- **`queryIncomingAnchor` stays exported** and now names the plate. `queryPendingFlashAnchor`'s last fallback keeps working unchanged.
- **The incoming-cue hook test is rewritten, not deleted** (K12). It keeps the seat-cue coverage, and the collapsed anchor stays in it to prove that it is ignored.
- **The 0.88 opacity hold** cannot be observed in happy-dom: L78 (row :256 text).
- **Known gap, not changed (K12):** in portrait and landscape the log *sheet*'s `action-log-panel` stays on its content `div`, which can be taller than the sheet body's scrollport, so a chip may aim off-screen on a long log. Gate item (Check 1, then `gate-items.md`).
- **New strings:** none.

```ts
// ui/layers.ts
export const Z_INDEX = { dragGhost: 40, sheet: 50, sheetBar: 80, hint: 84, coach: 86, banner: 90, toast: 95 } as const;

// screens/arena/use-arena-tutorial.ts: ArenaTutorialInput, ArenaCoachView, ArenaTutorial exactly as A.8.2, plus
export type ArenaTutorialHighlightId =
  | TutorialTourHighlight | 'draw' | 'shop' | TutorialCardActionSpotlight
  | 'shop-upgrade-point' | 'shop-absorber' | 'shop-absorber-buy' | 'opponent-portrait' | CardId;
export function useArenaTutorial(input: ArenaTutorialInput): ArenaTutorial;

// screens/arena/tutorial-coach-host.tsx: TutorialCoachHostProps exactly as A.8.3
export function TutorialCoachHost(props: TutorialCoachHostProps): ReactElement;

// ui/coach/tutorial-callout-geometry.ts (pure)
export interface CalloutRect { readonly left: number; readonly top: number; readonly width: number; readonly height: number }
export type CalloutArrowSide = 'top' | 'bottom';
export const CALLOUT_RING_GAP_PX = 4;
export const CALLOUT_ARROW_W_PX = 20;
export const CALLOUT_ARROW_H_PX = 12;
export interface CalloutPlacement { readonly ring: CalloutRect; readonly side: CalloutArrowSide; readonly arrow: CalloutRect }
/** §6.12: the arrow sits outside the ring, above a target whose centre is in the lower half of `viewport`, else below; centred on it and clamped into `viewport`. Box-relative px. */
export function calloutPlacement(target: CalloutRect, viewport: { readonly width: number; readonly height: number }): CalloutPlacement;

// ui/coach/tutorial-callout.tsx
export interface TutorialCalloutLayerProps { readonly active: boolean }
export function TutorialCalloutLayer(props: TutorialCalloutLayerProps): ReactElement | null;

// ui/kit/kit-portrait.tsx: KitPortraitProps gains
dataAttributes?: Readonly<Record<`data-${string}`, string>>;   // spread before the portrait's own anchors, which win

// Optional props, absent = today's behavior (A.8.6, A.8.7). L75-04 adds them all; Domain B pre-adds none (S6).
TopBarProps.onShowCoach?: () => void;  TopBarProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'timer' | 'flag' | null;
MenuSheetProps.onShowCoach?: () => void;
LogTickerProps.tutorialHighlight?: boolean;  LogPanelProps.tutorialHighlight?: boolean;
CentralPlateProps.tutorialHighlight?: boolean;
SelfStripProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'resources' | 'kit' | null;
HandFanProps.tutorial?: { readonly group: 'hand' | 'specials' | null; readonly instanceIds: readonly string[] };
DockProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'draw' | 'shop' | null;  DockProps.locked?: boolean;
ArenaSeatsProps.tutorial?: { readonly seats: boolean; readonly portraitIds: readonly string[] };
OpponentSeatProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'opponent' | 'opponent-portrait';   // private to the arena
HandDragInput.locked?: boolean;   // beside L75-03's mode?
export function canStartHandDrag(actions: CardActionsModel, targetingActive: boolean, locked = false): boolean;

// screens/arena/arena-screen.tsx (K4): the commit's allow, with the lock half of tutorial.allows
allow: (intent) => !reconnecting && tutorial.allows(intent);   // allows = () => !(active && locksTable) until L75-05

// screens/arena/test-view.ts (A.10)
export const TUTORIAL_HUMAN_INDICES: readonly number[];
export function makeTutorialArenaView(index: number, overrides?: Partial<PlayingStateView>): PlayingStateView;
```

**Brief corrections:**
- **Watch point (ruling 1).** The coach is `Z_INDEX.coach` 86, above every sheet (50+) and Minimize bar (80+) and under the banner, the toast and the `fx/` overlay, not "above `toast`" as in V6. The order is recorded in DESIGN.md and decisions.md.
- **Routing (R9).** `ui-switch.ts` and the `App.tsx` comment move here from the L75-05 brief.
- **"Use sends nothing during the tour" (open question 9).** Hand and special taps open no sheet during the tour and the Look gate (V2 parity), so Use is unreachable.
- **The brief's `view-model/tutorial-coach-copy.ts` file** needs no edit here: every helper the arena needs already exists.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-tutorial.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * The tutorial on the arena: technical spec v7 §6.12, §6.6.3, §6.10, §8.3, §14.3; Lot 75 plan
 * Appendix A.8 (L75-04); Review Focus 3 (the coach over a sheet) and Review Focus 1's coach half.
 */
import {
  TUTORIAL_LOOK_COACH, TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS, tutorialStepAt,
  type MirrorChoiceRequiredPayload, type PlayingStateView, type TutorialTourHighlight,
} from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, fireEvent, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { Z_INDEX } from '../../ui/layers';
import {
  ACTION_LOG_OPEN_LABEL, GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL, HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL, HOW_TO_PLAY_ARIA_LABEL,
  OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL, SKIP_TUTORIAL_ACTION_LABEL, SKIP_TUTORIAL_ARIA_LABEL, SKIP_TUTORIAL_CONFIRM_TITLE,
} from '../../view-model/table-copy';
import { makePending } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { TUTORIAL_IDLE_MS, TUTORIAL_IDLE_PLAY_TITLE } from '../../view-model/tutorial-coach-copy';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { HAND_GROUP_COPY } from './hand-fan-model';
import { SUB_CHOICE_COPY } from './sub-choice/sub-choice-model';
import {
  ARENA_INTENT_PROPS, ARENA_TEST_BOXES, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeTutorialArenaView,
  stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub,
} from './test-view';
import { MENU_LABEL } from './top-bar-model';

let stub: ArenaLayoutStub | null = null;
afterEach(() => { stub?.restore(); stub = null; });

type User = ReturnType<typeof renderUi>['user'];
function setup(index: number, props: Partial<ArenaScreenProps> = {}, view: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}) {
  const all = makeArenaProps({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(index, view), ...props });
  return { props: all, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...all} />, { reducedMotion: true }) };
}
function one(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (element === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return element;
}
const all = (selector: string): Element[] => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(selector));
const region = (name: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
const mode = (): string | null => one('main[data-arena="next"]').getAttribute('data-layout-mode');
const coach = (): HTMLElement | null => document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="tutorial-coach"]');
const coachTitle = (): string | null => coach()?.querySelector('h2')?.textContent ?? null;
const gotIt = (): HTMLElement => one('[data-zone="tutorial-coach"] [data-tutorial-ack="got-it"]');
const hideCoach = (): HTMLElement => within(one('[data-zone="tutorial-coach"]')).getByRole('button', { name: HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL });
const menuButton = (): HTMLElement => within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: MENU_LABEL });
const handCard = (id: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="hand"] [data-instance-id="${id}"]`);
const seatButton = (id: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="${id}"] > button`);
const dockButton = (anchor: 'draw' | 'shop'): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="dock"] [data-hint-anchor="${anchor}"]`);
const isDisabled = (button: HTMLElement): boolean => button.hasAttribute('disabled') || button.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true';
async function ack(user: User, times: number): Promise<void> {
  for (let step = 0; step < times; step += 1) await user.click(gotIt());
}
// PRIMARY and the lift: the commit-veto.test.tsx pattern.
const PRIMARY = { pointerId: 1, isPrimary: true, button: 0 } as const;
function lift(card: HTMLElement): void {
  fireEvent.pointerDown(card, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 100, clientY: 700 });
  fireEvent.pointerMove(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 103, clientY: 688 });
}
/** `data-tutorial-highlight` marks exactly `expected`, in document order, each with `id` (A.8.6). */
function expectMarked(id: string, expected: readonly Element[]): void {
  expect(expected.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  const marked = all('[data-tutorial-highlight]');
  expect(marked.map((element) => element.getAttribute('data-tutorial-highlight'))).toEqual(expected.map(() => id));
  expect(marked.every((element, at) => element === expected[at])).toBe(true);
}
const TOUR_TARGET: Readonly<Record<TutorialTourHighlight, () => readonly Element[]>> = {
  'your-zone': () => [region('self-strip'), region('hand'), region('dock')],
  hand: () => all('[data-arena-region="hand"] [data-zone="hand"]'),
  specials: () => all('[data-arena-region="hand"] [data-zone="specials"]'),
  resources: () => all('[data-arena-region="self-strip"] [data-zone="resources"]'),
  incoming: () => all('[data-arena-region="arena"] [data-zone="pending"]'),
  shop: () => [dockButton('shop')],
  opponent: () => all('[data-zone="opponent-seat"]'),
  'action-log': () => all('[data-arena-region="ticker"] [data-zone="log-collapsed"]'),
  timer: () => all('[data-arena-region="top-bar"] [data-top-bar="turn"]'),
  kit: () => all('[data-arena-region="self-strip"] [data-zone="kit-portrait"]'),
  flag: () => [within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: SKIP_TUTORIAL_ARIA_LABEL })],
};
const MODES = [
  { mode: 'portrait', box: null },
  { mode: 'landscape-compact', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscape },
  { mode: 'desktop', box: ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop },
] as const;
const TOUR_ROWS = MODES.flatMap(({ mode: name, box }) =>
  TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS.map((step, n) => ({ name, box, n, id: step.highlight, title: step.coach.title })));
const INCOMING = makePending({ id: 'tut-e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'basic-attack' });
const MIRROR: MirrorChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: ['tut-e1'], deadlineMs: 40_000 };
const spiedOn = (): Partial<PlayingStateView> => ({
  players: makeTutorialArenaView(0).players.map((player) => (player.id === 'p1' ? { ...player, spyingOnYou: true } : player)),
});

describe('the board tour (§6.12, §14.3, Q60)', () => {
  it.each(TOUR_ROWS)('$name, step $n: marks exactly the $id element, and Got it sends nothing', async ({ name, box, n, id, title }) => {
    if (box !== null) stub = stubArenaLayout(box.viewport, box.arena);
    const { user, props } = setup(0);
    expect(mode()).toBe(name);
    await ack(user, n);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(title);
    expectMarked(id, TOUR_TARGET[id]());
    await ack(user, 1);
    expect(document.querySelector(`[data-tutorial-highlight="${id}"]`)).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('ends after the eleventh Got it on the Draw lesson, with no Got it left', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(0);
    await ack(user, TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS.length);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(tutorialStepAt(0)?.coach?.title);
    expect(coach()?.querySelector('[data-tutorial-ack]')).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('marks an empty group too: the specials caption with no special card (Q60)', async () => {
    const { user } = setup(0, {}, { self: { ...makeTutorialArenaView(0).self, specialCards: [] } });
    await ack(user, 2);
    expectMarked('specials', TOUR_TARGET.specials());
    expect(one('[data-zone="specials"]').textContent).toContain(HAND_GROUP_COPY.specials.empty);
  });
});

describe('the tour and the Look gate lock the table (§6.6.3, open questions 8 and 9)', () => {
  it('during the tour Draw, a hand tap, a drag, Shop and Unspy send or open nothing; a seat tap still opens its sheet', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(0, {}, spiedOn());
    const unspy = one('[data-arena-region="dock"] [data-unspy]');
    expect([isDisabled(dockButton('draw')), isDisabled(dockButton('shop')), isDisabled(unspy)]).toEqual([false, false, false]);
    await user.click(dockButton('draw'));
    await user.click(handCard('tax-1'));
    lift(handCard('basic-attack-1'));
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="hand-drag-ghost"]')).toBeNull();
    fireEvent.pointerUp(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 103, clientY: 688 });
    await user.click(dockButton('shop'));
    await user.click(unspy);
    expect([all('[data-arena-sheet^="card:"]').length, all('[data-arena-sheet="shop"]').length, all('[data-unspy-picker]').length]).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS[0]?.coach.title);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(seatButton('p1'));
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet="seat:p1"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('at index 9 blocks every send until the revealed seat sheet opens, then never again in that mount', async () => {
    const { user, props, rerender } = setup(9);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(TUTORIAL_LOOK_COACH.title);
    expect(coach()?.textContent).toContain(TUTORIAL_LOOK_COACH.body);
    expectMarked('opponent-portrait', all('[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="p1"] [data-zone="opponent-portrait"]'));
    await user.click(dockButton('draw'));
    await user.click(handCard('shield-1'));
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet^="card:"]')).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(seatButton('p1'));
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet="seat:p1"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(tutorialStepAt(9)?.coach?.title);
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-tutorial-highlight="opponent-portrait"]')).toBeNull();
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} view={makeTutorialArenaView(9)} />);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(tutorialStepAt(9)?.coach?.title);
    await user.click(handCard('shield-1'));
    await user.click(one('[data-arena-sheet="card:shield-1"] [data-card-action="sell"]'));
    expect(vi.mocked(props.onSellCard).mock.calls).toEqual([['shield-1']]);
  });
});

describe('the coach (§6.12, Q45)', () => {
  it('hides with ×, reopens from Menu → Show coach, and reopens by itself on new copy', async () => {
    const { user, props, rerender } = setup(1);
    await user.click(menuButton());
    expect(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: MENU_LABEL })).queryByRole('button', { name: OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL })).toBeNull();
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await user.click(hideCoach());
    expect(coach()).toBeNull();
    await user.click(menuButton());
    const menu = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: MENU_LABEL });
    const entries = within(menu).getAllByRole('button');
    expect(entries.slice(0, 3).map((entry) => entry.textContent)).toEqual([HOW_TO_PLAY_ARIA_LABEL, OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL, ACTION_LOG_OPEN_LABEL]);
    expect(entries.slice(0, 2).map((entry) => entry.querySelector('svg')?.getAttribute('data-icon'))).toEqual(['help', 'coach']);
    await user.click(within(menu).getByRole('button', { name: OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL }));
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(tutorialStepAt(1)?.coach?.title);
    await user.click(hideCoach());
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} view={makeTutorialArenaView(3)} />);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(tutorialStepAt(3)?.coach?.title);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it("retitles the coach 'Play' after 20 s on the POV's turn, and reopens it", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(1) });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, { advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime, reducedMotion: true });
    await user.click(hideCoach());
    act(() => { vi.advanceTimersByTime(TUTORIAL_IDLE_MS - 1); });
    expect(coach()).toBeNull();
    act(() => { vi.advanceTimersByTime(1); });
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(TUTORIAL_IDLE_PLAY_TITLE);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'during the tour', index: 0, title: TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS[0]?.coach.title },
    { label: 'on a bot turn, which keeps the last lesson', index: 2, title: tutorialStepAt(1)?.coach?.title },
  ])('never retitles $label', ({ index, title }) => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...makeArenaProps({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(index) })} />, { advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime, reducedMotion: true });
    act(() => { vi.advanceTimersByTime(TUTORIAL_IDLE_MS * 2); });
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(title);
  });

  it('× from the keyboard hands the focus to Menu, where Show coach lives', async () => {
    const { user } = setup(1);
    hideCoach().focus();
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    expect([coach(), document.activeElement === menuButton()]).toEqual([null, true]);
  });

  it("Got it from the keyboard keeps the focus on the next step's Got it", async () => {
    const { user } = setup(0);
    gotIt().focus();
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    expect([coachTitle(), document.activeElement === gotIt()]).toEqual([TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS[1]?.coach.title, true]);
  });
});

describe('the coach over a sheet (Review Focus 3)', () => {
  it.each<{ label: string; subChoice: MirrorChoiceRequiredPayload | null; view: Partial<PlayingStateView>; open: (user: User) => Promise<HTMLElement> }>([
    { label: 'an open read-only card sheet', subChoice: null, view: {},
      open: async (user: User) => { await user.click(handCard('tax-1')); return one('[data-arena-sheet="card:tax-1"]'); } },
    { label: 'an expanded blocking sheet', subChoice: MIRROR, view: { pendingEffects: [INCOMING] },
      open: () => Promise.resolve(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: SUB_CHOICE_COPY.mirror.title })) },
  ])('stays visible and pressable over $label: × hides it, the sheet stays open and keeps the focus', async ({ subChoice, view, open }) => {
    const { user, props } = setup(1, { subChoice }, view);
    const dialog = await open(user);
    const panel = coach();
    if (panel === null) throw new Error('no coach');
    const host = panel.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-tutorial-coach-host]');
    const overlay = dialog.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="sheet-overlay"]');
    expect(host?.style.zIndex).toBe(String(Z_INDEX.coach));
    expect(Number(host?.style.zIndex)).toBeGreaterThan(Number(overlay?.style.zIndex));
    expect([panel.closest('[inert]'), dialog.contains(panel), dialog.contains(document.activeElement)]).toEqual([null, false, true]);
    await user.click(within(panel).getByRole('button', { name: HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL }));
    expect(coach()).toBeNull();
    expect([dialog.isConnected, dialog.closest('[inert]'), dialog.contains(document.activeElement)]).toEqual([true, null, true]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('Got it advances the tour with the seat sheet open, which keeps the focus', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(0);
    await user.click(seatButton('p1'));
    const sheet = one('[data-arena-sheet="seat:p1"]');
    await user.click(gotIt());
    expect([coachTitle(), sheet.isConnected, sheet.contains(document.activeElement)]).toEqual([TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS[1]?.coach.title, true, true]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});

describe('the coach across a mode change (Review Focus 1, coach half)', () => {
  it('keeps its message key and its hidden state from portrait to desktop to landscape-compact', async () => {
    const phone = ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390;

    await user.click(hideCoach());

    expect([mode(), coach()]).toEqual(['landscape-compact', null]);
    await user.click(menuButton());
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: MENU_LABEL })).getByRole('button', { name: OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL }));
    expect([coachTitle(), coach()?.getAttribute('data-tutorial-index')]).toEqual([TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS[2]?.coach.title, '0']);
    expectMarked('specials', TOUR_TARGET.specials());
  });
});

describe('tutorial table states (§6.10, §6.12, L51-07, Lot 45)', () => {
  it('rings the incoming Basic at index 3 in attack tone, with no tutorial mark on it', () => {
    setup(3);
    const badge = one('[data-zone="pending"] [data-pending-id="tut-3"]');

    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: SKIP_TUTORIAL_CONFIRM_TITLE })).getByRole('button', { name: SKIP_TUTORIAL_ACTION_LABEL }));

    }

  it('shows no coach, no mark and no lock on the finished tutorial board', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(0, { readOnly: true });
    expect([coach(), all('[data-tutorial-highlight]').length]).toEqual([null, 0]);
    await user.click(handCard('tax-1'));
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet="card:tax-1"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
```

That is 51 tests (33 tour rows + 18). Create `apps/client/src/ui/coach/tutorial-callout-geometry.test.ts`:

```ts
/** Tutorial callout geometry: technical spec v7 §6.12 (the arrow sits outside the highlight); L75-04. */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { CALLOUT_ARROW_H_PX, CALLOUT_ARROW_W_PX, CALLOUT_RING_GAP_PX, calloutPlacement } from './tutorial-callout-geometry';

const PHONE = { width: 390, height: 844 };

describe('calloutPlacement (spec v7 §6.12)', () => {
  it('rings the target with a gap and points down at it from above when it sits in the lower half', () => {
    const placed = calloutPlacement({ left: 100, top: 700, width: 80, height: 40 }, PHONE);
    const gap = CALLOUT_RING_GAP_PX;
    expect(placed.ring).toEqual({ left: 100 - gap, top: 700 - gap, width: 80 + 2 * gap, height: 40 + 2 * gap });
    expect(placed.side).toBe('top');
    expect(placed.arrow).toEqual({ left: 140 - CALLOUT_ARROW_W_PX / 2, top: placed.ring.top - CALLOUT_ARROW_H_PX, width: CALLOUT_ARROW_W_PX, height: CALLOUT_ARROW_H_PX });
  });
  it('points up at a target in the upper half from under its ring', () => {
    const placed = calloutPlacement({ left: 10, top: 8, width: 120, height: 60 }, PHONE);
    expect([placed.side, placed.arrow.top]).toEqual(['bottom', placed.ring.top + placed.ring.height]);
  });
  it('keeps the arrow inside the visible box at both edges', () => {
    expect(calloutPlacement({ left: 0, top: 600, width: 8, height: 40 }, PHONE).arrow.left).toBe(0);
    expect(calloutPlacement({ left: 384, top: 600, width: 6, height: 40 }, PHONE).arrow.left).toBe(PHONE.width - CALLOUT_ARROW_W_PX);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/client/src/ui/coach/tutorial-callout.render.test.tsx` (P4: `screens/table/tutorial-callout.test.ts` keeps the base name `tutorial-callout`):

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/** The tutorial callout layer: technical spec v7 §6.12, §8.3; Lot 75 plan Appendix A.8.6 (L75-04). */
import { waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { Z_INDEX } from '../layers';
import { Sheet } from '../primitives/sheet';
import { TutorialCalloutLayer } from './tutorial-callout';

const TARGET = new DOMRect(100, 600, 80, 120);
afterEach(() => { vi.restoreAllMocks(); });
/** Every marked element reports TARGET, unless `zero` names its id; anything else reports 0×0. */
function stubRects(zero: readonly string[] = []): void {
  vi.spyOn(Element.prototype, 'getBoundingClientRect').mockImplementation(function rect(this: Element) {
    const id = this.getAttribute('data-tutorial-highlight');
    return id === null || zero.includes(id) ? new DOMRect() : TARGET;
  });
}
const drawn = (): (string | null)[] =>
  Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-tutorial-callout]')).map((callout) => callout.getAttribute('data-tutorial-callout'));
const draw = (active = true): ReactElement => (
  <><button type="button" data-tutorial-highlight="draw">Draw</button><TutorialCalloutLayer active={active} /></>
);

describe('TutorialCalloutLayer (spec v7 §6.12, §8.3)', () => {
  it('rings each live, sized target once on the hint layer, with its arrow outside', () => {
    stubRects();
    renderUi(draw());
    expect(drawn()).toEqual(['draw']);
    const callout = document.querySelector('[data-tutorial-callout="draw"]');
    const root = callout?.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="tutorial-callouts"]');
    expect([callout?.getAttribute('data-callout-arrow'), callout?.hasAttribute('data-tutorial-highlight')]).toEqual(['top', false]);
    expect([root?.parentElement === document.body, root?.style.zIndex, root?.style.pointerEvents, root?.getAttribute('aria-hidden')])
      .toEqual([true, String(Z_INDEX.hint), 'none', 'true']);
  });
  it('draws nothing while inactive', () => {
    stubRects();
    renderUi(draw(false));
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="tutorial-callouts"]')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('skips a zero-size target and one inside an inert subtree', () => {
    stubRects(['hand']);
    renderUi(<><div data-tutorial-highlight="hand" /><div inert><button type="button" data-tutorial-highlight="shop">Shop</button></div>{draw()}</>);
    expect(drawn()).toEqual(['draw']);
  });
  it("draws only the live sheet's own targets while a sheet is live", async () => {
    stubRects();
    renderUi(<>{draw()}<Sheet open mode="read-only" placement="bottom" title="Tax" onClose={vi.fn()}><button type="button" data-tutorial-highlight="use">Use</button></Sheet></>);
    await waitFor(() => { expect(drawn()).toEqual(['use']); });
  });
  it('pulses under full motion and stays static under reduced motion (§8.3)', () => {
    stubRects();
    const { unmount } = renderUi(draw());
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-tutorial-callout]')?.getAttribute('data-motion')).toBe('full');
    unmount();
    renderUi(draw(), { reducedMotion: true });
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-tutorial-callout]')?.getAttribute('data-motion')).toBe('reduced');
  });
});
```

```tsx
  it('marks Draw or Shop for the tutorial, and while locked Shop and Unspy open nothing (L75-04, A.8.6, A.8.7)', async () => {

      <Dock model={selfStrip(view, { subChoice: null, readOnly: false }).dock} isMyTurn turnSequence={1} view={view}

  });
```
