Expected:
  - `arena-tutorial.test.tsx`, `test-view.test.ts` and `App.arena.test.tsx` fail at run time with a `TypeError`, not at resolve: `./test-view` exists, so the new named export `makeTutorialArenaView` imports as `undefined`;
  - both `ui/coach` files fail to resolve their module (`tutorial-callout` and `tutorial-callout-geometry` are new);
  - the hand-fan and dock cases find no `data-tutorial-highlight`, and the dock's locked Shop opens;
  - `canStartHandDrag(…, true)` returns true;
  - the sheet-stack literal and the fifth bar (84) fail;
  - the ui-switch case gets `'classic'` for a tutorial room.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - **`ui/layers.ts`.** The new `Z_INDEX`. The doc comment's order becomes read-only < expanded blocking < Minimize bars < hint < coach < banner < toast, and it names the `fx/` overlay (`z-[110]`) on top.
  - **`ui/primitives/sheet-stack.ts`.** In `sheetZIndex`, bars clamp at `Math.min(Z_INDEX.sheetBar + barIndex, Z_INDEX.hint - 1)`; update its doc comment.
  - **`ui/kit/kit-portrait.tsx`.** `dataAttributes?` spread on the root (button or span) **before** `{...anchors}`, so `data-zone="kit-portrait"` and `data-kit-*` win.
  - **`ui/coach/tutorial-callout-geometry.ts`.** `calloutPlacement` exactly as its doc says: the ring is the target inflated by `CALLOUT_RING_GAP_PX`; `side` is `'top'` when `target.top + target.height / 2 > viewport.height / 2`; the arrow `left` is the target centre minus `CALLOUT_ARROW_W_PX / 2`, clamped to `[0, max(0, viewport.width − W)]`; its `top` is `ring.top − H` (top) or `ring.top + ring.height` (bottom), clamped to `[0, max(0, viewport.height − H)]`.
  - **`ui/coach/tutorial-callout.tsx`, `TutorialCalloutLayer`.**
    - Inputs: `useVisualViewportBox()`, `useSheetStackStore()`, `usePrefersReducedMotion()`. State: `targets: readonly { id: string; rect: CalloutRect }[]`.
    - `measure` (a `useEffectEvent`): `live = liveSheetId(store.getSnapshot())`. It keeps each `[data-tutorial-highlight]` element that has no `[inert]` ancestor, whose `closest('[data-zone="sheet-overlay"]')?.getAttribute('data-sheet-id') ?? null` equals `live`, and whose client rect is non-zero. Rects are box-relative (minus `box.offsetLeft` / `offsetTop`). It sets state only when the list changed (compare ids and rects), as `use-arena-layout-box.ts` does.
    - A `useLayoutEffect` on `[active]`: when active, measure once, then on every `requestAnimationFrame` until cleanup (sheets and the fan move under motion, which no observer reports).
    - Render: `null` unless `active && targets.length > 0`. Otherwise `createPortal` to `document.body`: `<div data-zone="tutorial-callouts" aria-hidden="true" style={{ position: 'fixed', top/left/width/height of the box, zIndex: Z_INDEX.hint, pointerEvents: 'none' }}>`.
    - Per target, `calloutPlacement(rect, box)` gives `<div data-tutorial-callout={id} data-callout-arrow={side} data-motion={motionAttribute(reduced)}>`:
      - an absolute ring at `ring`: `rounded-v7-chrome-sm ring-3 ring-v7-threat-other`, plus `animate-pulse` only when not reduced;
      - an inline 20×12 SVG triangle at `arrow`, `fill={V7_COLORS['threat-other']}` with a 1.5 px `V7_COLORS.ink` stroke, pointing down for `'top'` and up for `'bottom'`.
      - Neither carries `data-tutorial-highlight`.
  - **`screens/arena/use-arena-tutorial.ts`, `useArenaTutorial`** (state in the hook, R7):
    - `useState`: `tourStep` 0, `portraitInspected` false, `idleIndex` null, `dismissedKey` null.
    - `active = view.playKind === 'tutorial' && !readOnly && view.tutorialIndex !== null`; `index = active ? view.tutorialIndex : null`; `tourActive = isTutorialTourActive(index, tourStep)`; `lookPending = isTutorialLookPending(index, portraitInspected)`; `locksTable = tourActive || lookPending`.
    - `tourHighlight = tourActive ? (tutorialTourStepAt(tourStep)?.highlight ?? null) : null`; `scriptHighlight = active && !locksTable && index !== null ? tutorialHighlightAt(index) : null`.
    - Coach: `presentation = index === null ? undefined : resolveTutorialPresentationCoach(index, tourStep, portraitInspected)`.
      - `idle = !locksTable && idleIndex === index`.
      - `title = locksTable ? presentation.copy.title : tutorialCoachTitle(presentation.copy, idle)`; `body = presentation.copy.body`.
      - `messageKey = \`${tourActive ? \`tour:${tourStep}\` : lookPending ? 'look' : 'script'}|${tutorialCoachMessageKey(index, title, body)}\``.
      - `coach = { index, title, body, messageKey, open: isTutorialCoachOpen(messageKey, dismissedKey), showAck: presentation.showAck }`, or null when inactive or without copy.
    - The idle timer: a `useEffect` that returns early unless `active && isMyTurn && !selfEliminated && !locksTable`. Otherwise `window.setTimeout(() => { setIdleIndex(index); }, TUTORIAL_IDLE_MS)`, cleared on cleanup. Deps `[active, index, isMyTurn, selfEliminated, tourStep, portraitInspected]` (V2 `table.tsx:539-566`).
    - Callbacks:
      - `onAck`: `setTourStep((step) => step + 1)`, only while `coach?.showAck`.
      - `onHideCoach`: `setDismissedKey(coach.messageKey)`. `onShowCoach`: `setDismissedKey(null)`.
      - `onSeatOpened(playerId)`: when `seatList(view).seats.find(...)?.kitHidden === false`, `setPortraitInspected(true)`. It is never reset, so the gate is latched per mount.
      - `allows`: `() => !(active && locksTable)`. The lock half only (K4): L75-05 adds the script half and `illegalIndex`, and the parameter-less function is assignable to `(intent: ArenaIntent) => boolean`, so `ArenaTutorial`'s shape does not change between the two tasks.
    - Export `ArenaTutorialHighlightId` (Produces). `view-model/` stays untouched.
  - **`screens/arena/tutorial-coach-host.tsx`, `TutorialCoachHost`.**
    - `createPortal` to `document.body`: `<div data-tutorial-coach-host="" style={{ position: 'fixed', box top/left/width/height (useVisualViewportBox), zIndex: Z_INDEX.coach, pointerEvents: 'none' }}>`.
    - The slot: `<div ref={slotRef} data-tutorial-coach-slot="" className="pointer-events-auto absolute w-[min(22rem,calc(100%-1rem))]" style={{ top, left }} onMouseDown={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); }}>`. A press never moves focus out of an open sheet (Review Focus 3).
    - Placement: `top` / `left` are the `[data-arena-region="arena"]` rect minus the box offset, plus a private `COACH_INSET_PX = 8`. Measure it in a `useLayoutEffect`, with a `ResizeObserver` on that element, the `use-arena-layout-box.ts` pattern; deps are the box. Before a measure, use 8 / 8.
    - Inside: `<CoachPanel key={coach.messageKey} title={coach.title} body={coach.body} zone="tutorial-coach" hideAriaLabel={HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL} onHide={hide} extraAttrs={{ 'data-tutorial-index': String(coach.index), 'data-tutorial-coach-open': 'true' }} {...(coach.showAck ? { footer: <Button variant="primary" fullWidth data-tutorial-ack="got-it" onClick={ack}>{GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL}</Button> } : {})} />`.
    - `hide`: when focus is inside the slot, first focus the top-bar Menu button (`[data-arena-region="top-bar"] button[aria-label="${MENU_LABEL}"]`), then `onHide()`.
    - `ack`: `const refocus = useRef(false)`. Set `refocus.current` to whether focus is inside the slot (the old panel's Got it, pressed from the keyboard), then call `onAck()`. A `useLayoutEffect` on `[coach.messageKey]` runs its focus move **only when `refocus.current` is true**, and it clears the ref first, whether or not it then moves focus: it focuses the new panel's `[data-tutorial-ack="got-it"]`, else its Hide control. Only `ack` arms the ref, and only with focus inside the slot, which is outside every sheet, so the move never leaves a sheet or dialog. A copy change that `ack` did not cause (the first paint, a follow-coach copy after a vetoed send in L75-05, the idle 'Play' title) arms nothing, so it never steals focus from an open sheet (the non-modal rule, Review Focus 3). A Got it pressed with the pointer while focus is elsewhere (an open sheet, or the page) leaves the ref false, since the slot's `onMouseDown` keeps focus where it was.
  - **Components** (each prop optional; absent renders exactly as today):
    - `top-bar.tsx`: `tutorialHighlight` spreads `data-tutorial-highlight="timer"` on `[data-top-bar="turn"]` and `"flag"` on the flag `IconButton`. `onShowCoach` is passed through to `MenuSheet`.
    - `menu-sheet.tsx`: when `onShowCoach` is given, a `<Button variant="quiet" icon="coach" fullWidth onClick={() => { onClose(); onShowCoach(); }}>{OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL}</Button>` sits between How to play and Action log. Update the header body-order line.
    - `log-ticker.tsx`, `log-panel.tsx`: `tutorialHighlight` puts `data-tutorial-highlight="action-log"` on the `data-zone="log-collapsed"` button (the ticker; the panel toggle, expanded or collapsed).
    - `central-plate.tsx`: `tutorialHighlight` puts `"incoming"` on `<section data-zone="pending">`.
    - `self-strip.tsx`: `"resources"` on `[data-zone="resources"]`, and `"kit"` through `KitPortrait dataAttributes`, in the full strip and in L75-03's `compact` strip.
    - `hand-fan.tsx`: `HandGroup` gets `highlighted` (`tutorial?.group === zone`) and puts `data-tutorial-highlight={zone}` on its `data-zone` div. Each `HandCard` whose instance id is in `tutorial.instanceIds` passes `dataAttributes={{ 'data-tutorial-highlight': instance.cardId }}` to `Card`.
    - `dock.tsx`: `tutorialHighlight` puts the attribute on the Draw button (`'draw'`) or the Shop button (`'shop'`). With `locked`, the Shop and Unspy `onClick` return early; Draw and Activate duplication still commit, and the commit vetoes them.
    - `arena-seats.tsx` → `opponent-seat.tsx`: per seat, `tutorial.seats ? 'opponent' : tutorial.portraitIds.includes(id) ? 'opponent-portrait' : undefined`. `'opponent'` goes on the seat root, `'opponent-portrait'` on its `[data-zone="opponent-portrait"]` div.
    - `hand-drag.ts`: `canStartHandDrag(actions, targetingActive, locked = false)` returns `actions.canDrag && !targetingActive && !locked`; fix its doc comment. `use-hand-drag.ts`: `HandDragInput.locked?`, passed as `input.locked ?? false` at the drag-start call.
  - **`screens/arena/arena-screen.tsx`** (`ArenaScreenInner`):
    - Hook order (S3, contract A.8.2). Insert `const tutorial = useArenaTutorial({ view, readOnly, isMyTurn: view.currentTurnPlayerId === view.you, selfEliminated: povEliminated })` directly below `const strip = selfStrip(view, ctx);`. That is after `useArenaLayoutBox` and L75-02 / L75-13's `logPanelExpanded` and `band` state, and before `usePovElimination`. The `pov` / `povEliminated` lines already sit above it.
    - Then move the whole `const commit = createArenaCommit(props, { … });` statement, with its two comment lines, from above the `seenReject` state down to directly below the tutorial call. It is not a hook, and nothing between its old and new places uses `commit`, so no hook moves.
    - Set `allow: (intent) => !reconnecting && tutorial.allows(intent)`, with a comment that the tutorial gate composes with the reconnect lock and never replaces it (R8, A.8.4, K4). Leave `onSent` as it is: L75-06 adds the hint cause there.
    - The three openers, in the order contract A.9.2 writes once (S2). Each tutorial check comes before the open, and L75-06 adds `hints.note(…)` between them and the open:
      - `openCard`: `if (tutorial.locksTable && (source.kind === 'hand' || source.kind === 'special')) return;`, then `openSheet(...)`.
      - `onOpenShop`: after the tap guard, `if (tutorial.locksTable) return;`, then `openSheet(...)`.
      - `onOpenSeat`: after the tap guard, `tutorial.onSeatOpened(playerId)`, then `openSheet(...)`.
    - Pass `locked: tutorial.locksTable` to `useHandDrag` and to `Dock`.
    - Marks from `const tour = tutorial.tourHighlight`:
      - `{ 'data-tutorial-highlight': 'your-zone' }` spread on the self-strip, hand and dock region roots while `tour === 'your-zone'`;
      - `TopBar tutorialHighlight={tour === 'timer' || tour === 'flag' ? tour : null}`;
      - `LogTicker` / `LogPanel tutorialHighlight={tour === 'action-log'}`;
      - `CentralPlate tutorialHighlight={tour === 'incoming'}`;
      - `SelfStrip tutorialHighlight={tour === 'resources' || tour === 'kit' ? tour : null}`;
      - `HandFan tutorial={{ group: tour === 'hand' || tour === 'specials' ? tour : null, instanceIds: [] }}`;
      - `Dock tutorialHighlight={tour === 'shop' ? 'shop' : null}`;
      - `ArenaSeats tutorial={{ seats: tour === 'opponent', portraitIds: tutorial.lookPending ? seatsModel.seats.filter((seat) => seat.tier === 'spy-plus').map((seat) => seat.playerId) : [] }}`, passed only while `tutorial.active`.
    - `TopBar`: `{...(tutorial.coach !== null && !tutorial.coach.open ? { onShowCoach: tutorial.onShowCoach } : {})}`.
    - Mount, as siblings after `TableBannerHost`: `{tutorial.coach?.open === true ? <TutorialCoachHost coach={tutorial.coach} onHide={tutorial.onHideCoach} onAck={tutorial.onAck} /> : null}` and `<TutorialCalloutLayer active={tutorial.active} />`. Their tree position never depends on the mode, so a rotation remounts neither (A.3).
    - Header: replace "the default and tutorial rooms keep `TableScreen`" (line 5) with "the default keeps `TableScreen`; tutorial rooms follow the switch since L75-04". Add an L75-04 bullet: §6.12; the hook; the hosts on `Z_INDEX.coach` / `.hint`; the locks; the Look exit.
  - **`screens/arena/test-view.ts`.**
    - `TUTORIAL_HUMAN_INDICES = TUTORIAL_STEPS.filter((step) => step.actor === 'human').map((step) => step.index)`.
    - `makeTutorialArenaView(index, overrides = {})` throws a `RangeError` when `tutorialStepAt(index)` is undefined. Otherwise it returns `makeArenaView({ seatCount: 2, overrides: { playKind: 'tutorial', tutorialIndex: index, currentTurnPlayerId: human ? 'me' : 'p1', turnSequence: index + 1, players, self, pendingEffects, ...overrides } })`. The fixture keeps the setup's points at every index, enough for every scripted play and buy; it replays no server state:

      | Index | Hand (instance ids from `makeCard`) | Specials | Lives | Upgrade points | Pending on `me` from `p1` (`id: tut-<index>`, `queuedAt: index − 1`) |
      |---|---|---|---|---|---|
      | 0–1 | tax-1, spy-1, basic-attack-1, shield-1, shield-2 (`shield-2` via `instanceId`) | super-regeneration-1 | `TUTORIAL_HUMAN_LIVES` | `TUTORIAL_HUMAN_UPGRADE_POINTS` | — |
      | 2–5 | same | same | 1 | 1 | 3: basic-attack |
      | 6–9 | spy-1 upgraded | same | 1 | 0 | — |
      | 10–15 | shield-1 sold | same | 1 | 0 (14–15: 1) | 11: spy |

      | 20–23 | same | none | 4 | 1 | — |

```
## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] The tutorial on the arena: layers, callouts, coach slot and locks (L75-04)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.12, §6.6.3, §6.1, §8.3, §14.3; the Lot 75 plan, Appendix A.8; controller
rulings R7–R9 and open questions 1, 8 and 9 (2026-11-03).

- **Layers.** `Z_INDEX` gains `hint` 84 and `coach` 86: above every sheet (50+) and Minimize bar (80+),
  under the banner (90), the toast (95) and the `fx/` overlay (110). Bars clamp at `hint - 1`. V6 put
  the coach at 110 and hints at 105 (the backlog L75-04 watch point); banners and toasts keep painting
  above the coach (ruling 1).
- **The coach** is the `ui/coach` `CoachPanel` in a `document.body` portal on `Z_INDEX.coach`, outside
  every sheet, so no sheet makes it inert and a press on it never reaches a backdrop. Its slot is the
  arena region's top-left, 8 px in, `min(22rem, 100% - 1rem)` wide, in every mode. Its mousedown is
  prevented, so × and Got it keep the focus inside an open sheet; from the keyboard, × hands the focus to
  Menu and Got it to the next step's Got it.
- **Callouts** are one measured layer (`ui/coach/tutorial-callout.tsx`) that rings each
  `[data-tutorial-highlight]` element, with an arrow outside the ring. It portals on `Z_INDEX.hint`,
  which a tutorial room never uses for hints, and draws only the live layer: the live sheet's own targets
  while a sheet is live, else the table's. The hand `ScrollArea`, the `overflow-hidden` arena and a
  sheet body therefore never clip it; V2 needed `overflow: visible` on the card band. The orange is
  `--threat-other`, with no new token. Real incoming threats keep the plate badges' `data-tone` rings.
- **Locks** (§6.6.3; open questions 8 and 9). While the tour or the Look gate is up:
  - the commit's `allow` (`!reconnecting && tutorial.allows(intent)`) vetoes every send silently, a
    sub-choice answer included;
  - no drag starts, Shop and Unspy do not open, and hand and special taps open nothing;
  - seat taps stay live. Opening the seat sheet of a seat whose kit is visible clears the Look gate, once
    per mount.
  L75-05 adds the script half of `tutorial.allows`.
- **Routing (R9).** `resolveTableUi` returns the preference for every room. The default stays the
  classic table until L75-09.

---
```

Say that bars clamp below `hint`.

- **Tutorial host (L75-04):** `screens/arena/tutorial-coach-host.tsx` portals the default panel to
  `document.body` in a `pointer-events: none` box on the visible viewport, on `Z_INDEX.coach`, outside
  every sheet. The slot is the arena region's top-left, 8px in, `min(22rem, 100% - 1rem)` wide, in every
  mode. Its mousedown is prevented, so a press never moves focus out of an open sheet; from the keyboard,
  × moves focus to the top bar's Menu (Show coach) and Got it to the next step's Got it. Got it is a
  full-width primary Button (`data-tutorial-ack="got-it"`). The panel reopens from Menu → Show coach
  (`icon="coach"`, distinct from How to play's `help`, Q45); there is no floating reopen control.
- **Tutorial callouts (L75-04):** `ui/coach/tutorial-callout.tsx` `<TutorialCalloutLayer active />` rings
  every live `[data-tutorial-highlight]` element. `tutorial-callout-geometry.ts` places a 3px
  `--threat-other` ring 4px outside the element and a 20×12 arrow outside the ring: above it when the
  element's centre is in the lower half of the visible box, else below, clamped into the box. The layer
  portals on `Z_INDEX.hint` (a tutorial room never shows a first-game hint), `pointer-events: none` and
  `aria-hidden`, and re-measures every animation frame while active. It draws the live sheet's own
  targets while a sheet is live, else the table's, and skips a zero-size or `inert` target, so no
  scroller, `overflow-hidden` region or sheet body clips it. Full motion pulses the ring's opacity;
  reduced motion keeps it static (`data-motion`). Real incoming threats keep the plate badges' `data-tone`
  rings and get no callout.

### §6.12 tutorial highlight map (L75-04)

Every `data-tutorial-highlight` id and the arena element that carries it (spec v7 §6.12, §14.3; Lot 75
plan Appendix A.8.6). The attribute is present only while its highlight is live.

| Id | Arena element |
|---|---|
| `your-zone` | the `self-strip`, `hand` and `dock` region roots |
| `hand` / `specials` | the hand fan's `[data-zone="hand"]` / `[data-zone="specials"]` groups, empty or not |
| `resources` | the self strip's `[data-zone="resources"]` row |
| `incoming` | the plate, `[data-zone="pending"]` |
| `shop` | the dock's Shop button (the tour; the script's `shop-*` steps until the Shop opens) |
| `opponent` | every `[data-zone="opponent-seat"]` root |
| `action-log` | the `[data-zone="log-collapsed"]` button: the log ticker (portrait, landscape-compact), the log panel toggle (desktop) |
| `timer` | the top bar's `[data-top-bar="turn"]` card (banner and clock) |
| `kit` | the self strip's kit portrait, `[data-zone="kit-portrait"]` |
| `flag` | the top bar's flag (Skip tutorial) |
| `opponent-portrait` (Look) | the Spy+-revealed seat's `[data-zone="opponent-portrait"]`; tapping the seat opens its seat sheet and clears the gate |
| `draw` | the dock's Draw button (L75-05) |
| `<cardId>` | each spotlit hand or special card (L75-05) |
| `use` / `upgrade` / `sell` | the spotlit card's sheet button `[data-card-action]` (L75-05) |
| `shop-upgrade-point` / `shop-absorber` / `shop-absorber-buy` | the Upgrade points Buy; the Absorber tile; Buy selected once Absorber is selected (L75-05) |

**Browser check:** none in this task's acceptance. The callout, coach and tour visuals ride the controller's Check 2 after L75-05 (ruling 13); see that task.

**Acceptance map:**
- Tutorial view at index 0 in portrait, landscape-compact and desktop boxes: the 33 `$name, step $n` rows.
- Each tour step marks exactly its mapped element: the same rows (`expectMarked`). It exists even when empty (Q60): `marks an empty group too…` and the empty-plate `incoming` rows.
- Got it advances without sending an intent: the tour rows (`expectNoArenaIntent` after each Got it) and `ends after the eleventh Got it…`.
- Drag, Use and Draw send nothing during the tour: `during the tour Draw, a hand tap, a drag, Shop and Unspy…`.
- At index 9 every send stays blocked until the revealed seat sheet opens, then never again in that mount: `at index 9 blocks every send…`.
- The coach hides, reopens from the menu and reopens on new copy: `hides with ×, reopens from Menu → Show coach…`. Its distinct icon (Q45) is in the same test.
- Fake timers retitle it 'Play' after 20 s: `retitles the coach 'Play' after 20 s…` and `never retitles $label`.
- The incoming Basic at index 3 is ringed in attack tone: `rings the incoming Basic at index 3…`.
- The finished tutorial board has no coach: `shows no coach, no mark and no lock on the finished tutorial board` and App's `opens the finished tutorial board on the arena, with no coach`.
- The flag opens 'Skip the tutorial?' and Skip only leaves: `opens 'Skip the tutorial?' from the flag during the tour…`.
- Watch point (layers): the sheet-stack `Z_INDEX` literal and clamp tests, and Review Focus 3.
- Review Focus 3: `stays visible and pressable over $label…` (both rows) and `Got it advances the tour with the seat sheet open…`.
- Review Focus 1, coach half: `keeps its message key and its hidden state from portrait to desktop to landscape-compact`.
- Brief extras:
  - orange callouts with the arrow outside, static under reduced motion: the `TutorialCalloutLayer` and `calloutPlacement` tests, then Check 2;
  - tutorial seats hide the bot difficulty and the clock is `none`: `hides the bot difficulty…`;
  - the id map is in the backlog: Step 5.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Callouts are a measured portal layer** (now fixed in contract A.8.6). It is the only way to stay unclipped by the hand `ScrollArea`, the arena's `overflow-hidden` and sheet bodies without changing Domain B's overflow classes. It filters to the live layer.
- **The callout layer reuses `Z_INDEX.hint` (K6).** The contract pins the `Z_INDEX` literal, and sharing is safe because `shouldShowFirstGameHints` (inside `selectHint`) keeps every first-game hint out of a tutorial room. L75-06 must keep that gate; its `a tutorial room shows no first-game hint` row pins it.
- **`KitPortrait` gains `dataAttributes?` (K7).** It is a `ui/` change, so the Kit section of DESIGN.md gets its line in this commit (Step 5). `OpponentSeatProps.tutorialHighlight?` stays private to the arena, fed by `ArenaSeatsProps.tutorial`.
- **Designer question:** callout colour. Default `--threat-other` (#ffa53d), the V7 "orange otherwise" of §6.12, with no new token.
- **Designer question:** callout chrome. Default: a 3 px ring 4 px out, and a 20×12 arrow on the side facing the screen centre. Opacity pulses only under full motion.
- **Designer question:** coach slot. Default: the arena region's top-left, 8 px in, `min(22rem, 100% − 1rem)`, in every mode. V2 used a fixed top-left under the turn strip; in landscape-compact the panel covers the left band seats until hidden.
- **Focus.** A press on the coach never moves focus (Review Focus 3). From the keyboard, × hands focus to Menu and Got it to the next Got it, so focus never drops to `body`. The Got it hand-off is gated on a ref that only `ack` arms (focus was inside the slot) and the layout effect clears after use, so a copy change that is not a Got it (the first paint, a follow-coach copy, the idle 'Play' title) never moves focus out of an open sheet. The `CoachPanel` itself takes no focus.
- **The Look exit** is opening the seat sheet of a seat with `!kitHidden` (contract A.8.2). The highlight marks only `spy-plus` seats.
- **`allows` in L75-04 is the lock half** (`!(active && locksTable)`), and this task composes `allow` (K4). L75-05 adds the script half and the follow-coach `illegalIndex`.
- **The coach slot is measured by the host (K8).** `TutorialCoachHost` reads the `[data-arena-region="arena"]` rect itself, so `TutorialCoachHostProps` stays as A.8.3 has it.
- **Dock lock.** `locked` stops only Shop and Unspy; Draw and duplication reach `allow` and are vetoed there (A.8.7). A tap guard still precedes the lock in `onOpenShop`.
- **The fixture** keeps the setup's 38 points at every index; the table in Step 3 is the contract's "what the script expects there", not a server replay.
- **New strings:** none. Reused: `OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL` 'Show coach', `HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL` 'Hide coach', `GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL` 'Got it', every `TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS`, `TUTORIAL_STEPS` and `TUTORIAL_LOOK_COACH` copy, and 'Skip tutorial' / 'Skip the tutorial?'.

/**
 * The tutorial gate's legal-kind mapping: technical spec v7 §6.12, §6.6.4; Lot 75 plan Appendix A.8.5
 * (L75-05). One row per ArenaIntent kind, then the cards the POV does not hold.
 */
import { ACTIVE_SHIELD_INSTANCE_ID } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { makeCard, makePlayingView, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import type { TutorialSendIntent } from '../../view-model/tutorial-coach-copy';
import type { ArenaIntent } from './arena-commit';
import { tutorialIntentOf } from './tutorial-gate';

const VIEW = makePlayingView({
  self: makeSelf({
    hand: [makeCard('basic-attack', { isUpgraded: true }), makeCard('shield'), makeCard('spy')],
    specialCards: [makeCard('super-regeneration')],
  }),
});
const OTHER: TutorialSendIntent = { kind: 'other' };

describe('tutorialIntentOf (A.8.5)', () => {
  it.each<{ label: string; intent: ArenaIntent; expected: TutorialSendIntent }>([
    { label: 'drawCard', intent: { kind: 'drawCard' }, expected: { kind: 'draw' } },
    { label: 'playCard: a held upgraded Basic', intent: { kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'basic-attack-1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' } }, expected: { kind: 'playCard', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: true } },
    { label: 'playCard: a held special', intent: { kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'super-regeneration-1' } }, expected: { kind: 'playCard', cardId: 'super-regeneration', isUpgraded: false } },
    { label: 'sellCard', intent: { kind: 'sellCard', payload: { instanceId: 'shield-1' } }, expected: { kind: 'sellCard', cardId: 'shield' } },
    { label: 'upgradeCard: a held card', intent: { kind: 'upgradeCard', payload: { instanceId: 'spy-1' } }, expected: { kind: 'upgradeCard', cardId: 'spy' } },
    { label: 'buyCard', intent: { kind: 'buyCard', payload: { cardId: 'absorber' } }, expected: { kind: 'buyCard', cardId: 'absorber' } },
    { label: 'buyUpgradePoint', intent: { kind: 'buyUpgradePoint' }, expected: { kind: 'buyUpgradePoint' } },
    { label: 'playMultipleAttacks', intent: { kind: 'playMultipleAttacks', payload: { attacks: [] } }, expected: { kind: 'playMultipleAttacks' } },
    { label: 'sellUpgradePoint', intent: { kind: 'sellUpgradePoint' }, expected: { kind: 'sellUpgradePoint' } },
    { label: 'buySpecialCard', intent: { kind: 'buySpecialCard' }, expected: { kind: 'buySpecialCard' } },
    { label: 'buyPoolCard', intent: { kind: 'buyPoolCard' }, expected: OTHER },
    { label: 'clearSpy', intent: { kind: 'clearSpy', payload: { targetPlayerId: 'p1' } }, expected: OTHER },
    { label: 'deactivatePersistent', intent: { kind: 'deactivatePersistent', payload: { effectId: 'eff-poison' } }, expected: OTHER },
    { label: 'activateDuplication', intent: { kind: 'activateDuplication' }, expected: OTHER },
    { label: 'resolveSubChoice (never consulted: allows passes it outside the locks)', intent: { kind: 'resolveSubChoice', payload: { kind: 'mirror', pendingEffectId: 'e1', newTargetPlayerId: 'p1' } }, expected: OTHER },
    { label: 'playCard: a card the POV does not hold', intent: { kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'gone' } }, expected: OTHER },
    { label: 'sellCard: a card the POV does not hold', intent: { kind: 'sellCard', payload: { instanceId: 'gone' } }, expected: OTHER },
    { label: 'upgradeCard: a card in play (the active Shield)', intent: { kind: 'upgradeCard', payload: { instanceId: ACTIVE_SHIELD_INSTANCE_ID } }, expected: OTHER },
  ])('$label', ({ intent, expected }) => {
    expect(tutorialIntentOf(intent, VIEW)).toEqual(expected);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-tutorial-gate.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Every arena commit path is gated in the tutorial: technical spec v7 §6.12, §6.6.4, §6.6.3, §6.8;
 * Lot 75 plan Appendix A.8.4–A.8.6 (L75-05); Review Focus 2 (a vetoed send leaves its control live).
 */
import {
  TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS, tutorialStepAt, type MirrorChoiceRequiredPayload, type PlayingStateView,
} from '@card-battle/shared';
import { fireEvent, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { readVisualViewportBox } from '../../ui/visual-viewport';
import { CARD_BUY_LABEL, UNSPY_ACTION_LABEL } from '../../view-model/chrome-labels';
import { SHOP_TAB_LABELS } from '../../view-model/shop';
import { DEACTIVATE_CONFIRM_TITLE, GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL } from '../../view-model/table-copy';
import { makeCard, makePending, makePersistent } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ILLEGAL_ACTION_COPY } from '../illegal-action-copy';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { CANCEL_ACTION_LABEL, DEACTIVATE_ACTION_LABEL } from './card-sheet-actions';
import { UNSPY_CONFIRM_LABEL } from './dock-model';
import { sellZoneRect } from './hand-drag';
import { SUB_CHOICE_COPY } from './sub-choice/sub-choice-model';
import {
  ARENA_INTENT_PROPS, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeTutorialArenaView, TUTORIAL_HUMAN_INDICES,
} from './test-view';

type User = ReturnType<typeof renderUi>['user'];
type Sent = readonly [(typeof ARENA_INTENT_PROPS)[number], readonly unknown[]];
const FOLLOW = ILLEGAL_ACTION_COPY['tutorial-follow-coach'];
/** Reduced motion by default; the drop test passes `reducedMotion: false` (see it): under reduced motion `springBack` (`use-hand-drag.ts`) clears the ghost at once. */
function setup(index: number, options: { props?: Partial<ArenaScreenProps>; view?: (view: PlayingStateView) => PlayingStateView; reducedMotion?: boolean } = {}) {
  const view = makeTutorialArenaView(index);
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view: options.view?.(view) ?? view, ...options.props });
  return { props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, { reducedMotion: options.reducedMotion ?? true }) };
}
function one(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (element === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return element;
}
const region = (name: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
const coach = (): HTMLElement | null => document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="tutorial-coach"]');
const coachTitle = (): string | null => coach()?.querySelector('h2')?.textContent ?? null;
const handCard = (id: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="hand"] [data-instance-id="${id}"]`);
const cardSheet = (id: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-sheet="card:${id}"]`);
const action = (sheet: HTMLElement, kind: string): HTMLElement => {
  const button = sheet.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-card-action="${kind}"]`);
  if (button === null) throw new Error(`no ${kind}`);
  return button;
};
const dockButton = (anchor: 'draw' | 'shop'): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="dock"] [data-hint-anchor="${anchor}"]`);
const target = (id: string): HTMLElement => screen.getByRole('button', { name: `${id}, target` });
const buyCard = (): HTMLElement => one('[data-arena-sheet="shop"] [data-shop-action="buy-card"]');
const isDisabled = (button: HTMLElement): boolean => button.hasAttribute('disabled') || button.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true';
const PRIMARY = { pointerId: 1, isPrimary: true, button: 0 } as const;
function lift(card: HTMLElement): void {
  fireEvent.pointerDown(card, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 100, clientY: 700 });
  fireEvent.pointerMove(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: 103, clientY: 688 });
}
function dropOnSell(): void {
  const sell = sellZoneRect({ left: 0, top: 0, width: 0, height: 0 }, 100, readVisualViewportBox().width);
  fireEvent.pointerUp(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: sell.left + sell.width / 2, clientY: sell.top + sell.height / 2 });
}
async function finishTour(user: User): Promise<void> {
  for (let step = 0; step < TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS.length; step += 1) await user.click(one('[data-tutorial-ack="got-it"]'));
}
async function clearLook(user: User): Promise<void> {
  await user.click(one('[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="p1"] > button'));
  await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
}
/** Exactly these elements carry `data-tutorial-highlight`, in document order, with these ids. */
function expectMarked(pairs: readonly (readonly [string, Element])[], scope: ParentNode = document): void {
  const marked = Array.from(scope.querySelectorAll('[data-tutorial-highlight]'));
  expect(marked.map((element) => element.getAttribute('data-tutorial-highlight'))).toEqual(pairs.map(([id]) => id));
  expect(marked.every((element, at) => element === pairs[at]?.[1])).toBe(true);
}
/** Exactly one intent: `name` got `args` once, and no other intent prop was called (by name, never by spy identity). */
function expectOnly(props: ArenaScreenProps, [name, args]: Sent): void {
  for (const prop of ARENA_INTENT_PROPS) {
    const handler = props[prop];
    if (handler !== undefined) expect(vi.mocked(handler).mock.calls).toEqual(prop === name ? [args] : []);
  }
}
function expectFollowCoach(): void {
  expect(coachTitle()).toBe(FOLLOW.title);
  expect(coach()?.textContent).toContain(FOLLOW.body);
}

type ScriptRow =
  | { readonly index: number; readonly card: string; readonly id: string; readonly action: 'use' | 'upgrade' | 'sell'; readonly target?: true; readonly sent: Sent }
  | { readonly index: number; readonly dock: 'draw' | 'shop'; readonly shop?: 'upgrade-point' | 'absorber'; readonly sent: Sent };
const basicAt = (index: number): ScriptRow => ({ index, card: 'basic-attack-1', id: 'basic-attack', action: 'use', target: true, sent: ['onPlayCard', ['basic-attack-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p1' }]] });
const spyAt = (index: number): ScriptRow => ({ index, card: 'spy-1', id: 'spy', action: 'use', target: true, sent: ['onPlayCard', ['spy-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p1' }]] });
const SCRIPT: readonly ScriptRow[] = [
  { index: 0, dock: 'draw', sent: ['onDraw', []] },
  { index: 1, card: 'tax-1', id: 'tax', action: 'use', sent: ['onPlayCard', ['tax-1']] },
  basicAt(3),
  { index: 5, card: 'spy-1', id: 'spy', action: 'upgrade', sent: ['onUpgradeCard', ['spy-1']] },
  spyAt(7),
  { index: 9, card: 'shield-1', id: 'shield', action: 'sell', sent: ['onSellCard', ['shield-1']] },
  spyAt(11),
  { index: 13, dock: 'shop', shop: 'upgrade-point', sent: ['onBuyUpgradePoint', []] },
  { index: 15, dock: 'shop', shop: 'absorber', sent: ['onBuyCard', ['absorber']] },
  { index: 17, card: 'shield-2', id: 'shield', action: 'use', sent: ['onPlayCard', ['shield-2']] },
  { index: 19, card: 'super-regeneration-1', id: 'super-regeneration', action: 'use', sent: ['onPlayCard', ['super-regeneration-1']] },
  { index: 21, card: 'tax-1', id: 'tax', action: 'use', sent: ['onPlayCard', ['tax-1']] },
  { index: 23, card: 'basic-attack-1', id: 'basic-attack', action: 'upgrade', sent: ['onUpgradeCard', ['basic-attack-1']] },
  basicAt(25),
  { index: 27, card: 'absorber-1', id: 'absorber', action: 'use', target: true, sent: ['onPlayCard', ['absorber-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p1' }]] },
  basicAt(29),
];

describe('the scripted path at every human index (§6.12, A.8.6)', () => {
  it('covers every human index once', () => {
    expect(SCRIPT.map((row) => row.index)).toEqual(TUTORIAL_HUMAN_INDICES);
  });

  it.each(SCRIPT)('index $index: the highlighted control is marked, and the scripted intent goes out once', async (row) => {
    const { user, props } = setup(row.index);
    if (row.index === 0) await finishTour(user);
    if (row.index === 9) await clearLook(user);
    if ('card' in row) {
      expectMarked([[row.id, handCard(row.card)]]);
      await user.click(handCard(row.card));
      const sheet = cardSheet(row.card);
      expectMarked([[row.action, action(sheet, row.action)]], sheet);
      await user.click(action(sheet, row.action));
      if (row.target === true) await user.click(target('p1'));
    } else {
      expectMarked([[row.dock, dockButton(row.dock)]]);
      await user.click(dockButton(row.dock));
      if (row.shop === 'upgrade-point') {
        const buy = one('[data-arena-sheet="shop"] [data-shop-action="buy-upgrade-point"]');
        expectMarked([['shop-upgrade-point', buy]]);
        await user.click(buy);
      } else if (row.shop === 'absorber') {
        const tile = one('[data-arena-sheet="shop"] [data-shop-tile="absorber"]');
        expectMarked([['shop-absorber', tile]]);
        await user.click(tile);
        expectMarked([['shop-absorber-buy', buyCard()]]);
        await user.click(buyCard());
      }
    }
    expectOnly(props, row.sent);
  });
});

const upgradeBasic = (view: PlayingStateView): PlayingStateView => ({
  ...view, self: { ...view.self, hand: view.self.hand.map((card) => (card.cardId === 'basic-attack' ? { ...card, isUpgraded: true } : card)) },
});
const spiedOn = (view: PlayingStateView): PlayingStateView => ({
  ...view, players: view.players.map((player) => (player.id === 'p1' ? { ...player, spyingOnYou: true } : player)),
});
const withPool = (view: PlayingStateView): PlayingStateView => ({ ...view, pool: [makeCard('mirror', { instanceId: 'pool-mirror' })] });
const WRONG: readonly { label: string; index: number; view?: (view: PlayingStateView) => PlayingStateView; run: (user: User) => Promise<void> }[] = [
  { label: 'another card: Shield at the Tax step', index: 21, run: async (user) => { await user.click(handCard('shield-2')); await user.click(action(cardSheet('shield-2'), 'use')); } },
  { label: 'Basic+ at the Counter step (3)', index: 3, view: upgradeBasic, run: async (user) => { await user.click(handCard('basic-attack-1')); await user.click(action(cardSheet('basic-attack-1'), 'use')); await user.click(target('p1')); } },
  { label: 'buying Basic at the Absorber step', index: 15, run: async (user) => { await user.click(dockButton('shop')); await user.click(buyCard()); } },
  { label: 'a drop on Sell at the Counter step', index: 3, run: () => { lift(handCard('basic-attack-1')); dropOnSell(); return Promise.resolve(); } },
  { label: 'Draw at the Tax step', index: 1, run: async (user) => { await user.click(dockButton('draw')); } },
  { label: 'Unspy at the Tax step', index: 1, view: spiedOn, run: async (user) => { await user.click(one('[data-arena-region="dock"] [data-unspy]')); await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: UNSPY_ACTION_LABEL })).getByRole('button', { name: UNSPY_CONFIRM_LABEL })); } },
  { label: 'Buy random from the pool at the Absorber step', index: 15, view: withPool, run: async (user) => { await user.click(dockButton('shop')); await user.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: SHOP_TAB_LABELS.pool })); await user.click(one('[data-shop-action="buy-pool"]')); } },
];

describe('a wrong path sends nothing (§6.6.4, R8)', () => {
  it.each(WRONG)('$label sends nothing and shows the follow-coach copy', async ({ index, view, run }) => {
    const { user, props } = setup(index, view === undefined ? {} : { view });
    await run(user);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    expectFollowCoach();
  });
});

describe('a vetoed send leaves its control live (Review Focus 2)', () => {
  it('card sheet: a vetoed Use keeps the sheet open with Use enabled; the copy clears once Tax goes out', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(21);
    await user.click(handCard('shield-2'));
    const use = action(cardSheet('shield-2'), 'use');
    await user.click(use);
    expect([cardSheet('shield-2').closest('[inert]'), isDisabled(use)]).toEqual([null, false]);
    expectFollowCoach();
    await user.click(use);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await user.click(handCard('tax-1'));
    await user.click(action(cardSheet('tax-1'), 'use'));
    expectOnly(props, ['onPlayCard', ['tax-1']]);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(tutorialStepAt(21)?.coach?.title);
  });

  it("Draw: the tour's silent veto latches nothing, so after the last Got it the same Draw sends once", async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(0);
    await user.click(dockButton('draw'));
    expect([isDisabled(dockButton('draw')), coachTitle()]).toEqual([false, TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS[0]?.coach.title]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await finishTour(user);
    await user.click(dockButton('draw'));
    expectOnly(props, ['onDraw', []]);
  });

  it('Shop: a vetoed Buy keeps the Shop open, and Absorber then buys once', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(15);
    await user.click(dockButton('shop'));
    await user.click(buyCard());
    expect([one('[data-arena-sheet="shop"]').closest('[inert]'), isDisabled(buyCard())]).toEqual([null, false]);
    expectFollowCoach();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(one('[data-shop-tile="absorber"]'));
    await user.click(within(one('[data-arena-sheet="shop"]')).getByRole('button', { name: CARD_BUY_LABEL }));
    expectOnly(props, ['onBuyCard', ['absorber']]);
  });

  it('targeting: a vetoed seat tap keeps targeting live, and Basic then targets once', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(3);
    await user.click(handCard('spy-1'));
    await user.click(action(cardSheet('spy-1'), 'use'));
    await user.click(target('p1'));
    expect([document.querySelector('[data-zone="targeting-bar"]') !== null, target('p1').getAttribute('data-target-seat')]).toEqual([true, 'valid']);
    expectFollowCoach();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(within(one('[data-zone="targeting-bar"]')).getByRole('button', { name: CANCEL_ACTION_LABEL }));
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await user.click(handCard('basic-attack-1'));
    await user.click(action(cardSheet('basic-attack-1'), 'use'));
    await user.click(target('p1'));
    expectOnly(props, ['onPlayCard', ['basic-attack-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p1' }]]);
  });

  it('drop: a vetoed drop on Sell springs back, and Basic then goes out once', async () => {
    // Full motion, as `commit-veto.test.tsx` renders it: under reduced motion the ghost is gone at once, so only the animated spring leaves one to assert.
    const { user, props } = setup(3, { reducedMotion: false });
    lift(handCard('basic-attack-1'));
    dropOnSell();
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="hand-drag-ghost"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expectFollowCoach();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    handCard('basic-attack-1').focus();
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');
    await user.click(action(cardSheet('basic-attack-1'), 'use'));
    await user.click(target('p1'));
    expectOnly(props, ['onPlayCard', ['basic-attack-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p1' }]]);
  });

  it('Deactivate: a vetoed confirm stays open with Deactivate enabled (carry-forward fix)', async () => {
    const poison = makePersistent({ id: 'eff-poison', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 });
    const { user, props } = setup(1, { view: (view) => ({ ...view, self: { ...view.self, activePersistentEffects: [poison] } }) });
    await user.click(within(region('self-strip')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Poison, 3 card lives' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Poison' })).getByRole('button', { name: DEACTIVATE_ACTION_LABEL }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: DEACTIVATE_CONFIRM_TITLE });
    const deactivate = within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: DEACTIVATE_ACTION_LABEL });
    await user.click(deactivate);
    expect([screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: DEACTIVATE_CONFIRM_TITLE }) === confirm, confirm.closest('[inert]'), isDisabled(deactivate)]).toEqual([true, null, false]);
    expectFollowCoach();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});

describe('sub-choice answers (open question 8)', () => {
  const MIRROR: MirrorChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: ['tut-e1'], deadlineMs: 40_000 };
  const incoming = (view: PlayingStateView): PlayingStateView => ({
    ...view, pendingEffects: [makePending({ id: 'tut-e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'basic-attack' })],
  });
  const confirmRedirect = async (user: User): Promise<void> => {
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: SUB_CHOICE_COPY.mirror.title })).getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm redirect' }));
  };

  it('goes out outside the tour and the Look gate', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(1, { props: { subChoice: MIRROR }, view: incoming });
    await confirmRedirect(user);
    expect(vi.mocked(props.onResolveSubChoice).mock.calls).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('is held silently during the tour, with no follow-coach copy', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(0, { props: { subChoice: MIRROR }, view: incoming });
    await confirmRedirect(user);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    expect(coachTitle()).toBe(TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS[0]?.coach.title);
    expect(document.querySelector(`[data-zone="tutorial-coach"] [data-tutorial-ack="got-it"]`)?.textContent).toBe(GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL);
  });
});
```

That is 32 tests (1 + 16 + 7 + 6 + 2). Add to `apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet.test.tsx` (import `waitFor`, `DEACTIVATE_CONFIRM_TITLE`, `DEACTIVATE_ACTION_LABEL` and `type ArenaCommit`):

  it('closes the Deactivate confirm only on a sent commit: a veto keeps it open with Deactivate live (L75-05)', async () => {
    const commit = vi.fn<ArenaCommit>().mockReturnValueOnce(false).mockReturnValue(true);
    const onClose = vi.fn();

    };

In `apps/client/src/view-model/tutorial-coach-copy.test.ts`, delete the `'marks incoming Attack, Spy, and Thief as threats'` case (L238–253) and the `tutorialIncomingThreatIds` import (:23). The `tutorialShopSpotlight` import (:26) and its assertions stay.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail.** `pnpm vitest run apps/client/src/screens/arena/tutorial-gate.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-tutorial-gate.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-own-fx.test.tsx`. Expected:

  - in `arena-tutorial-gate.test.tsx`:
    - `covers every human index once` passes;
    - the script rows fail on `expectMarked`, since no script mark exists yet;
    - the wrong paths and the Review Focus 2 cases send their intent (no script gate);
    - the tour-held sub-choice, `sub-choice answers › goes out outside the tour and the Look gate` (the lock half allows every intent at index 1) and the `Draw` lock case already pass (L75-04's lock half);
  - the card-sheet case closes the confirm on the veto;
  - the own-FX case flies the Shield.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - **`tutorial-gate.ts`.**
    - A private `held(view, instanceId)` returns the first of `view.self.hand` then `view.self.specialCards` with that `instanceId`, else `undefined`.
    - The switch, in `ArenaIntent` order, ending in a `never` default:
      - `drawCard` → `{ kind: 'draw' }`;
      - `playCard` → `{ kind: 'playCard', cardId, isUpgraded }` of the held card, else `OTHER`;
      - `sellCard` → `{ kind: 'sellCard', cardId }`, else `OTHER`;
      - `upgradeCard` → `{ kind: 'upgradeCard', cardId }`, else `OTHER` (an in-play target is not held);
      - `buyCard` → `{ kind: 'buyCard', cardId: intent.payload.cardId }`;
      - `buyUpgradePoint`, `playMultipleAttacks`, `sellUpgradePoint`, `buySpecialCard` → their own kind;
      - `buyPoolCard`, `clearSpy`, `deactivatePersistent`, `activateDuplication` and `resolveSubChoice` → `OTHER`, each an explicit case. `resolveSubChoice` needs its case only for the exhaustive switch: `allows` never consults `tutorialIntentOf` for it (K5).
    - Cite A.8.5 and `game-room.ts` `rejectTutorialIllegalAction` in the doc comment.
  - **`use-arena-tutorial.ts`.**
    - Add `useState<number | null>(null)` for `illegalIndex`, and `illegal = !locksTable && index !== null && illegalIndex === index`.
    - The coach precedence becomes: `locksTable` → the presentation copy; `illegal` → `resolveIllegalActionCopy('tutorial-follow-coach', '')` (title and body); otherwise the script copy with the idle title. The `messageKey` mode stays `script` for the follow-coach copy, so a veto reopens a hidden coach (V2).
    - `allows(intent)`:
      - inactive → `true`;
      - `locksTable` → `false`, without touching `illegalIndex`;
      - `resolveSubChoice` → `true` (open question 8), without calling `tutorialIntentOf` (K5);
      - `!tutorialSendAllowed(index, tutorialIntentOf(intent, view))` → `setIllegalIndex(index)`, `false`;

```
## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] Every arena commit path is gated in the tutorial (L75-05)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.12, §6.6.4, §6.8; the Lot 75 plan, Appendix A.8.4–A.8.6 and A.8.9; controller
ruling R8 and open question 8 (2026-11-03).

- **One gate.** `allow = !reconnecting && tutorial.allows(intent)`. `tutorialIntentOf` maps each
  `ArenaIntent` kind through an exhaustive switch. Unspy, pool buys, Deactivate and Activate duplication
  map to `other`, which no human step allows; the server rejects them the same way (`tutorial-follow-coach`).
  V2 never gated Deactivate or the sub-choice answers.
- **Sub-choice answers** pass outside the tour and the Look gate. The server gates only turn actions, and
  a vetoed answer would strand the player under a blocking sheet.
- **The veto lands on the send.** Use on a targeted card still enters targeting; the gate vetoes the
  seat tap. V2 vetoed at Use. A veto shows the follow-coach copy, keeps the control live and flies no
  own FX.
- **Spotlights.** The card sheet marks Use, Upgrade or Sell only on the spotlit card's sheet; V2 marked
  it on any card's sheet. The Shop highlights and never preselects (§6.8): the Absorber tile until it is
  selected, then Buy selected.
- **Kept:** `tutorialShopSpotlight`, because `view-model/shop.ts` uses it (the brief's removal was wrong,
  R8), and the `arena-sheets.ts` early return, because no caller passes a Shop `tab`.
- **Deleted:** `tutorialIncomingThreatIds` and `isTutorialIncomingThreatCard`, dead since L51-07.
- **Carry-forward fix:** the card-sheet Deactivate confirm closes only on a sent commit.

A defect is fixed in its own `fix(arena): … (L75-NN)` commit; anything unreachable goes to the workspace `gate-items.md` for L75-12.

**Acceptance map:**
- A table-driven test covers every human index (0 and odd 1–29): `covers every human index once` and the 16 `index $index` rows.
- At each index the highlighted control carries `data-tutorial-highlight`: the `expectMarked` checks in those rows, including the sheet button and the Shop items.
- The scripted intent reaches a mocked connection with the right payload: `expectOnly` in those rows.
- A wrong path (another card, Basic+ at index 3, buying Basic, a drop on Sell) sends nothing and shows the follow-coach copy: the `WRONG` rows, which add Draw, Unspy and pool buys (R8).
- A tutorial room under `?ui=next` renders the arena: L75-04's `opens a tutorial room on the arena under ?ui=next…` (R9).
- The full tutorial (0–30) is played to the win in the browser at 375×812: Check 2.
- Own-play ghosts fire only when the send passes: `a tutorial veto flies nothing; the scripted send then flies once`.
- Every commit path is gated (R8): the 18 `tutorialIntentOf` rows, plus the arena Unspy, pool and Deactivate paths.
- Review Focus 2: the six `a vetoed send leaves its control live` cases (card sheet, Draw, Shop, targeting, drop, Deactivate) and the card-sheet confirm unit test. The copy shows on the veto and clears after the next allowed send in the card-sheet case.
- Open question 8: the two `sub-choice answers` cases.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **The veto lands on the send** (§6.6.4: Use on a targeted card sends nothing). The tutorial never vetoes Use itself.
- **Designer question:** the card-sheet spotlight. Default: only on the spotlit card's sheet; V2 marked any card's sheet.
- **Designer question:** the Shop Absorber mark. Default: on the tile until it is selected, then on Buy selected only.
- **The follow-coach copy** comes from `resolveIllegalActionCopy('tutorial-follow-coach', '')`. It shows only while `illegalIndex === index` and nothing locks, so a new index or an allowed send clears it (V2 precedence).
- **The Draw case of Review Focus 2** uses the tour's silent veto. A Draw cannot become legal at a later index in the same view object, so finishing the tour is the only same-view way to prove the latch stayed open.
- **The drop case's scripted retry uses the keyboard** (Enter on the card). A pointer press during the spring-back would start a new drag phase.
- **The drop case renders with full motion** (`setup(3, { reducedMotion: false })`; every other case in the file keeps reduced motion). Under reduced motion `springBack` (`use-hand-drag.ts:101-106`) clears the ghost in the same update as the veto, so the ghost assertion, the only proof the drop sprang back, can only hold under the animated spring. `commit-veto.test.tsx` renders its drop test the same way.
- **New strings:** none. Reused: `ILLEGAL_ACTION_COPY['tutorial-follow-coach']` ('Tutorial step' / 'This tutorial step asks for a different action.') and every Shop and card-sheet label.

export interface ArenaHintsInput {
  readonly view: PlayingStateView;
  readonly readOnly: boolean;
  /** `strip.pov`: only 'seated' shows hints. */
  readonly pov: PovMode;

}

  readonly dialogOpen: boolean;
  readonly note: (cause: HintDismissCause) => void;
  readonly onGotIt: () => void;
  readonly onSkipAll: () => void;
}
export function useArenaHints(input: ArenaHintsInput): ArenaHints;

/**
 * Hint dismiss causes on the arena: technical spec v7 §6.12 (the L46 auto-dismiss rules); Lot 75 plan
 * A.9.2, ruling R11 (L75-06). V2 parity: the `noteHintCause` call sites in `screens/table.tsx`.
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { HintDismissCause } from '../../view-model/select-hint';
import { makeCard } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import type { ArenaIntent } from './arena-commit';
import { hintCauseForCardSheet, hintCauseForIntent } from './arena-hint-causes';
import type { CardSheetSource } from './card-sheet-source';

const PLAYING = 'playing-intent';
const SPY = makeCard('spy');

describe('hintCauseForIntent (A.9.2)', () => {
  it.each<{ label: string; intent: ArenaIntent; cause: HintDismissCause | null }>([
    { label: 'drawCard', intent: { kind: 'drawCard' }, cause: 'draw' },
    { label: 'playCard', intent: { kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'c1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' } }, cause: PLAYING },
    { label: 'playMultipleAttacks', intent: { kind: 'playMultipleAttacks', payload: { attacks: [{ instanceId: 'c1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' }] } }, cause: PLAYING },
    { label: 'sellCard', intent: { kind: 'sellCard', payload: { instanceId: 'c1' } }, cause: PLAYING },
    { label: 'upgradeCard', intent: { kind: 'upgradeCard', payload: { instanceId: 'c1' } }, cause: PLAYING },
    { label: 'buyCard', intent: { kind: 'buyCard', payload: { cardId: 'spy' } }, cause: PLAYING },

    { label: 'a Mirror answer', intent: { kind: 'resolveSubChoice', payload: { kind: 'mirror', pendingEffectId: 'e1', newTargetPlayerId: 'p2' } }, cause: null },
