```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * HintOverlay on the V7 coach chrome — technical spec v7 §6.12 (a compact translucent panel next to
 * `data-hint-anchor`), §4.2 (rebuilt on `ui/`); L46-01, L46-02; Appendix A `first-game-hint-host`;
 * classification rows 90, 91 and 93 (L75-06). The old-table reads stay in `hint-overlay.test.ts`.
 */
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../test/render';
import { hintAnchorId, hintPlacePrefer, type HintId } from '../ui/hint-ids';
import { Z_INDEX } from '../ui/layers';
import { GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL, HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL } from '../view-model/table-copy';
import { HINT_COPY, SKIP_ALL_HINTS_LABEL } from './hint-copy';
import { HintOverlay } from './hint-overlay';
import { placeHintCard } from './place-hint-card';

function one(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (found === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return found;
}
function addAnchor(id: string, rect: DOMRect): void {
  const node = document.createElement('div');
  node.setAttribute('data-hint-anchor', id);
  document.body.append(node);
  vi.spyOn(node, 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue(rect);
}
function setup(hintId: HintId, dialogOpen = false) {
  const onGotIt = vi.fn<() => void>();
  const onSkipAll = vi.fn<() => void>();
  return { onGotIt, onSkipAll, ...renderUi(<HintOverlay hintId={hintId} dialogOpen={dialogOpen} onGotIt={onGotIt} onSkipAll={onSkipAll} />) };
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  for (const node of document.querySelectorAll('[data-hint-anchor]')) node.remove();
});

describe('HintOverlay (§6.12, L46-01, L46-02)', () => {
  it('is a non-modal card: Got it and × call onGotIt, Skip all calls onSkipAll, no toggle, no focus taken', async () => {
    const { user, onGotIt, onSkipAll } = setup('draw');
    const card = screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: HINT_COPY.draw.title });
    expect([card.getAttribute('data-zone'), card.getAttribute('data-hint-id'), screen.queryByRole('dialog'), document.activeElement])
      .toEqual(['first-game-hint', 'draw', null, document.body]);
    const buttons = within(card).getAllByRole('button');
    expect(buttons.map((button) => button.getAttribute('aria-label') ?? button.textContent)).toEqual([HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL, GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL, SKIP_ALL_HINTS_LABEL]);
    expect([one('[data-hint-ack="got-it"]').getAttribute('data-variant'), one('[data-hint-ack="skip-all"]').getAttribute('data-variant')]).toEqual(['primary', 'quiet']);
    for (const button of buttons) await user.click(button);
    expect([onGotIt.mock.calls.length, onSkipAll.mock.calls.length]).toEqual([2, 1]);
  });

  it('renders nothing while dialogOpen', () => {
    setup('draw', true);
    expect([document.querySelector('[data-hint-layer]'), document.querySelector('[data-zone="first-game-hint"]')]).toEqual([null, null]);
  });

  it('portals one pointer-transparent layer to document.body on Z_INDEX.hint; only the card takes presses', () => {
    setup('draw');
    const layer = one('[data-hint-layer]');
    expect([layer.parentElement, layer.style.zIndex, layer.classList.contains('pointer-events-none'), one('[data-hint-slot]').classList.contains('pointer-events-auto')])
      .toEqual([document.body, String(Z_INDEX.hint), true, true]);
  });

  it.each(['draw', 'hand', 'incoming-thief'] as const)('places %s next to its first visible anchor', (id) => {
    addAnchor(hintAnchorId(id), new DOMRect(0, 0, 0, 0)); // mounted at zero size: skipped
    const rect = new DOMRect(150, 300, 90, 40);
    addAnchor(hintAnchorId(id), rect);
    setup(id);
    const slot = one('[data-hint-slot]');
    const placed = placeHintCard(rect, { width: slot.offsetWidth, height: slot.offsetHeight }, { width: window.innerWidth, height: window.innerHeight }, { prefer: hintPlacePrefer(id) });
    expect([slot.style.top, slot.style.left]).toEqual([`${String(placed.top)}px`, `${String(placed.left)}px`]);
  });

  it('falls back to the bottom-left corner when no anchor is rendered', () => {
    setup('shop');
    const slot = one('[data-hint-slot]');
    expect([slot.style.left, slot.style.bottom]).toEqual(['0.5rem', '7.5rem']);
  });
});
```

That is 7 tests. happy-dom leaves `visualViewport` undefined, so the overlay reads `window.innerWidth/innerHeight` (390×844 under R2), as the test does.

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-hints.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * First-game hints on the arena: technical spec v7 §6.12 (same selector order and auto-dismiss rules,
 * a compact panel next to `data-hint-anchor`), §6.3 (Q66), §6.7, §14.3; L46-01..03; Appendix A
 * `first-game-hint-host`, `table-first-game-hints`, `global-help-storage`; Lot 75 plan A.9, ruling
 * R11 (L75-06). Also the arena replacements that classification rows 92 and 96 name.
 */
import { formatCardLabel, type PendingEffectView, type RewardChoiceRequiredPayload, type SubChoiceRequiredPayload } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { SKIP_ALL_HINTS_LABEL } from '../../help/hint-copy';
import { placeHintCard } from '../../help/place-hint-card';
import { renderUi, type RenderUiOptions, type RenderUiResult } from '../../test/render';
import { HINTS_STORAGE_KEY, readHintState, resetHelpStorage, writeHintState } from '../../ui/help-storage';
import { HINT_IDS, hintAnchorId, hintPlacePrefer, type HintId } from '../../ui/hint-ids';
import { Z_INDEX } from '../../ui/layers';
import { MINIMIZE_LABEL } from '../../ui/ui-copy';
import { SHOP_ACTION_LABEL } from '../../view-model/chrome-labels';
import { GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL, HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL, RETURN_HOME_ARIA_LABEL, SKIP_TUTORIAL_ARIA_LABEL } from '../../view-model/table-copy';
import { makeCard, makePending, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { SUB_CHOICE_COPY } from './sub-choice/sub-choice-model';
import { ARENA_TEST_BOXES, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView, makeTutorialArenaView, stubArenaLayout, type ArenaLayoutStub } from './test-view';
import { LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME, MENU_LABEL } from './top-bar-model';

type User = RenderUiResult['user'];
const REWARD: RewardChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'elimination-reward', eliminationId: 'el-1', eliminatedPlayerId: 'p1', availableCards: [], deadlineMs: 40_000 };
const REWARD_TITLE = SUB_CHOICE_COPY['elimination-reward'].title;
const MIRROR: SubChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 40_000 };
const onMe = (cardId: 'basic-attack' | 'thief'): PendingEffectView => makePending({ id: `e-${cardId}`, sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId });
const ON_TURN: readonly HintId[] = ['your-turn', 'draw', 'hand', 'specials', 'shop', 'resources'];
const HELD = makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ hand: [makeCard('tax')], specialCards: [makeCard('super-regeneration')] }) } });
const ANCHOR = new DOMRect(120, 220, 80, 44);
const WIDTH = { narrow: 'w-[min(15.5rem,calc(100vw-1rem))]', wide: 'w-[min(22rem,calc(100vw-1rem))]' } as const;
const MODES = [['portrait', ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390], ['landscape-compact', ARENA_TEST_BOXES.landscape], ['desktop', ARENA_TEST_BOXES.desktop]] as const;

let layout: ArenaLayoutStub | undefined;
beforeEach(() => { resetHelpStorage(); });
afterEach(() => { layout?.restore(); layout = undefined; });

function setup(overrides: Partial<ArenaScreenProps> = {}, options: RenderUiOptions = {}): RenderUiResult & { props: ArenaScreenProps } {
  const props = makeArenaProps(overrides);
  return { props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, options) };
}
function one(selector: string, root: ParentNode = document): HTMLElement {
  const found = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (found === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return found;
}
const shown = (): string | null => document.querySelector('[data-zone="first-game-hint"]')?.getAttribute('data-hint-id') ?? null;
const hint = (): HTMLElement => one('[data-zone="first-game-hint"]');
const region = (name: string): HTMLElement => one(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
const openCard = (user: User, group: 'Hand' | 'Specials', cardId: 'tax' | 'super-regeneration'): Promise<void> =>
  user.click(within(screen.getByRole('list', { name: group })).getByRole('button', { name: formatCardLabel(cardId, false) }));
const useTax = async (user: User): Promise<void> => {
  await openCard(user, 'Hand', 'tax');
  await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: formatCardLabel('tax', false) })).getByRole('button', { name: /^Use/ }));
};
const tapSeat = (user: User, id: string): Promise<void> =>
  user.click(within(one(`[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="${id}"]`)).getByRole('button', { name: id }));
const draw = (user: User): Promise<void> => user.click(within(region('dock')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Draw, plus / }));

/** The props and stored dismissals that make `id` the selected hint (selector order, select-hint.ts). */
function caseFor(id: HintId): { readonly props: Partial<ArenaScreenProps>; readonly dismissed: readonly HintId[] } {
  switch (id) {
    case 'incoming': return { props: { view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { pendingEffects: [onMe('basic-attack')] } }) }, dismissed: [] };
    case 'incoming-thief': return { props: { view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { pendingEffects: [onMe('thief')] } }) }, dismissed: [] };
    case 'hidden-kit': return { props: { view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' } }) }, dismissed: [] };
    case 'reward': return { props: { subChoice: REWARD }, dismissed: [] };
    default: return { props: {}, dismissed: ON_TURN.slice(0, ON_TURN.indexOf(id)) };
  }
}

describe('hint placement in every layout mode (§6.12, §14.3)', () => {
  it.each(MODES.flatMap(([mode, box]) => HINT_IDS.map((id) => ({ mode, box, id }))))('$id sits next to its anchor in $mode', ({ mode, box, id }) => {
    const selector = `[data-hint-anchor="${hintAnchorId(id)}"]`;
    layout = stubArenaLayout(box.viewport, box.arena, { rectFor: (element) => (element.matches(selector) ? ANCHOR : undefined) });
    const { props, dismissed } = caseFor(id);
    writeHintState({ dismissed, skipAll: false });
    setup(props);
    expect([one('[data-arena="next"]').getAttribute('data-layout-mode'), shown()]).toEqual([mode, id]);
    const layer = one('[data-hint-layer]');
    const slot = one('[data-hint-slot]', layer);
    const placed = placeHintCard(ANCHOR, { width: slot.offsetWidth, height: slot.offsetHeight }, box.viewport, { prefer: hintPlacePrefer(id) });
    expect([layer.parentElement, layer.style.zIndex, slot.style.top, slot.style.left])
      .toEqual([document.body, String(Z_INDEX.hint), `${String(placed.top)}px`, `${String(placed.left)}px`]);
    expect(slot.classList.contains(mode === 'landscape-compact' ? WIDTH.wide : WIDTH.narrow)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('keeps one hint card across mode changes and re-places it at the new anchor (Review Focus 1 invariant)', () => {
    let rect = new DOMRect(40, 600, 120, 44);
    const stub = stubArenaLayout(ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390.viewport, ARENA_TEST_BOXES.phone390.arena, {
      rectFor: (element) => (element.matches('[data-hint-anchor="draw"]') ? rect : undefined),
    });
    layout = stub;
    writeHintState({ dismissed: ['your-turn'], skipAll: false });
    setup();
    const card = hint();
    for (const [width, height, mode, cls] of [[1440, 900, 'desktop', WIDTH.narrow], [844, 390, 'landscape-compact', WIDTH.wide]] as const) {
      rect = new DOMRect(width - 300, height - 120, 120, 44);
      act(() => { stub.setViewport(width, height); });
      const slot = one('[data-hint-slot]');
      const placed = placeHintCard(rect, { width: slot.offsetWidth, height: slot.offsetHeight }, { width, height }, { prefer: 'below' });
      expect([one('[data-arena="next"]').getAttribute('data-layout-mode'), hint() === card, slot.style.top, slot.style.left, slot.classList.contains(cls)])
        .toEqual([mode, true, `${String(placed.top)}px`, `${String(placed.left)}px`, true]);
    }
  });

  it.each([['base-spy', 'hidden-kit'], ['spy-plus', null]] as const)('a lone %s opponent off turn selects %s (§6.3, Q66)', (tier, expected) => {
    setup({ view: makeArenaView({ seatCount: 2, tiers: { p1: tier }, overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' } }) });
    expect(shown()).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe('when hints show (§6.12, R11)', () => {
  it.each<{ label: string; props: () => Partial<ArenaScreenProps>; coach: boolean }>([
    { label: 'a tutorial room', props: () => ({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(1) }), coach: true },
    { label: 'the finished board', props: () => ({ readOnly: true }), coach: false },
    { label: 'the finished tutorial board', props: () => ({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(30), readOnly: true }), coach: false },
    { label: 'an eliminated POV', props: () => ({ view: makeArenaView({ pov: 'eliminated' }) }), coach: false },
    { label: 'a walk-in', props: () => ({ view: makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in' }) }), coach: false },
  ])('$label shows no first-game hint', ({ props, coach }) => {
    setup(props());
    expect([shown(), document.querySelector('[data-zone="tutorial-coach"]') !== null]).toEqual([null, coach]);
  });

  it('hides under a read-only sheet and a non-reward sub-choice, and comes back after (§6.7)', async () => {
    const { user, props, rerender } = setup({}, { reducedMotion: true });
    expect(shown()).toBe('your-turn');
    await user.click(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: MENU_LABEL }));
    expect(shown()).toBeNull();
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await waitFor(() => { expect(shown()).toBe('your-turn'); });
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} subChoice={MIRROR} />);
    expect(shown()).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('opening the Shop hides the card and dismisses shop; closing it shows the next lesson (R11)', async () => {
    writeHintState({ dismissed: ['your-turn', 'draw', 'hand', 'specials'], skipAll: false });
    const { user, props } = setup({}, { reducedMotion: true });
    expect(shown()).toBe('shop');
    await user.click(within(region('dock')).getByRole('button', { name: SHOP_ACTION_LABEL }));
    expect([shown(), readHintState().dismissed.includes('shop')]).toEqual([null, true]);
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await waitFor(() => { expect(shown()).toBe('resources'); });
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('keeps the reward hint over the reward sheet, hides it while minimized and brings it back on restore (R11)', async () => {
    const { user } = setup({ subChoice: REWARD }, { reducedMotion: true });
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: REWARD_TITLE });
    expect(shown()).toBe('reward');
    expect(Number(one('[data-hint-layer]').style.zIndex)).toBeGreaterThan(Number(sheet.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="sheet-overlay"]')?.style.zIndex));
    await user.click(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: MINIMIZE_LABEL }));
    expect(shown()).toBeNull();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: REWARD_TITLE }));
    expect(shown()).toBe('reward');
  });
});

describe('dismissal (L46-01, L46-03)', () => {
  it.each([
    { name: GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL, stored: { dismissed: ['your-turn'], skipAll: false }, next: 'draw' },
    { name: HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL, stored: { dismissed: ['your-turn'], skipAll: false }, next: 'draw' },
    { name: SKIP_ALL_HINTS_LABEL, stored: { dismissed: [], skipAll: true }, next: null },
  ])('$name stores its patch and shows the next hint', async ({ name, stored, next }) => {
    const { user, props } = setup();
    await user.click(within(hint()).getByRole('button', { name }));
    expect([readHintState(), shown()]).toEqual([stored, next]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it.each<{ label: string; props: Partial<ArenaScreenProps>; run: (user: User) => Promise<void>; dismissed: readonly HintId[] }>([
    { label: 'Draw', props: {}, run: draw, dismissed: ['your-turn', 'draw'] },
    { label: 'Draw with an attack incoming', props: { view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { pendingEffects: [onMe('basic-attack')] } }) }, run: draw, dismissed: ['your-turn', 'incoming', 'draw'] },
    { label: 'a sent Use (Tax)', props: { view: HELD }, run: useTax, dismissed: ['hand', 'your-turn'] },
    { label: 'a hand card sheet', props: { view: HELD }, run: (user) => openCard(user, 'Hand', 'tax'), dismissed: ['hand'] },
    { label: 'a special card sheet', props: { view: HELD }, run: (user) => openCard(user, 'Specials', 'super-regeneration'), dismissed: ['specials'] },
    { label: 'a Spy+ seat sheet', props: { view: makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'spy-plus' } }) }, run: (user) => tapSeat(user, 'p1'), dismissed: ['hidden-kit'] },
    { label: 'an unspied seat sheet', props: {}, run: (user) => tapSeat(user, 'p1'), dismissed: [] },
  ])('$label dismisses $dismissed', async ({ props, run, dismissed }) => {
    const { user } = setup(props);
    await run(user);
    expect(readHintState().dismissed).toEqual(dismissed);
  });

  it.each([['incoming', 'basic-attack'], ['incoming-thief', 'thief']] as const)('%s is dismissed when its threat leaves the view, never on mount', (id, cardId) => {
    const { props, rerender } = setup({ view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { pendingEffects: [onMe(cardId)] } }) });
    expect([shown(), readHintState().dismissed]).toEqual([id, []]);
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} view={{ ...props.view, pendingEffects: [] }} />);
    expect(readHintState().dismissed).toEqual([id]);
  });

  it('a sent reward Confirm dismisses reward through the commit (confirm-reward, R11)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup({ subChoice: REWARD });
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: REWARD_TITLE })).getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm rewards' }));
    expect(props.onResolveSubChoice).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(readHintState().dismissed).toEqual(['reward']);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'Skip tutorial', props: () => ({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(1) }), flag: SKIP_TUTORIAL_ARIA_LABEL, chrome: LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME.skipTutorial },
    { label: 'Return home from the finished tutorial', props: () => ({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(30), readOnly: true }), flag: RETURN_HOME_ARIA_LABEL, chrome: LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME.returnHome },
  ])('$label leaves once and never writes the hint blob (L46-03)', async ({ props, flag, chrome }) => {
    const rendered = setup(props());
    await rendered.user.click(within(region('top-bar')).getByRole('button', { name: flag }));
    await rendered.user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: chrome.title })).getByRole('button', { name: chrome.confirm }));
    expect(rendered.props.onLeave).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(localStorage.getItem(HINTS_STORAGE_KEY)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('a sent tutorial step writes no hint dismissal (L46-03)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup({ view: makeTutorialArenaView(1) });
    await useTax(user);
    expect(props.onPlayCard).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(localStorage.getItem(HINTS_STORAGE_KEY)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

That is 57 tests (30 + 1 + 2; 5 + 3; 3 + 7 + 2 + 1 + 2 + 1).

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail.** `pnpm vitest run apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-hint-causes.test.ts apps/client/src/help/hint-overlay.render.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-hints.test.tsx`. Expected:
  - the causes test fails to resolve `./arena-hint-causes`;
  - the render test fails on the missing `[data-hint-layer]` / `[data-hint-slot]`, the V6 `green` / `orange` variants and the fixed `z-[105]` root;
  - the arena tests fail on `shown()`: the arena mounts no hint. Ten pass already and stay as pins: the five "no hint" rows, the Spy+ row, the unspied-seat row and the three L46-03 tests.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - **`arena-hint-causes.ts`.** Two exhaustive switches, each with `default: return unreachable(x)`. Header cites §6.12, A.9.2 and V2's call sites.
    - `hintCauseForIntent`: `drawCard` → `'draw'`. `playCard`, `playMultipleAttacks`, `sellCard`, `upgradeCard`, `buyCard`, `buyUpgradePoint`, `sellUpgradePoint`, `buySpecialCard`, `buyPoolCard`, `clearSpy` → `'playing-intent'`. `deactivatePersistent`, `activateDuplication` → `null` (V2 sent no cause). `resolveSubChoice` → `intent.payload.kind === 'elimination-reward' ? 'confirm-reward' : null`.
    - `hintCauseForCardSheet`: `hand` → `'inspect-hand'`, `special` → `'inspect-special'`, the six others → `null`.
  - **`use-arena-hints.ts`**, `useArenaHints({ view, readOnly, pov, isMyTurn, seats, subChoice })`. Header cites §6.12, §6.3 / Q66 and §6.7. Hooks, unconditional and in this order: `const [hintState, setHintState] = useState(readHintState)`, the threat `useState`, `useSheetStackState()`.
    - `tutorial = view.playKind === 'tutorial'`. `hasIncomingAttack` and `hasIncomingThief` are the `.size > 0` of `incomingAttackTargetingYouIds` / `incomingThiefTargetingYouIds(view.pendingEffects, view.you)`.
    - **Threat edge, render-phase** (the `use-pov-elimination.ts` pattern, never an effect): `const [seenThreats, setSeenThreats] = useState({ attack: hasIncomingAttack, thief: hasIncomingThief })`. When either flag differs: `setSeenThreats(next)`. Then, unless `tutorial`, collect `hintIdsDismissedBy('incoming-cleared', NONE)` when the attack went true → false, and `'incoming-thief-cleared'` the same way for the thief (`NONE = { hasIncomingAttack: false, hasIncomingThief: false }`). Non-empty → `setHintState((prev) => applyHintPatch(prev, { ids }))`. The seed is the mount value, so nothing is dismissed on mount.
    - `hintId = selectHint({ playKind: view.playKind, readOnly, selfEliminated: pov !== 'seated', isMyTurn: isMyTurn && !readOnly && pov === 'seated', skipAll: hintState.skipAll, dismissed: hintState.dismissed, hasIncomingAttack, hasIncomingThief, hasRewardChoice: subChoice?.kind === 'elimination-reward', hasUnspiedLivingOpponent: seats.some((seat) => seat.kitHidden) })`. `selectHint` runs `shouldShowFirstGameHints` on `playKind`, so a tutorial room never selects a hint. Keep that gate (K6): L75-04's `TutorialCalloutLayer` shares `Z_INDEX.hint` and relies on it.
    - `dialogOpen`: any `entries` item with `mode === 'read-only'` (every arena sheet, Dialog and the menu; a targeting-collapsed card sheet too) **or** any `mode === 'blocking' && minimized`, **or** `subChoice !== null && !(hintId === 'reward' && subChoice.kind === 'elimination-reward')`. This is V2's `hintDialogOpen` (K1, A.9.1).
    - `note(cause)`: returns at once when `tutorial` (V2). Otherwise `setHintState((prev) => applyHintPatch(prev, { ids: hintIdsDismissedBy(cause, { hasIncomingAttack, hasIncomingThief }) }))`.
    - `onGotIt`: when `hintId !== null`, `applyHintPatch(prev, { ids: [hintId] })`. `onSkipAll`: `applyHintPatch(prev, { skipAll: true })`. Both go through the updater form, so `applyHintPatch` writes the storage.
  - **`help/hint-overlay.tsx`.** Same props. The imports become `Button` from `../ui/primitives/button` and `CoachPanel` from `../ui/coach/coach-panel`; it imports nothing from `design/`.
    - `const box = useVisualViewportBox(); const mode = useViewportMode();`. While `dialogOpen`, return `null` (as today).
    - Otherwise `createPortal(…, document.body)`:
      - the root `<div data-hint-layer="" className="pointer-events-none fixed" style={{ zIndex: Z_INDEX.hint, top: box.offsetTop, left: box.offsetLeft, width: box.width, height: box.height }}>`;
      - the slot `<div ref={slotRef} data-hint-slot="" className={`pointer-events-auto absolute ${mode === 'landscape-compact' ? 'w-[min(22rem,calc(100vw-1rem))]' : 'w-[min(15.5rem,calc(100vw-1rem))]'}`} style={{ bottom: '7.5rem', left: '0.5rem' }}>`;
      - inside it, `CoachPanel key={hintId} compact zone="first-game-hint" extraAttrs={{ 'data-hint-id': hintId }} onHide={onGotIt} hideAriaLabel={HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL}`, with the footer `<Button variant="primary" className="flex-1" data-hint-ack="got-it" onClick={onGotIt}>{GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL}</Button><Button variant="quiet" className="flex-1" data-hint-ack="skip-all" onClick={onSkipAll}>{SKIP_ALL_HINTS_LABEL}</Button>` (the L72-17 gallery look).
    - Placement is the existing layout effect, with deps `[hintId, dialogOpen, mode]`:
      - find the anchor with a private `firstVisibleAnchor(selector)`: the first of `document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(selector)` whose `getBoundingClientRect()` has a non-zero width and height, else `null`. It is private to `help/hint-overlay.tsx`, because `help/` never imports `fx/` (K2); the render test's zero-size twin pins the duplicate-anchor case;
      - read `const vv = readVisualViewportBox()` at call time, and pass `placeHintCard({ top: rect.top - vv.offsetTop, left: rect.left - vv.offsetLeft, width: rect.width, height: rect.height }, { width: slot.offsetWidth, height: slot.offsetHeight }, { width: vv.width, height: vv.height }, { prefer: hintPlacePrefer(hintId) })`;
      - write `slot.style.top/left` in px and `bottom = 'auto'`. No anchor → today's fallback (`top: ''`, `left: 0.5rem`, `bottom: 7.5rem`);
      - keep today's re-place triggers: a `ResizeObserver` on the anchor, and `resize` on `window` and `visualViewport`.

  - **`arena-screen.tsx`.**

L75-04 already placed the tutorial hook below `strip` and moved the commit statement right under it, so nothing else moves and no existing hook is reordered.

The arena passes \`seats.some((seat) => seat.kitHidden)\` (L75-06); the old table keeps its pre-Q66 value until L75-10 deletes it. */`.

```
## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] First-game hints on the arena: the Q66 input, the minimized reward sheet, V7 chrome (L75-06)

Source: technical spec v7 §4.2, §6.3, §6.7, §6.12, §12.1; Appendix A Q66, `first-game-hint-host`,
`table-first-game-hints`; the Lot 75 plan A.9 and ruling R11.

- **Base-Spied seats count as kit hidden** (§6.3, Q66). The arena passes `seats.some((seat) =>
  seat.kitHidden)` as `hasUnspiedLivingOpponent`, so the selector input and the `hidden-kit` anchor
  agree: a base Spy shows resources, not the kit. The input keeps its name, because renaming it would
  edit `screens/table.tsx` (R14). Under `?ui=classic` the old table keeps its pre-Q66 value until
  L75-10 deletes it.
- **The reward hint hides while the reward sheet is minimized** (R11, planner's pick). The Minimize
  bar carries no anchor; the hint returns on restore. This needs no `ui/` change.
- **Hidden under sheets.** The card hides while any read-only sheet-stack entry is up (every arena
  sheet, Dialog and the menu), a minimized blocking sheet, or a non-reward sub-choice (V2's
  `hintDialogOpen`). The reject toast is not a sheet and paints above the card (`Z_INDEX.toast`), so
  it does not hide it.
- **Chrome.** `HintOverlay` renders the `ui/coach` compact panel on `Z_INDEX.hint` and imports nothing
  from `design/`. The V6 compact-chrome source assertion (classification row 91) is retired,
  superseded by §6.12 and §4.2. Under `?ui=classic` the old table shows the V7 card too until the flip
  (L75-09, same PR; contract open question 12).
- **Anchor lookup.** The card takes the first `[data-hint-anchor]` with a non-zero rect, so a mounted
  but hidden twin never takes it. The overlay has its own lookup: `help/` imports no `fx/` code.

---
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Each id renders next to its anchor in all three layout modes: `$id sits next to its anchor in $mode` (30 rows), plus `keeps one hint card across mode changes …` (RF1's invariant).
- A table whose only opponent is base-Spied still selects `hidden-kit`; a Spy+ one does not: `a lone %s opponent off turn selects %s (§6.3, Q66)`.
- Opening the Shop sheet hides the card and dismisses `shop`: `opening the Shop hides the card and dismisses shop; …`.
- The reward hint stays over the reward sheet: `keeps the reward hint over the reward sheet, hides it while minimized …` (and `a sent reward Confirm dismisses reward …`).
- Tutorial rooms and the finished board show no hint: `$label shows no first-game hint`.
- Skipping or finishing the tutorial never writes `skipAll` (L46-03): `$label leaves once and never writes the hint blob (L46-03)` and `a sent tutorial step writes no hint dismissal (L46-03)`.
- The `hint-overlay.tsx` assertions in `hint-overlay.test.ts` become behavior tests: `hint-overlay.render.test.tsx`, with classification rows 90–93.
- Watch point, the landscape-compact host width: the `WIDTH.wide` class check in the matrix and crossing tests.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6. Pixel placement on real devices is part of the L75-12 gate (first-game hints).

**Decisions:**
- **Designer question: footer look.** Got it is `primary` and Skip all `quiet`, as in the L72-17 gallery (V2 used green and orange). Default: the gallery.
- **Designer question: the reward hint while minimized** (R11). Default: hide it, and show it again on restore. The alternative, an anchor on the bar, needs a `ui/` change.
- **Designer question: what does not hide the card.** It stays up beside the reject toast and the desktop log panel (neither is a sheet). Default: it stays.
- **The hint layer is shared (K6).** L75-04's `TutorialCalloutLayer` also portals on `Z_INDEX.hint`. That is safe only because `selectHint`'s `shouldShowFirstGameHints` gate keeps every hint out of a tutorial room, so this task keeps that gate (`a tutorial room shows no first-game hint` pins it).
- **Width units.** The watch point's `100vw - 1rem` literal is kept. The root is sized to the visual viewport, and `placeHintCard` clamps to that box, so a pinch-zoomed card cannot leave the screen.
- **A drag's pre-send card sheet counts as a hand card sheet** (`inspect-hand`): `openCard` is the one opener (V2 noted it on every own-card select).
- **New strings:** none. Reused: 'Got it', 'Skip all', 'Hide coach' and the `HINT_COPY` bodies.

  it('lists the five thieves and keeps the old truth table on every real card id', () => {
    expect([...INCOMING_THIEF_CARD_IDS]).toEqual(['thief', 'card-thief', 'spy-thief', 'upgrade-point-thief', 'attack-thief']);
    for (const id of [...ATTACK_CARD_IDS, ...ACTION_CARD_IDS, ...SPECIAL_CARD_IDS]) {

**Acceptance map:**
- Each of the five card ids yields its own body: `gives each of the five thieves its own body, …`.
- Only Thief mentions the 10-point cap: `mentions the 10-point cap only for Thief`.
- The copy map is typed over the five thief ids: `is typed over exactly the five thief ids; …` (`expectTypeOf`, plus the key set), and `lists the five thieves and keeps the old truth table on every real card id`.
- The arena shows the Spy Thief body for an incoming Spy Thief and the Thief body for an incoming Thief: `$label shows the $body body` (rows 1 and 2). Row 3 and `picks the earliest-queued thief on you, …` cover the R12 review line.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Designer question: the four new bodies** (Card Thief, Spy Thief, Upgrade Point Thief, Attack Thief), defaults as in Step 3. They are written from rules spec §3 and §5 and the server resolvers (`resolve-pending.ts`: a Card Thief takes a random hand or special card, or the chosen one under the thief's Spy; a Spy Thief takes every point and leaves a Spy+; an Upgrade Point Thief strips every upgrade; an Attack Thief takes one random shared attack card, or all of them when upgraded).
- **One body for both tiers.** The map is keyed by card id (A.9.4), so each body is true for the base and the upgraded card. The Attack Thief body names both tiers.
- **No 'Shield' in any body**, extending the existing `incoming-thief` pin. An upgraded Shield blocks only the base Thief, and the Thief body already left it out.
- **New strings:** the four new bodies above. The Thief body is L75-01's.

## Appendix A — Interface contract, Lot 75 Part 1 (L75-01 … L75-08, L75-13)

Every Part 1 task builds against these names and types. A task may add private helpers. It never renames or reshapes a Lot 74 export (Lot 74 plan Appendix A.1–A.9). Every change below is additive unless a ruling (R1–R16, `controller-rulings-p1.md`; K1–K13 and S1–S8, `consistency-rulings-p1.md`) says otherwise. A new optional prop or parameter has a default that keeps today's behavior. This appendix states the final shapes after the consistency pass (2026-11-03): where it names a K or S ruling, that ruling has amended it.

Sources:
- the code on `claude/v7-lot-75` at `c6941bec`, checked for this contract, with the maps `map-layout-fx.md` and `map-tutorial-hints.md`;

- the Lot 75 briefs and the `docs/backlog_v7.md` Lot 75 rows.

When this appendix and a brief disagree, this appendix wins. When this appendix and the code disagree on a fact, the planner reports it under "Contract issues" and does not deviate silently.
