### A.2 Layout mode API (L75-02)

There is no new hook. The mode is the one L73 already computes, read once per render:

```ts
// arena-screen.tsx (ArenaScreenInner), unchanged calls
const layoutBox = useArenaLayoutBox(arenaRef);   // { viewport: visual box, arena: measured region }
const seatsModel = seats(view, layoutBox);       // layout.mode === layoutModeFor(layoutBox.viewport)
const mode: LayoutMode = seatsModel.layout.mode; // the shell's single source (R1)
// root: <main data-layout-mode={mode} …>   'portrait' | 'landscape-compact' | 'desktop'
```

- **Thresholds (R1), from `ui/viewport-mode.ts` `viewportModeFor`:** visible height < 500 → `landscape-compact`; otherwise width > height → `desktop`; otherwise `portrait`. Pinned boxes:
  - 1440×900, 1024×600 → desktop;
  - 768×1024, 360×740, 375×812, 390×844 → portrait;
  - 844×390, 667×375, 740×360 → landscape-compact.

  The L75-02 brief's "width ≥ 1024 and height ≥ 700" is stale: L75-02's task text says so.
- `screens/arena/` never calls `useViewportMode()`. A child that needs the mode takes `mode: LayoutMode` as a prop (`CentralPlate` already does). `ui/` components keep their own `useViewportMode()` (`CoachPanel`, `Carousel`, and `HintOverlay` from L75-06). It is the same rule, and those modes agree with the arena even while ruling 7's keyboard hold is on: `useViewportMode()` holds its mode through `useTextEntryFocused()` (`ui/use-visual-viewport-box.ts`, L75-02, U1 ruling), while `useVisualViewportBox()` stays live so sheets and toasts follow the keyboard. `Sheet` is the exception: it resolves `placement="auto"` from the live `useVisualViewportBox()` box (`ui/primitives/sheet.tsx:196, :232`), not from `useViewportMode()`. That is harmless: `auto` is `bottom` in both portrait and landscape-compact, so the placement can differ from the held mode only on desktop.
- The visible box includes the on-screen keyboard. A portrait phone typing in a sheet can drop under 500 px. Under open-question ruling 7 (K12), `useArenaLayoutBox` holds the viewport box it had while a text `input`, a `textarea` or a contenteditable element has focus, so the mode holds; it recomputes on blur, with the draft kept (L75-02; Review Focus 1).

### A.3 Regions per mode (L75-02, L75-03)

**Invariant (binding):**
- The six region elements stay **direct children of `<main>`, in `ARENA_REGIONS` order, in every mode**. Each keeps its `data-arena-region` / `data-zone` / `data-player-id` and every anchor of Lot 74 A.3.
- The mode changes only `<main>`'s grid template, each region's classes, and the region's `data-arena-row`. There is no mode-conditional wrapper element, so a mode change remounts no region subtree. The owner-local menu, leave confirm, Unspy picker, drafts and latches survive a rotation (Review Focus 1).
- The `arena` region stays `relative min-h-0 overflow-hidden`, unpadded, and measured. The seat, threat and plate layers stay its direct absolute children.
- What a region *renders inside* may differ by mode. The `ticker` region renders `LogTicker` in portrait and landscape-compact, and `LogPanel` on desktop.

```ts
// arena-regions.ts — additive (L75-02)
export const ARENA_ROWS = ['top', 'ticker', 'arena', 'strip', 'hand', 'dock', 'side'] as const;
export type ArenaRow = (typeof ARENA_ROWS)[number];
/** The grid row a region sits in for `mode` (the table below). Pure; node-tested. */
export function arenaRowOf(region: ArenaRegion, mode: LayoutMode): ArenaRow;
/** Spread on a region root beside `arenaRegionAttributes(region)`. */
export function arenaRowAttributes(region: ArenaRegion, mode: LayoutMode): { readonly 'data-arena-row': ArenaRow };
/** `<main>`'s grid for `mode` (inline style: Tailwind cannot spell quoted area templates). Each region is one named area, its region id (K11). */
export interface ArenaGridStyle { readonly gridTemplateAreas: string; readonly gridTemplateColumns: string; readonly gridTemplateRows: string }
export function arenaGridStyle(mode: LayoutMode): ArenaGridStyle;
/** `{ gridArea: region }`, spread as a region root's `style` (K11). */
export function arenaAreaStyle(region: ArenaRegion): { readonly gridArea: ArenaRegion };
```

| Region | portrait | landscape-compact | desktop |
|---|---|---|---|
| `top-bar` | `top` | `top` | `top` (spans all three columns) |
| `ticker` | `ticker` | `top` (beside the top bar) | `side` (the right-hand log column, under the top bar) |
| `arena` | `arena` (`1fr`) | `arena` (`1fr`) | `arena` (`1fr`, left column) |
| `self-strip` | `strip` | `hand` (compact strip, left of the hand) | `strip` (left column) |
| `hand` | `hand` | `hand` | `hand` (left column) |
| `dock` | `dock` | `hand` (right of the hand) | `hand` (right of the hand) |

- **Desktop has three columns (K12):** hand, dock and the log. The top bar spans all three; the arena and the self strip span the first two; the `ticker` region is the third column under the top bar (L75-02's `arenaGridStyle('desktop')`: areas `"top-bar top-bar top-bar" "arena arena ticker" "self-strip self-strip ticker" "hand dock ticker"`).
- "The dock sits in the hand row" (L75-02, L75-03 acceptance) is `region('dock').dataset.arenaRow === region('hand').dataset.arenaRow`. "Hand and dock in one row" is the same check.
- The DOM order of regions is `ARENA_REGIONS` in every mode. The L74-01 test that pins it stays green under the portrait default (R2).
- The landscape-compact placement of the `ticker` and `self-strip` regions is a default (open question 6); the desktop column split is also a default (open question 5). The planner may tune classes and sizes, never the `data-arena-row` values above, unless the controller rules otherwise.

### A.4 Desktop (L75-02; spec §6.4, R3)

#### A.4.1 The log panel

```ts
// log-panel.tsx (L75-02)
export interface LogPanelProps {
  /** ArenaScreenInner state `logPanelExpanded`, default true (open question 5). */
  expanded: boolean;
  view: PlayingStateView;
  /** `log(view)`: the same `logModel` the ticker and the sheet read. */
  model: LogModel;
  onToggle: () => void;
  onOpenCard: (source: CardSheetSource) => void;
  /** L75-04: `data-tutorial-highlight="action-log"` on the toggle. L75-02 does not pre-add it (S6). */
  tutorialHighlight?: boolean;
}
export function LogPanel(props: LogPanelProps): ReactElement;

// log-rounds.tsx, use-log-follow.ts (L75-02; K11)
export interface LogRoundsProps {
  view: PlayingStateView; model: LogModel; onOpenCard: (source: CardSheetSource) => void;
  /** 'sheet-body' keeps `-top-3` (the Sheet body's 12 px padding, G27); 'panel' sticks at `top-0`. */
  inset: 'sheet-body' | 'panel';
}
export function LogRounds(props: LogRoundsProps): ReactElement;
/** While `active`: open on the newest line, follow within 48 px, refresh `lastTop` on every stick. */
export function useLogFollow(active: boolean, content: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>, scrollerOf: (content: HTMLElement) => HTMLElement | null): void;
```

- **The toggle is always rendered**, collapsed or expanded: a 44 px `<button type="button" data-zone="log-collapsed" aria-expanded={expanded}>` named `ACTION_LOG_OPEN_LABEL` ('Action log'). It is the rendered log element the acceptance requires when collapsed. It is also the FX log endpoint while collapsed and the tour's `action-log` element.
- **Expanded**, the visible scrollport, and never its taller content, carries `data-zone="action-log-panel"` (A.7). The rounds render as in `LogSheet`, with sticky `Round N` headers at `top-0` instead of `-top-3`, outside a Sheet body.
  - The scroll-follow rule is `followLogScroll` / `LOG_STICK_THRESHOLD_PX` with the panel's own scroller. L75-02 adds the missing `lastTop` call-site test (carry-forward).
- **Collapsed**, nothing in the panel carries `data-zone="action-log-panel"`: the list unmounts (L75-02). Independently, `queryActionLogAnchor` skips any zero-rect `action-log-panel` (L75-08, A.7.2), such as a depth-hidden log sheet. The two together are Review Focus 5.
- **The log sheet on desktop.** Menu → Action log expands the panel and opens no sheet. On the other modes it opens `{ kind: 'log' }` as today. A log sheet already open when the mode turns desktop stays open until it is dismissed (open question 5).
- `logPanelExpanded` lives in `ArenaScreenInner` (`useState(true)`), so it survives a mode change. It is not a sheet and never calls `openArenaSheet`, so it never ends targeting.

#### A.4.2 Centered dialogs

- `CardSheet`, `SeatSheet` and `ShopSheet` pass `placement="auto"` (`ui/primitives/sheet-placement.ts`). `'auto'` is `center` exactly when `viewportModeFor(visual box) === 'desktop'`.
- **The `Sheet` primitive already has `'center'` and `'auto'`, so R3 needs no `ui/` change.** The panel exposes `data-sheet-placement` for tests.
- The kit, log, chat, rules, menu and Unspy sheets keep `placement="bottom"`. Shared dialogs (leave confirm, the Deactivate confirm) keep `Dialog`'s own `'auto'` (§7.7).
- A card sheet collapsed into the targeting bar uses the bar, which the primitive places at the bottom in every placement.

#### A.4.3 Seats at their per-mode box

```ts
// seat-geometry.ts — additive
/** The drawn seat box for `mode`: SEAT_BOX[mode] (portrait 76×92, landscape-compact 76×92 from L75-03 (was 76×84, K11), desktop 120×136). */
export function seatRenderBox(mode: LayoutMode): Size;
/** `mode` defaults to 'portrait' (today's behavior). */
export function seatRect(center: SeatPlacement, mode?: LayoutMode): Rect;
// SEAT_RENDER_BOX stays exported, === seatRenderBox('portrait') (seat-geometry.test.ts:11 pins it).
// seatCenters(layout, arena): grid spacing uses seatRenderBox(layout.mode).
```

- `OpponentSeatProps` gains `mode?: LayoutMode` (default `'portrait'`). The seat root carries `data-seat-size={mode}`, and its anatomy is sized per mode. The portrait anatomy and its tests (decisions.md "V7 opponent seat anatomy in the 76×92 box") stay unchanged. `ArenaSeats` passes `model.layout.mode`, and `threat-geometry.ts` passes `layout.mode` to `seatRect`.
- `ArenaSeats` root `data-seat-layout` is `'arc' | 'band' | 'grid'`. `'band'` = `layout.kind === 'arc' && layout.mode === 'landscape-compact'`, the L75-03 acceptance's "band container". Portrait and desktop arcs stay `'arc'`.
- `SEAT_BOX.desktop`, `PLATE_BOX.desktop` and `SEAT_BOX['landscape-compact']` are starting values (§14.3). A task may tune them with their `seat-layout.test.ts` cases and a `decisions.md` line. The portrait boxes stay (R5 compacts the band instead).
- **L75-03 tunes `SEAT_BOX['landscape-compact']` to 76×92** (K11): the band draws the portrait anatomy unclipped. Tests in every domain read `SEAT_BOX[mode]` or `seatRenderBox(mode)`, never a literal box size.

### A.5 Landscape-compact (L75-03; spec §5.4, §6.4, R4)

#### A.5.1 The height-bound hand size helper

```ts
// ui/card/hand-card-size.ts (L75-03; DESIGN.md › Card › Size contract, "hand size rule")
export const HAND_CARD_MIN_W_PX = 64;   // the Card `hand` clamp floor (card.tsx)
export const HAND_CARD_MAX_W_PX = 120;  // the Card `hand` clamp ceiling
/** Card height / width (`ui/card/card.tsx`: height = --card-w × 1.5). Additive (K11); L75-13 reads it. */
export const HAND_CARD_ASPECT = 1.5;
/**
 * Landscape-compact: the hand card width (px) bound to the visible height (§5.4), so card
 * (1.5× tall) + the fan's vertical padding fit the hand row. Clamped to [MIN, MAX]. Pure.
 */
export function heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(visibleHeightPx: number): number;
```

- `HandFanProps` gains `readonly cardWidthPx?: number`. When set, the fan root's inline style sets `--hand-card-w: ${px}px` instead of `clamp(64px,19vw,120px)`. `--card-w` stays the Card's only resize path. When absent, nothing changes.
- The fan keeps the ≥ 44 px visible strip, readable chips, and horizontal scroll inside the hand only (§6.1 item 4). `STRIP_WIDTH` already reads `100cqi`, so a narrower hand region re-budgets the overlap.
- The shell passes `cardWidthPx` as follows:
  - `mode === 'landscape-compact'` → `heightBoundHandCardWidthPx(layoutBox.viewport.height)`;
  - portrait with `band === 'compact'` → `COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX` (A.6);
  - otherwise absent.

#### A.5.2 Compact strip

- `SelfStripProps` gains `readonly compact?: boolean` (default false): one row, a smaller kit portrait, the same anchors (`data-zone="resources"` with its four `data-resource-kind`, `data-hint-anchor="resources"`, a `data-zone="kit-portrait"` descendant). The strip root carries `data-self-strip`: `'full'` or `'compact'`.
- L75-03 uses it in landscape-compact, and L75-13 reuses it for the portrait compact band. L75-03 adds no `TopBar` / `LogTicker` `compact?`; Check 1 adds one in a fix commit only if the 667×375 arena leaves the plate on the band.
- **The anchors L75-04 relies on, in every mode (S5).** B's tests pin them (L75-03's `arena-landscape.test.tsx` › 'keeps every first-game hint anchor and the tutorial anchors …'), and C's tests rely on them:
  - `[data-arena-region="ticker"] [data-zone="log-collapsed"]` is the log element: the `LogTicker` button, or the desktop `LogPanel` toggle named "Action log" with `aria-expanded`;
  - the `self-strip` region holds `[data-zone="resources"]` and the `KitPortrait` root `[data-zone="kit-portrait"]`, in the compact strip too;
  - `[data-top-bar="turn"]`, the flag `IconButton` and `data-turn-clock` stay;
  - `data-self-strip` is `'full'` or `'compact'`.

#### A.5.3 A mode change cancels a drag

- `useHandDrag` measures its drop rects once when the drag lifts. A drag that is pressed or dragging when `mode` changes ends like `pointercancel`: it springs back and nothing is sent.
- `HandDragInput` gains `mode?: LayoutMode`; when it is absent, nothing changes. The shell passes `mode`. Review Focus 1 tests it.
- L75-13 adds `band?: BottomBand` and keys the cancel on `${mode}:${band}`: a flip of the portrait bottom band (A.6) changes the arena height, the strip and the hand card width under a lifted card, so it ends the gesture too. `arena-bottom-band.test.tsx` tests it.

### A.6 The compact bottom band (L75-13; R5, designer 2026-11-03 "Bas plus court")

```ts
// bottom-band.ts (L75-13, pure; node-tested)
export type BottomBand = 'full' | 'compact';
/** The hand card width in the compact portrait band (below the 64 px floor is allowed; strip ≥ 44 px and chips stay readable). */
export const COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX: number;   // 52
/** The plate footprint the overlap check uses: landing frame + one incoming badge row (open question 2). */
export const PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX: number;   // 122 = 74 + 4 + 44
/** The slack a compact band needs before it returns to full (S1): the modelled savings can miss about 44 px when an upgraded-shield pill adds a fourth pill row to the full strip at 360 px. */
export const BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX: number;   // 48
/** Height the compact band gives back to the arena at this viewport width (hand + strip): the strip part is the measured constant 160 − 61 px (pill wrapping depends on font metrics), the hand part from the Card clamp; rounded up. */
export function bottomBandSavingsPx(viewportWidth: number): number;
/** The plate rect in arena pixels for `mode` (bottom-anchored as CentralPlate draws it), `height` tall. */
export function plateRect(mode: LayoutMode, arena: Size, height: number): Rect;
/**
 * The largest overlap (px) of any seat rect (`seatCenters` + `seatRect(…, layout.mode)`) that meets the plate rect
 * horizontally: max(0, seatRect.bottom − plateRect.top); 0 = clear. Monotone in the arena height (the plate is pinned
 * to the arena's bottom edge, so a seat drawn below the arena is covered too): it never falls as the arena shrinks.
 */
export function plateOverlapPx(layout: SeatLayout, arena: Size, plateHeight: number): number;
/**
 * Portrait only (any other mode → 'full'); an unmeasured arena (width 0, as before the first measure) → 'full'.
 * 'compact' when the judged arena would let the plate cover a seat:
 * plateOverlapPx(layoutSeats(view, judged), judged.arena, PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX) > 0, where
 * judged.arena.height = box.arena.height − (current === 'compact' ? bottomBandSavingsPx(width) + BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX : 0).
 * Full → compact keeps the plain threshold; compact → full needs the full band's arena to clear the
 * plate with BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX to spare (S1). Stable under its own effect (Review Focus 4).
 */
export function bottomBandFor(view: PlayingStateView, box: LayoutBox, current: BottomBand): BottomBand;
```

- **Shell wiring:** `const [band, setBand] = useState<BottomBand>('full')`, then `const nextBand = bottomBandFor(view, layoutBox, band)`, with `setBand(nextBand)` when it differs. That is the render-phase adjust, like the targeting end, never an effect. The root carries `data-bottom-band={band}`.
  - `compact` passes `compact` to `SelfStrip` (A.5.2) and `cardWidthPx={COMPACT_HAND_CARD_W_PX}` to `HandFan`.
  - The dock keeps its controls. Q87-1 / Q87-2 were not answered, so the dock stays as it is (R16).
- **The hand's scroll-edge fades (Q87-4, designer 2026-11-03):** `HandFan` takes `useHiddenEdges(region)` from `hand-scroll-cue.ts` and draws `<span aria-hidden data-scroll-cue="start" | "end">` over the scroller's left edge (once scrolled away from the start) and right edge (while more lies beyond the end). Static, decorative, in every mode.
- "Seat count" in R5 is the drawn opponents, `seatsModel.seats.length`: 7 for a seated 8-seat table (4 + 3 grid), 8 for an 8-seat walk-in (4 + 4).
- **Acceptance geometry (pure test):** at 360×740 and 375×812, with 8 seats (seated and walk-in) and with a 2-seat Duplicator dock, `plateOverlapPx(…) === 0` for the compact band's arena. The arena heights come from A.10 `ARENA_TEST_BOXES` (open question 3). The L75-12 gate re-checks them in the browser.
- **No flip-flop (S1):** a pure test sweeps a full-band arena within `BAND_HYSTERESIS_PX` of the threshold, with the modelled savings exact and 44 px short, and asserts the band never changes after its first decision.
- **A band flip cancels a lifted drag (A.5.3):** `useHandDrag` takes `band` and keys its cancel on `${mode}:${band}`; the shell passes it. A lifted card's drop rects were measured at lift, and the flip changes the arena height and the hand card width under it.
- **No flap below one seat row:** two pure tests: a 2-seat view at 360 px with a 164 px compact arena stays `compact` (its judged arena is −8 px), and an 8-seat 360×0 measured arena goes `compact`, from `full` and from `compact`.
- The `decisions.md` entry ("Bas plus court": the bottom band compacts; seat and plate boxes unchanged) is written by the controller in the plan commit, from Domain B's proposed text (S8). L75-13 cites it and appends the measured numbers in a new dated entry.

### A.7 The FX anchor contract (L75-08; R6)

#### A.7.1 Endpoints (every one must resolve to a rendered, non-zero arena element in each mode)

| Endpoint (fx helper) | Selector | portrait / landscape-compact | desktop |
|---|---|---|---|
| seat (`queryPlayerFxAnchor`, `rectForPlayerSeat`, `measureTargetingCue`, elimination beat) | `[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id=ID]` (portrait-first: `… [data-zone="opponent-portrait"]`) | `OpponentSeat` root / its avatar | same, at the desktop box |
| seat resource (`rectForPlayerResource`) | `…opponent-seat[data-player-id=ID] [data-resource-kind=K]` | seat `<li data-resource-kind>` | same |
| POV (`queryPlayerFxAnchor(you)`) | `[data-zone="private-zone"][data-player-id=you]` | self-strip region (compact strip too) | same |
| self resource (`measureTokenFlyout`, no playerId) | `[data-zone="resources"] [data-resource-kind=K]` | self-strip `Pill` | same |
| kit portrait (`rectForPlayerSeatOrPortrait`) | `…private-zone [data-zone="kit-portrait"]` | `KitPortrait` root in the strip | same |
| hand instance (`measurePlayCardGhost`, `measureSellCardFlyout`) | `[data-instance-id=I]` | the hand `Card` root (first in DOM order; sheets portal later) | same |
| hand / specials group (buy flyouts, own seat) | `[data-zone="hand"]`, `[data-zone="specials"]` | `HandFan` groups | same |
| plate (ghost target; `queryPendingFlashAnchor` fallback) | `[data-plate-landing]` then `[data-zone="pending"]` | `CentralPlate` | same |
| pending id (resolution flash) | `[data-pending-id=E]` (first visible), else the plate | arrow label / plate badge | same |
| log (`queryActionLogAnchor`) | first **visible** of `[data-zone="action-log-panel"]`, `[data-zone="log-collapsed"]` | ticker button (`log-collapsed`) | expanded: panel scrollport (`action-log-panel`); collapsed: the toggle (`log-collapsed`) |
| dock (reward pulse) | `[data-zone="dock"]` | dock region | dock region (in the hand row) |
| felt (last fallback) | `[data-zone="felt"]` | arena region | same |

`TableBannerHost` reads no anchor: the test only checks that it renders in all three modes on the visual-viewport box. The `fx/` overlay stays `fixed inset-0 z-[110]`, above every `Z_INDEX` layer including `hint` and `coach`.

In portrait and landscape-compact, an open log **sheet**'s `action-log-panel` stays on its content `div`, which can be taller than the sheet body's scrollport, so a chip may aim off-screen on a long log. Part 1 does not change it: it is a gate item (K12).

#### A.7.2 Changes in `fx/`

```ts
// fx/play-flyout.ts — additive helper, behavior fixes
/** For each selector in order, every match in document order: the first element whose client rect is non-zero; else null. */
export function queryFirstVisible(selectors: readonly string[]): Element | null;
/** Now `queryFirstVisible([panel, collapsed])`: a mounted, zero-size panel never swallows the fallback (R6). */
export function queryActionLogAnchor(): Element | null;
/** Same name, new target: a 48×72 rect centred on the plate (`[data-plate-landing]` ?? `[data-zone="pending"]`), else the felt, else null — no plate/hand midpoint (L75-08 brief, R6). */
export function measureFeltCenterRect(): DomRectLite | null;
```

- **Retired** (R6, with one `decisions.md` entry citing spec §6.1, §6.4, §6.9 and §6.12; open question 4):
  - `queryOpponentsCollapsedAnchor` and the collapsed tail of `queryPlayerFxAnchor`;
  - the `incoming-pending` / `incoming-collapsed` branch of `queryIncomingAnchor`, which is re-pointed to the plate (`[data-zone="pending"]`);
  - `measureIncomingCollapsedCue` and its second `targetingCue` in `use-table-fx-triggers.ts:351-362`;
  - the constants `OPPONENTS_COLLAPSED_ZONE`, `INCOMING_PENDING_ZONE` and `INCOMING_COLLAPSED_ZONE` in `ui/fx-anchors.ts`. `ACTION_LOG_PANEL_ZONE` and `LOG_COLLAPSED_ZONE` stay.

  None of them fires on the arena today, so no arena FX is lost. In the same commit, `play-flyout.test.ts:57-73` (classification row :89) retires, and its owner moves from L75-10 to L75-08, recorded in `docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md`. `use-table-fx-triggers.test.ts:83-90` is **rewritten, not deleted** (K12): it keeps the seat cue covered and keeps the `incoming-collapsed` anchor to prove it gets no cue.
- **Moved to behavior tests** (R6):
  - the `play-flyout.test.ts` L51-13 source part (row :88) becomes a `measureDeckCardFlyout` / `measurePlayCardGhost` behavior test (plate target, 48×72, null with no anchor, never the log);
  - `fx/table-fx-overlay.play-flyout.test.ts` (row :256) becomes an overlay component test.

  The pure L51-09 / L51-14 assertions stay as they are (rows :40, :42).

### A.8 Tutorial on the arena (L75-04, L75-05; R7–R9)

#### A.8.1 Layers (L75-04)

```ts
// ui/layers.ts (L75-04; DESIGN.md › Overlays)
export const Z_INDEX = { dragGhost: 40, sheet: 50, sheetBar: 80, hint: 84, coach: 86, banner: 90, toast: 95 } as const;
// ui/primitives/sheet-stack.ts sheetZIndex: Minimize bars clamp at `Z_INDEX.hint - 1` (was `banner - 1`),
// so no bar ever paints over a hint or the coach. sheet-stack.test.ts:64 updates its Z_INDEX literal.
```

- The order is read-only < blocking < bars < **hint < coach** < banner < toast < `fx/` overlay (`z-[110]`). The banner and toast order is kept (R7; open-question ruling 1).
- **`Z_INDEX.hint` is shared (K6).** L75-04's `TutorialCalloutLayer` portals on it as well as L75-06's `HintOverlay`. That is safe because `shouldShowFirstGameHints` (inside `selectHint`) keeps every first-game hint out of a tutorial room, and the callouts draw only in one. L75-06 must keep that gate; both tasks state it.
- The coach and hint hosts portal to `document.body` on a `position: fixed` box sized to `useVisualViewportBox()`, as `TableBannerHost` does. Sheets make only other sheets inert, so a host outside every sheet is never inert. The `CoachPanel` stays non-modal (no `role`, no `aria-modal`, no focus taken), so the sheet focus trap keeps Tab and Esc (Review Focus 3).

#### A.8.2 The tutorial hook (state lives in the arena component, R7)

```ts
// use-arena-tutorial.ts (L75-04 with the lock half of `allows`; L75-05 adds the script half, K4)
export interface ArenaTutorialInput {
  readonly view: PlayingStateView;
  readonly readOnly: boolean;
  /** `view.currentTurnPlayerId === view.you` (the idle timer runs only then). */
  readonly isMyTurn: boolean;
  /** The POV is eliminated (no idle timer). */
  readonly selfEliminated: boolean;
}
export interface ArenaCoachView {
  readonly index: number;
  /** Tour / Look copy, else the follow-coach copy after a veto, else the script copy; 'Play' after 20 s idle (`tutorialCoachTitle`). */
  readonly title: string;
  readonly body: string;
  /** `${'tour:N' | 'look' | 'script'}|${tutorialCoachMessageKey(index, title, body)}`, as V2. */
  readonly messageKey: string;
  /** `isTutorialCoachOpen(messageKey, dismissedKey)`: new copy reopens it. */
  readonly open: boolean;
  /** Tour steps only: the Got it footer. */
  readonly showAck: boolean;
}
export interface ArenaTutorial {
  /** `view.playKind === 'tutorial' && !readOnly && view.tutorialIndex !== null`. */
  readonly active: boolean;
  readonly index: number | null;
  readonly tourStep: number;
  readonly tourActive: boolean;     // isTutorialTourActive(index, tourStep)
  readonly lookPending: boolean;    // isTutorialLookPending(index, portraitInspected)
  /** tourActive || lookPending: no drag, Shop / Unspy opens ignored, hand taps withheld, every send vetoed (§6.6.3). */
  readonly locksTable: boolean;
  readonly tourHighlight: TutorialTourHighlight | null;
  /** `tutorialHighlightAt(index)`, or null while locked or inactive. */
  readonly scriptHighlight: TutorialHighlight;
  /** Null when inactive, which includes the finished board. */
  readonly coach: ArenaCoachView | null;
  /**
   * The tutorial half of `allow` (A.8.4). Always true when inactive. L75-04 ships the lock half,
   * `() => !(active && locksTable)`; L75-05 adds the script half and the follow-coach `illegalIndex`.
   * The shape does not change between the two tasks (K4).
   */
  readonly allows: (intent: ArenaIntent) => boolean;
  readonly onAck: () => void;        // tourStep + 1, sends nothing
  readonly onHideCoach: () => void;  // dismissedKey = messageKey
  readonly onShowCoach: () => void;  // dismissedKey = null
  /** The shell calls it before opening a seat sheet; it clears the Look gate for a seat whose kit is visible (`!kitHidden`). Latched per mount. */
  readonly onSeatOpened: (playerId: string) => void;
}
export function useArenaTutorial(input: ArenaTutorialInput): ArenaTutorial;
```

- State lives in this hook, inside `ArenaScreenInner`: `tourStep` (0), `portraitInspected` (false), `idleIndex` (null), `dismissedKey` (null), from L75-04; `illegalIndex` (null) from L75-05, which is the only task that sets it (K4).
- The idle timer is `window.setTimeout(…, TUTORIAL_IDLE_MS)`, under V2's conditions (`table.tsx:539-566`). The pure helpers stay in `view-model/tutorial-coach-copy.ts`.
- **Hook order in `ArenaScreenInner` (S3).** Nothing reorders an existing hook; the new hooks and the commit statement end in this order:
  1. `useArenaLayoutBox(arenaRef)`;
  2. L75-02's `useState(true)` (`logPanelExpanded`), then L75-13's `useState<BottomBand>('full')` and its render-phase adjust;
  3. `seats(...)`, `threats(...)`, `const strip = selfStrip(view, ctx)` (not hooks);
  4. L75-04's `const tutorial = useArenaTutorial(...)`, directly below `strip`;
  5. L75-06's `const hints = useArenaHints(...)`, directly below the tutorial call;
  6. the `const commit = createArenaCommit(props, …)` statement. L75-04 moves it, with its comment lines, from above the `seenReject` state to directly below the tutorial call, and L75-06 inserts the hint hook between the two. It is not a hook, and nothing between its old and new places uses `commit`;
  7. `usePovElimination(...)` and every later existing hook, unchanged.

#### A.8.3 The coach host (L75-04)

```ts
// tutorial-coach-host.tsx (L75-04)
export interface TutorialCoachHostProps {
  coach: ArenaCoachView;   // mounted only while `tutorial.coach !== null && tutorial.coach.open`
  onHide: () => void;
  onAck: () => void;       // footer shown only when coach.showAck
}
export function TutorialCoachHost(props: TutorialCoachHostProps): ReactElement;
```

- The host renders `CoachPanel` from `ui/coach/coach-panel.tsx` (L72-17) with these props:
  - `zone="tutorial-coach"`, `key={messageKey}`, `hideAriaLabel={HIDE_COACH_ARIA_LABEL}`;
  - `extraAttrs={{ 'data-tutorial-index': String(index), 'data-tutorial-coach-open': 'true' }}`;
  - when `showAck`, a footer `Button` carrying `data-tutorial-ack="got-it"` and the label `GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL`.
- The host root portals with `zIndex: Z_INDEX.coach`. No floating reopen toggle: it reopens from the menu (§6.1).
- **The host measures its own slot (K8).** It reads the `[data-arena-region="arena"]` rect (a `ResizeObserver` on that element, the `use-arena-layout-box.ts` pattern) and places the panel at its top-left, 8 px in. `TutorialCoachHostProps` does not change.
- `TopBarProps.onShowCoach?: () => void` is passed through to `MenuSheetProps.onShowCoach?: () => void`. When it is present, the menu shows an entry between How to play and Action log, with `Icon name="coach"` and the label `OPEN_COACH_ARIA_LABEL` ('Show coach'). The entry closes the menu first. The shell passes it only while `tutorial.coach !== null && !tutorial.coach.open`.

#### A.8.4 The `allow` composition (L75-04 composes it with the lock half; L75-05 fills the script half; R8, K4)

```ts
const tutorial = useArenaTutorial({ view, readOnly, isMyTurn: view.currentTurnPlayerId === view.you, selfEliminated: povEliminated });
// L75-06 inserts `const hints = useArenaHints(...)` here (A.8.2 hook order, S3).
const commit = createArenaCommit(props, {
  allow: (intent) => !reconnecting && tutorial.allows(intent),  // L75-04; never replaces the reconnect lock
  onSent: (intent, context) => { /* L74-17 own FX, L74-25 lastSent; L75-06 adds the hint cause (A.9.2) */ },
});
```

In L75-04, `tutorial.allows` is the lock half only: `!(active && locksTable)`. From L75-05, `tutorial.allows(intent)` works as follows:
- inactive → `true`;
- `locksTable` → `false`, silently (no illegal index, V2);
- `index === null` or `!tutorialSendAllowed(index, tutorialIntentOf(intent, view))` → `false`, and the illegal index is set to `index`. The coach then shows `ILLEGAL_ACTION_COPY['tutorial-follow-coach']` ('Tutorial step' / 'This tutorial step asks for a different action.');
- otherwise → `true`, and the illegal index is cleared.

`resolveSubChoice` is the one exception (open-question ruling 8): after the `locksTable` check it returns `true` without consulting `tutorialIntentOf`, and it never sets the illegal index (K5). Own-intent FX keep flying only from `onSent`, after the allow.

Carry-forward fixes, both in L75-05:
- **The card-sheet Deactivate confirm closes only on a sent commit.** The sheet-internal `send` returns the commit's boolean, and the confirm's `setConfirming(false)` runs only on `true`.
- **The `arena-sheets.ts` early return.** `openArenaSheet` keeps an already-top Shop's old `tab`. No Part 1 caller passes `tab`: the Shop opens on `model.initialTab`, which already reads the tutorial highlight (`view-model/shop.ts` `highlightOf`). So the reducer stays unchanged in Part 1; L75-05 records why in its decisions.md entry.

#### A.8.5 The legal-kind mapping (L75-05; pure, node-tested, exhaustive `never` switch)

```ts
// tutorial-gate.ts (L75-05)
/** Every ArenaIntent → the V2 TutorialSendIntent `tutorialSendAllowed` reads. A held card is looked up in view.self.hand, then view.self.specialCards. */
export function tutorialIntentOf(intent: ArenaIntent, view: PlayingStateView): TutorialSendIntent;
```

| `ArenaIntent.kind` | `TutorialSendIntent` | Human legal kinds it can match |
|---|---|---|
| `drawCard` | `{ kind: 'draw' }` | `draw` (index 0) |
| `playCard` | `{ kind: 'playCard', cardId, isUpgraded }` of the held instance; not held → `{ kind: 'other' }` | `play-tax`, `play-basic` (not upgraded), `play-basic-upgraded`, `play-spy`, `play-shield`, `play-super-regeneration`, `play-absorber` |
| `sellCard` | `{ kind: 'sellCard', cardId }`; not held → `other` | `sell-shield` |
| `upgradeCard` | `{ kind: 'upgradeCard', cardId }` of the held instance; an in-play target → `other` | `upgrade-spy`, `upgrade-basic` |
| `buyCard` | `{ kind: 'buyCard', cardId }` | `buy-absorber` |
| `buyUpgradePoint` | `{ kind: 'buyUpgradePoint' }` | `buy-upgrade-point` |
| `playMultipleAttacks` | `{ kind: 'playMultipleAttacks' }` | none (always vetoed) |
| `sellUpgradePoint` | `{ kind: 'sellUpgradePoint' }` | none |
| `buySpecialCard` | `{ kind: 'buySpecialCard' }` | none |
| `buyPoolCard` | `{ kind: 'other' }` (explicit case) | none |
| `clearSpy` | `{ kind: 'other' }` (explicit case) | none |
| `deactivatePersistent` | `{ kind: 'other' }` (explicit case; V2 never gated it) | none |
| `activateDuplication` | `{ kind: 'other' }` (explicit case) | none |
| `resolveSubChoice` | `{ kind: 'other' }` (explicit case, for the exhaustive switch only). `allows` never consults it: it returns `!locksTable` for a sub-choice answer, which passes outside the locks and is held silently during them (open-question ruling 8; K5; V2 never gated it, and the server gates only `TurnAction`s) | — |

`TutorialSendIntent` and `matchesLegalKind` stay unchanged. Every "none" row matches the server, which rejects every such `TurnAction` with `tutorial-follow-coach` (`game-room.ts` `rejectTutorialIllegalAction`). Each of the 14 kinds gets one row in the table-driven gate test.

#### A.8.6 Highlight attributes (L75-04 tour and Look; L75-05 script)

One attribute: `data-tutorial-highlight="<id>"`. It is present only while that highlight is active; there is never an empty value.

```ts
// use-arena-tutorial.ts (L75-04) — exported type
export type ArenaTutorialHighlightId =
  | TutorialTourHighlight                       // your-zone hand specials resources incoming shop opponent action-log timer kit flag
  | 'draw' | 'shop' | TutorialCardActionSpotlight  // use upgrade sell
  | 'shop-upgrade-point' | 'shop-absorber' | 'shop-absorber-buy' | 'opponent-portrait'
  | CardId;                                     // a spotlit hand card carries its cardId (V2)
```

| Id | Element (arena) | How it gets there |
|---|---|---|
| `your-zone` | the `self-strip`, `hand` and `dock` region elements (three) | the shell spreads it on the region roots |
| `hand` / `specials` | `[data-zone="hand"]` / `[data-zone="specials"]` groups | `HandFanProps.tutorial?: { readonly group: 'hand' \| 'specials' \| null; readonly instanceIds: readonly string[] }` |
| `<cardId>` (script) | each spotlit hand / special `Card` (`tutorialSpotlightInstanceIds(scriptHighlight, [...hand, ...specials])`) | `HandFanProps.tutorial.instanceIds` → `Card dataAttributes` |
| `resources` / `kit` | `[data-zone="resources"]` / the self-strip `KitPortrait` root (`[data-zone="kit-portrait"]`), in the full and the compact strip | `SelfStripProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'resources' \| 'kit' \| null`; `kit` reaches the root through `KitPortraitProps.dataAttributes?` (K7, below) |
| `incoming` | the plate `[data-zone="pending"]` | `CentralPlateProps.tutorialHighlight?: boolean` |
| `shop` (tour, and script `shop-*` until the Shop opens) / `draw` | the dock Shop / Draw buttons | `DockProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'draw' \| 'shop' \| null` (`tutorialEconomySpotlight(scriptHighlight, shopOpen)` or the tour) |
| `opponent` | every `[data-zone="opponent-seat"]` root | `ArenaSeatsProps.tutorial?: { readonly seats: boolean; readonly portraitIds: readonly string[] }`, fed to each seat as the private `OpponentSeatProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'opponent' \| 'opponent-portrait'` (K7) |
| `opponent-portrait` (Look) | `[data-zone="opponent-portrait"]` of each Spy+-revealed seat while `lookPending` | `ArenaSeatsProps.tutorial.portraitIds` → `OpponentSeatProps.tutorialHighlight` (K7) |
| `action-log` | the `[data-zone="log-collapsed"]` button: `LogTicker` (portrait, landscape-compact), `LogPanel` toggle (desktop) | `tutorialHighlight?: boolean` on both |
| `timer` / `flag` | `[data-top-bar="turn"]` (banner + clock) / the flag `IconButton` | `TopBarProps.tutorialHighlight?: 'timer' \| 'flag' \| null` |
| `use` / `upgrade` / `sell` | `[data-card-action="use" \| "upgrade" \| "sell"]` in the card sheet | `CardSheetProps.tutorialAction?: TutorialCardActionSpotlight` (`tutorialCardActionSpotlight(scriptHighlight)`) |
| `shop-upgrade-point` / `shop-absorber` / `shop-absorber-buy` | `[data-shop-action="buy-upgrade-point"]` / `[data-shop-tile="absorber"]` / the footer `[data-shop-action="buy-card"]` while Absorber is selected | `ShopSheet` reads `model.highlight` (already in `ShopModel`); no new prop. Highlighted, not preselected (§6.8) |

- The guide callout is orange with its arrow outside the highlight, static under reduced motion. It must not be clipped by the hand `ScrollArea`, the `overflow-hidden` arena region or a sheet body (V2 needed `[data-zone="card-band"]:has([data-tutorial-highlight]) { overflow: visible }`). **L75-04 builds a measured portal layer** and records it in DESIGN.md:

  ```ts
  // ui/coach/tutorial-callout-geometry.ts (L75-04, pure)
  export interface CalloutRect { readonly left: number; readonly top: number; readonly width: number; readonly height: number }
  export type CalloutArrowSide = 'top' | 'bottom';
  export const CALLOUT_RING_GAP_PX = 4;
  export const CALLOUT_ARROW_W_PX = 20;
  export const CALLOUT_ARROW_H_PX = 12;
  export interface CalloutPlacement { readonly ring: CalloutRect; readonly side: CalloutArrowSide; readonly arrow: CalloutRect }
  export function calloutPlacement(target: CalloutRect, viewport: { readonly width: number; readonly height: number }): CalloutPlacement;
  // ui/coach/tutorial-callout.tsx (L75-04): portals on Z_INDEX.hint (K6), draws the live sheet's own targets, else the table's
  export interface TutorialCalloutLayerProps { readonly active: boolean }
  export function TutorialCalloutLayer(props: TutorialCalloutLayerProps): ReactElement | null;
  ```
- **`KitPortrait` gains `dataAttributes?` (K7):** ``KitPortraitProps.dataAttributes?: Readonly<Record<`data-${string}`, string>>``, spread on the root before its own anchors, so `data-zone="kit-portrait"` and `data-kit-*` win. It is a `ui/` change: a DESIGN.md › Kit line and an A.11 entry.
- **Who adds the tutorial props (S6).** L75-04 adds every optional prop in this table and in A.8.7 to Domain B's components: `LogPanel.tutorialHighlight?`, `LogTicker.tutorialHighlight?`, `TopBar.onShowCoach?` / `tutorialHighlight?`, `MenuSheet.onShowCoach?`, `SelfStrip.tutorialHighlight?`, `HandFan.tutorial?`, `Dock.tutorialHighlight?` / `locked?`, `ArenaSeats.tutorial?`, `CentralPlate.tutorialHighlight?`, `HandDragInput.locked?` beside L75-03's `mode?`, and `canStartHandDrag(…, locked = false)`. Domain B pre-adds none. Every one of those components exists under that name at `c6941bec`, except `LogPanel` (L75-02) and the compact strip (L75-03), which come earlier in the R13 order.
- Real incoming threats keep the plate's existing `data-tone` rings with no arrow. "The incoming Basic at index 3 is ringed in attack tone" needs only a test.

#### A.8.7 Locks while `locksTable` (L75-04; §6.6.3)

- `HandDragInput.locked?: boolean` (default false) makes `canStartHandDrag(actions, targetingActive, locked = false)` return false. The third parameter is additive and optional.
- `DockProps.locked?: boolean` (default false): Shop and Unspy opens do nothing. The shell's `onOpenShop` also returns early.
- Hand and special card taps open no sheet (V2 parity; open question 9). Seat taps stay live, because they are the Look gate's exit. Every send is vetoed through `allow` anyway.

#### A.8.8 Routing (L75-04; R9)

- `ui-switch.ts` `resolveTableUi` returns `input.preference` for every `playKind`, so the tutorial follows `?ui=next`. The default stays `classic`.
- `App.tsx` changes only its header comment (line 8). `ui-switch.test.ts:65` flips to "tutorial rooms follow the preference". `App.arena.test.tsx` gains a tutorial case: `UI_SWITCH_STORAGE_KEY = 'next'` mounts `[data-arena="next"]` for a tutorial view, and the finished tutorial board mounts the arena with no coach.

#### A.8.9 Dead helpers (L75-05; R8)

- Delete `tutorialIncomingThreatIds` and `isTutorialIncomingThreatCard`, and drop the then-unused `isAttackCardId` import. In `tutorial-coach-copy.test.ts`, remove only the `tutorialIncomingThreatIds` import at :23 and the case at L238–253 (K13).
- **Keep `tutorialShopSpotlight`**: `view-model/shop.ts:144` uses it, so the brief's removal is wrong. Its import at `tutorial-coach-copy.test.ts:26` and its assertions stay (K13).

### A.9 First-game hints on the arena (L75-06, L75-07; R11, R12)

#### A.9.1 The hint hook (L75-06)

```ts
// use-arena-hints.ts (L75-06; L75-07 adds `body`)
export interface ArenaHintsInput {
  readonly view: PlayingStateView;
  readonly readOnly: boolean;
  /** `strip.pov`: only 'seated' shows hints (V2's selfEliminated || walk-in gate). */
  readonly pov: PovMode;
  /** `strip.isActive`. */
  readonly isMyTurn: boolean;
  /** `seatsModel.seats`. */
  readonly seats: readonly SeatModel[];
  readonly subChoice: SubChoiceRequiredPayload | null;
}
export interface ArenaHints {
  /** `selectHint(...)`, or null. Null in a tutorial room and on the finished board (`shouldShowFirstGameHints`). */
  readonly hintId: HintId | null;
  /** L75-07: the earliest-queued incoming thief's body, present only when hintId === 'incoming-thief'. */
  readonly body?: string;
  /**
   * Hidden, with V2's `hintDialogOpen` semantics (K1): any `read-only` sheet-stack entry is up (every
   * arena sheet, Dialogs and the menu), or any minimized `blocking` entry, or a sub-choice, unless the
   * shown hint is `reward` over an `elimination-reward` prompt. The expanded reward sheet's own
   * blocking entry therefore does not hide the reward hint.
   */
  readonly dialogOpen: boolean;
  /** applyHintPatch(prev, { ids: hintIdsDismissedBy(cause, ctx) }); a no-op in a tutorial room (V2). */
  readonly note: (cause: HintDismissCause) => void;
  readonly onGotIt: () => void;
  readonly onSkipAll: () => void;
}
export function useArenaHints(input: ArenaHintsInput): ArenaHints;
```

- **Position:** directly below `useArenaTutorial(...)` and above the `createArenaCommit(...)` statement (A.8.2 hook order, S3).
- **The tutorial gate stays (K6):** `hintId` comes from `selectHint`, whose `shouldShowFirstGameHints` keeps hints out of a tutorial room; `Z_INDEX.hint` is shared with the tutorial callouts (A.8.1).
- **Selector input (R11, Q66).** `hasUnspiedLivingOpponent = seats.some((seat) => seat.kitHidden)`. `kitHidden` is true for unspied and base-Spied seats and false for Spy+ and eliminated ones.
  - **The input keeps its name.** Renaming it would force a `screens/table.tsx` edit that R14 does not allow. L75-06 updates its doc comment in `view-model/select-hint.ts` to the Q66 meaning, and the old table keeps its pre-Q66 value until L75-10 deletes it.
  - The other inputs follow `table.tsx:637-659`, with `selfEliminated = pov !== 'seated'`:
    - `hasIncomingAttack = incomingAttackTargetingYouIds(...).size > 0`;
    - `hasIncomingThief` is the same over `incomingThiefTargetingYouIds`;
    - `hasRewardChoice = subChoice?.kind === 'elimination-reward'`.
- **The reward hint while minimized (R11, the planner's pick).** The reward hint hides while the blocking reward sheet is minimized: `useSheetStackState().entries.some((entry) => entry.mode === 'blocking' && entry.minimized)` is part of `dialogOpen` (K1). This needs no `ui/` change, and the hint returns on restore. Over the expanded reward sheet the hint stays, anchored to the panel's `data-hint-anchor="reward"`.
- **Storage.** `readHintState` / `applyHintPatch` (`ui/help-storage.ts`) do not change. The arena's `LeaveConfirmSheet` imports no hint code, so Skip and finishing the tutorial never write `skipAll` (L46-03; a behavior test).

#### A.9.2 Causes (L75-06; pure, exhaustive)

```ts
// arena-hint-causes.ts (L75-06)
/** drawCard → 'draw'; playCard, playMultipleAttacks, buyUpgradePoint, sellUpgradePoint, buyCard, buySpecialCard, buyPoolCard, clearSpy, sellCard, upgradeCard → 'playing-intent'; deactivatePersistent, activateDuplication → null (V2); resolveSubChoice → payload.kind === 'elimination-reward' ? 'confirm-reward' : null. */
export function hintCauseForIntent(intent: ArenaIntent): HintDismissCause | null;
/** hand → 'inspect-hand'; special → 'inspect-special'; any other source → null. */
export function hintCauseForCardSheet(source: CardSheetSource): HintDismissCause | null;
```

**The final openers and `onSent` order in `ArenaScreenInner` (S2).** This is the one place it is written; L75-04 (the tutorial half) and L75-06 (the hint half) cite it:
- `openCard(source)`: the tutorial lock for a hand or special card (`if (tutorial.locksTable && (source.kind === 'hand' || source.kind === 'special')) return;`, L75-04) → `hints.note(cause)` when `hintCauseForCardSheet(source)` is not null (L75-06) → `openSheet({ kind: 'card', source })`. It has no tap guard.
- `onOpenShop()`: the tap guard (`if (tapGuard.guarded) return;`) → `if (tutorial.locksTable) return;` (L75-04) → `hints.note('open-shop')` (L75-06; R11: opening the Shop also hides the card, through `dialogOpen`) → `openSheet({ kind: 'shop' })`.
- `onOpenSeat(playerId)`: the tap guard → `tutorial.onSeatOpened(playerId)` (L75-04) → `hints.note('inspect-opponent')` for a seat with `kitHidden === false` (L75-06) → `openSheet({ kind: 'seat', playerId })`.
- The commit's `onSent(intent, context)`: the own-intent FX (L74-17) → `setLastSent(…)` (L74-25) → `hints.note(cause)` when `hintCauseForIntent(intent)` is not null (L75-06). L75-06 never touches `allow`.

The hook diffs the threat sets for `incoming-cleared` and `incoming-thief-cleared`, as `table.tsx:418-449` does (skipped in a tutorial). The reward Confirm reaches `'confirm-reward'` through `onSent`, the `noteHintCause('confirm-reward')` that the Lot 74 note left for L75-06.

#### A.9.3 `HintOverlay` restyled (L75-06; `help/`)

```ts
// help/hint-overlay.tsx — props additive
export interface HintOverlayProps {
  hintId: HintId;
  dialogOpen: boolean;
  onGotIt: () => void;
  onSkipAll: () => void;
  /** L75-07: replaces HINT_COPY[hintId].body (the per-card incoming-thief copy). */
  body?: string;
}
export function HintOverlay(props: HintOverlayProps): ReactElement | null;
```

- The overlay renders `ui/coach/coach-panel.tsx` `CoachPanel compact` with `zone="first-game-hint"`, `extraAttrs={{ 'data-hint-id': hintId }}` and `onHide={onGotIt}`.
- Its footer has two `ui/primitives/button` buttons, `data-hint-ack="got-it"` (`GOT_IT_ACTION_LABEL`) and `data-hint-ack="skip-all"` (`SKIP_ALL_HINTS_LABEL`). It has no `design/` import.
- The root portals to `document.body`, `position: fixed` on the visual-viewport box, with `zIndex: Z_INDEX.hint` and `pointer-events: none`; only the card takes presses.
- Placement keeps `placeHintCard` and `hintPlacePrefer`. The anchor is the first visible `[data-hint-anchor="<hintAnchorId(id)>"]`, found by a private `firstVisibleAnchor` in `help/hint-overlay.tsx`: `help/` never imports `fx/`, so it does not reuse `queryFirstVisible` (K2). `help/hint-overlay.render.test.tsx` pins the duplicate-anchor case (a zero-size twin before the visible one). The widths are `min(15.5rem, 100vw - 1rem)` in portrait and on desktop, and `min(22rem, 100vw - 1rem)` in landscape-compact (backlog L75-06 watch point).
- The old table keeps mounting the same component, so its hint card turns V7 under `?ui=classic` until the flip (open question 12).
- The `hint-overlay.tsx` source assertions in `hint-overlay.test.ts` become `help/hint-overlay.render.test.tsx` (classification rows :90, :91, :93; row :92 records the arena replacement of the `table.tsx` read). The `table.tsx`, `card-band.tsx` and `sub-choice-host.tsx` assertions stay in `hint-overlay.test.ts` for L75-10, so row :94 (the `card-band.tsx` `w-max` part) is not edited.

#### A.9.4 Per-card incoming-thief copy (L75-07; R12)

```ts
// view-model/incoming-threat-diff.ts — additive
export const INCOMING_THIEF_CARD_IDS = ['thief', 'card-thief', 'spy-thief', 'upgrade-point-thief', 'attack-thief'] as const satisfies readonly CardId[];
export type IncomingThiefCardId = (typeof INCOMING_THIEF_CARD_IDS)[number];
/** Now a type guard over the five ids (same truth table on every real card id). */
export function isIncomingThiefCardId(cardId: string): cardId is IncomingThiefCardId;
/** The non-persistent thief effect targeting `you` that resolves first: min `queuedAt`, ties in wire order (golden rule 3); null when none. */
export function earliestIncomingThief(pendingEffects: readonly PendingEffectView[], you: string): IncomingThiefCardId | null;

// help/hint-copy.ts — additive
export const INCOMING_THIEF_HINT_BODY: Readonly<Record<IncomingThiefCardId, string>>;  // only `thief` names the 10-point cap
// HINT_COPY['incoming-thief'] keeps title 'Incoming'; its body becomes INCOMING_THIEF_HINT_BODY.thief.
```

The hint id, title, selector order and dismissal stay unchanged. The arena passes `hints.body` to `HintOverlay body`.

### A.10 Test helpers (`test-view.ts`, test-only)

```ts
// L75-02
export interface ArenaLayoutStubOptions {

}

}

export function stubArenaLayout(viewport: Size, arena: Size, options?: ArenaLayoutStubOptions): ArenaLayoutStub;
// No `stubArenaViewport`: R2's global 390×844 default stands. L75-02's planner measured it at `c6941bec`

/** The 16 human indices: 0, then odd 1–29 (`TUTORIAL_STEPS` actor 'human'). */

/**
 * A 2-seat tutorial view at `index`: 'me' (Indestructible) vs bot 'p1' (Ghost), playKind 'tutorial',
```

`test-view.test.ts` stays a node file (K9). It pins that each `ARENA_TEST_BOXES` box is named after its mode (L75-02), and that the human-index list and the per-index tutorial view hold (L75-04). The live `data-layout-mode` flip under `stubArenaLayout` is pinned in `arena-desktop.test.tsx` (L75-02), because a node test cannot render.

### A.11 Shared files outside `screens/arena/` each task may edit

Every other path outside `apps/client/src/screens/arena/` and the task's docs stays untouched. A `ui/` or token change updates `DESIGN.md` in the same commit (§5.8). `docs/backlog_v7.md`, `docs/agent/frontend.md` and `docs/agent/decisions.md` are open to every task.

| File | Task | What |

### Open questions for the controller

Each item names the fact the maps and rulings leave open, why a planner cannot settle it alone, and the default the contract assumes.

1. **The `hint` and `coach` layer values.**
   - R7 says "above sheet and sheetBar, keeps the toast and banner order". That fits two orders: between `sheetBar` and `banner`, or above `toast` as in V6 (hint 105, coach 110).
   - **Default:** `hint: 84, coach: 86`, with the Minimize bars clamped at `hint - 1`. Transient banners ("You are being attacked") and reject toasts then paint over a 359 px-wide coach on a 375 phone, and the `fx/` overlay stays on top. This costs one `sheet-stack.ts` line and its test.
2. **The plate footprint in the R5 "no seat covered" check.**
   - The plate grows upward with content: 96 px empty; 122 px with one badge row; 170 px with chips, or with a second badge row at 3 or more incoming effects in a 120 px plate.
   - **Default:** `PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX = 122`, the landing frame plus one badge row. Taller plates rely on the existing press-through (L74-26 G-note). The 170 px case is a gate item.
3. **The R5 test inputs.**
   - The arena heights 214 (360×740), 282 (375×812) and 140 (the 2-seat Duplicator at 360) are measured or back-solved from the Lot 74 gate, not freshly measured.
   - **Default:** use them in `ARENA_TEST_BOXES`, and the controller re-measures them at L75-13's browser check. If they differ, L75-13 updates the boxes and the savings constants in a numbers note.
4. **R6 "retire" against R14 "Part 1 deletes nothing".**
   - L75-08 deletes the three collapsed-chrome fallbacks, which are `fx/` code, not old-table files. Under `?ui=classic` the old table loses only the fallbacks for chrome it collapses.
   - **Default:** retire them in L75-08 (A.7.2). Their two test rows move from owner L75-10 to L75-08, with the decisions.md entry.
5. **The desktop log column and state.** This is cross-domain: B builds it, C highlights the toggle, A anchors hints around it.
   - **Default:** a right column under a full-width top bar; expanded on mount; about 320 px wide when expanded; a 44 px rail with the toggle when collapsed.
   - Menu → Action log expands the panel, and opens no sheet on desktop. A log sheet already open when the mode turns desktop stays open until it is dismissed.
   - The default state and the width are a Designer question.
6. **The landscape-compact composition.**
   - R4 fixes only the band, the hand and the dock row.
   - **Default:** the `ticker` sits beside the top bar in the `top` row, and a compact self strip sits left of the hand in the `hand` row (A.3). This is a Designer question. It binds B's layout, C's `your-zone` / `action-log` marks and A's anchors.
7. **A keyboard-driven mode flip.**
   - With the phone keyboard up in a portrait sheet (chat, Feedback), the visible height can drop under 500 px, and the arena re-lays out as landscape-compact under the sheet.
   - **Default:** no freeze. Review Focus 1 pins that nothing is lost: sheets, drafts, focus and owner-local sheets survive, and a lifted drag cancels.
   - The alternative is to hold the mode while an editable element has focus. That is a rule on top of §6.4, so the controller should decide.
8. **`resolveSubChoice` in the tutorial gate.**
   - The server never gates sub-choice answers: they are not `TurnAction`s. No scripted step raises one, but vetoing an unexpected one would strand the player under a blocking sheet.
   - **Default:** allowed except during the tour and the Look gate, where it is silently vetoed. It never sets the illegal index.
9. **Hand taps during the tour and the Look gate.**
   - V2 withheld own-card taps, so no card sheet opens. The L75-04 acceptance "Use sends nothing during the tour" reads either way.
   - **Default:** V2 parity, so hand and special taps and drags do nothing. Seat taps stay live as the Look exit, and `allow` vetoes every send regardless.
10. **The R2 global portrait default against Lot 76's parallel tests.**
    - The 390×844 default also applies to every Lot 76 happy-dom test. One that silently relied on happy-dom's 1024×768 desktop (a `Dialog`, or a `Carousel gridAtDesktop`) would break when the branches meet.
    - **Default:** keep R2's global default. The controller tells the Lot 76 session. The Part 2 lot-end merge adds an explicit viewport stub to any such Lot 76 test and names it in the merge commit.
11. **R10 against R14 for `screens/whats-new-dialog.test.ts`, a Lot 76 (L76-05) file.**
    - **Default:** L75-01 edits only the item-count pin. If Lot 76 has already moved or rewritten that test on `dev` at task time, the pin is reconciled at the lot-end merge.
12. **Shared modules change the old table under `?ui=classic` before the flip.**
    - The V7 `HintOverlay` (L75-06) and the plate ghost target (L75-08) change the old table too, in look or target.
    - **Default:** accept it. Nothing ships before L75-09 in the same PR, and keeping V2 copies would mean editing or forking old-table code that R14 freezes.
13. **The browser-check process for the Part 1 acceptance lines** (L75-02, L75-03, L75-05 full tutorial at 375×812, L75-08 scenarios, L75-13 boxes).
    - **Default (superseded by ruling 13 below and S7):** the Lot 74 L74-12 / L74-17 precedent. The controller runs each check after the task review and records it in a separate `docs(arena): record the L75-NN browser check (L75-NN)` commit. The task flips `Done` with its tests green.
    - The Mirror auto-resolve re-check (carry-forward) joins L75-08's Mirror scenario.

### Controller rulings on the open questions (2026-11-03, binding)

1. **Layers.** The default stands: `hint: 84`, `coach: 86`, and Minimize bars clamp at `hint - 1`. Banners and toasts keep painting above the coach.
2. **Plate footprint.** `PLATE_FOOTPRINT_PX = 122`. The 170 px case goes to the browser check.
3. **Test boxes.** Use the back-solved boxes in `ARENA_TEST_BOXES`. The layout browser check re-measures them, and L75-13 updates them if they differ.
4. **Collapsed-chrome FX fallbacks.** L75-08 retires them. It is `fx/` code, not an old-table file, so R14 is not broken. Record a decisions.md entry and move the test rows to L75-08.
5. **Desktop log.** The default stands: right column, expanded on mount, about 320 px wide, a 44 px rail when collapsed. Menu → Action log expands the panel. The state and width were a Designer question: Designer 2026-11-03 kept them.
6. **Landscape-compact composition.** The default stands: the ticker sits beside the top bar, and a compact self strip sits left of the hand. It is a Designer question.
7. **Keyboard-driven mode flip.** Hold the current layout mode while an editable element (`input`, `textarea`, `[contenteditable]`) has focus inside the app. The mode recomputes on blur.
   - Why: a chat or Feedback keyboard must not re-lay the arena out as landscape-compact behind the sheet.
   - L75-02 owns it, with a test, and records it in decisions.md. (Resolved: L75-02 builds it in `useArenaLayoutBox`, holding the whole viewport box; L75-03's Review Focus 1 keyboard case relies on it, K12.)
   - Cost if wrong: a stale mode until blur.
8. **`resolveSubChoice` in the tutorial.** It is allowed, except during the tour and the Look gate, where it is silently vetoed and never sets the illegal index.
9. **Hand taps during the tour and the Look gate.** V2 parity: hand and special taps and drags do nothing. Seat taps stay live as the Look exit, and `allow` vetoes every send.
10. **Default portrait viewport.** R2's global portrait default stands. The controller tells the Lot 76 session now. The Part 2 lot-end merge stubs any Lot 76 test that relied on the old desktop default.
11. **`whats-new-dialog.test.ts`.** L75-01 edits only the item-count pin. The lot-end merge reconciles it.
12. **Shared modules.** The changes to the old table under `?ui=classic` are accepted. Nothing ships before L75-09, which is in the same PR.
13. **Browser checks.** They are batched, each run by a dedicated subagent on its own dev servers and recorded in its own `docs(arena): …` commit.
    - **Check 1 runs after L75-08.** It covers L75-02, L75-03, L75-13 and L75-08, plus the Mirror auto-resolve re-check. It records in one commit, `docs(arena): record browser check 1 (L75-08)`, whose body lists L75-02, L75-03, L75-13 and L75-08 (S7).
    - **Check 2 runs after L75-05.** It covers the full tutorial at 375×812, and records in `docs(arena): record browser check 2 (L75-05)` (S7).
    - Neither check commit is a task's own commit. Each task flips to `Done` once its tests are green. A defect a check finds is fixed in its own `fix(arena): … (L75-NN)` commit.

## Appendix B — Designer questions and new strings (Part 1)

Each question has a default the plan implements and tests. On 2026-11-03 the designer answered four of them (the desktop log panel, Q87-3, Q87-4 and Q87-5; each is marked "Designer 2026-11-03" below), and every other question ships with its default, Q87-1 and Q87-2 included. A later override changes only the owning task's code and tests. Q87-1 … Q87-5 carry PR #87's open questions (controller ruling R16).

### Domain A (L75-01, L75-06, L75-07)

#### Designer questions (with defaults)

- **L75-01, every changed string:** the seven `HINT_COPY` bodies, the five coach bodies, the tour Opponent body. Defaults: the Step 3 text.
- **L75-01, `your-turn`:** fix the run-on sentence? Default: unchanged (not in §10.2).
- **L75-01, What's new:** Before `Some tips and tutorial steps had typos or outdated Spy and reward text.`, After `Fixed some bugs.`
- **L75-06, footer look:** Got it `primary`, Skip all `quiet` (the gallery), or V2's green / orange? Default: the gallery.
- **L75-06, the minimized reward sheet:** hide the hint (default), or anchor it to the bar (a `ui/` change).
- **L75-06, cards that stay up:** the hint stays beside the reject toast and the open desktop log panel. Default: yes.
- **L75-07, the Card Thief, Spy Thief, Upgrade Point Thief and Attack Thief bodies:** defaults as in its Step 3.

#### New strings

- **L75-01:**
  - `HINT_COPY` bodies: `draw`, `hand`, `specials`, `resources`, `incoming-thief` (typo only), `hidden-kit`, `reward`;
  - `TUTORIAL_STEPS` bodies at indices 3, 11, 17, 27, 29;
  - the tour Opponent body;
  - the What's new Before line `Some tips and tutorial steps had typos or outdated Spy and reward text.`
- **L75-06:** none. It reuses 'Got it', 'Skip all' and 'Hide coach'.
- **L75-07:** `INCOMING_THIEF_HINT_BODY['card-thief']`, `['spy-thief']`, `['upgrade-point-thief']`, `['attack-thief']`.

### Domain B (L75-02, L75-03, L75-13, L75-08)

#### Designer questions (with defaults)

- The desktop log panel opens expanded, 320 px wide, with a 44 px rail (open question 5). **Designer 2026-11-03: kept.** (L75-02)
- The desktop Shop is a centred dialog at the Sheet's 28rem cap (V2: 48rem). Default: 28rem. (L75-02)
- The desktop seat anatomy: a 72×96 tile portrait and a 40 px resource column in the 120×136 box. Default: as planned. (L75-02)
- The landscape-compact composition: ticker beside the top bar; compact strip, hand and dock in one row (open question 6). Default: yes. (L75-03)
- A landscape hand card a quarter of the visible height tall, 65 px at 390 px. Default: yes. (L75-03)
- The compact strip hides the resource captions; they stay spoken and in `title`, and the full strip keeps them (L43-01). Default: hidden. (L75-03, L75-13)
- 52 px compact hand cards put the longest names at the 10 px floor, where they may wrap mid-word. Default: accept. (L75-13)
- Q87-1, dock reason lines; Q87-2, "Not your turn" twice. Default: no change (not answered; an answer lands in L75-13).
- Q87-3, the turn label at 375 px. **Designer 2026-11-03: keep the truncation.** (L75-02)
- Q87-4, the hand scroll cue. **Designer 2026-11-03: fade added.** A static fade on the hand's right edge when it overflows, and on its left edge once it is scrolled away from the start. (L75-13)
- Q87-5, the ground scanlines. **Designer 2026-11-03: keep them,** with a `repeating-stripes-gradient` detector ignore scoped to `ui/tokens.css`. (L75-02)

#### New strings

None. Reused: 'Action log' (`ACTION_LOG_OPEN_LABEL`, the desktop toggle and the panel header word), 'No actions yet', 'Round N' (`chatRoundLabel`), and the resource captions, which the compact pills speak.

### Domain C (L75-04, L75-05)

#### Designer questions (with defaults)

- **L75-04, callout colour:** `--threat-other` (#ffa53d), with no new token.
- **L75-04, callout chrome:** a 3 px ring 4 px out, and a 20×12 arrow outside the ring facing the screen centre. Opacity pulses under full motion only.
- **L75-04, coach slot:** the arena region's top-left, 8 px in, `min(22rem, 100% − 1rem)` wide, in every mode. In landscape-compact it covers the left band seats until hidden.
- **L75-05, card-sheet spotlight:** only on the spotlit card's sheet.

## Appendix C — Notes for Part 2 and brief history

### Part 1 notes (domain reports, after the consistency pass)

Contract issues are marked with the ruling that settled them (Appendix A; K and S rulings).

#### Domain A (L75-01, L75-06, L75-07)

##### Contract issues

1. **Resolved by K1.** **A.9.1 `dialogOpen`, "any sheet-stack entry is up".** The reward sub-choice is itself a sheet-stack entry (`mode: 'blocking'`), so read literally the reward hint could never show over its sheet, against R11 and the L75-06 acceptance. L75-06 implements V2's `hintDialogOpen`: the card hides for any `read-only` entry, any minimized `blocking` entry, or a sub-choice, unless the shown hint is `reward` over an `elimination-reward` prompt. Every arena sheet, Dialog and the menu are `read-only` entries, and the sub-choice sheet is the arena's only blocking one.
2. **Resolved by K2.** **A.9.3 "the first visible anchor" and A.7.2 `queryFirstVisible`.** A.7.2 does not say whether `queryFirstVisible` scans every match of a selector or only the first. The hint also needs the first non-zero element among duplicates (`hidden-kit` matches every hidden seat). Reusing it would also make `help/` import `fx/`. So L75-06 uses a private `firstVisibleAnchor` in `hint-overlay.tsx`, and its render test pins the duplicate case.
3. **Resolved by K3** (the parity file joins A.11, Notes text only: L75-01 points `table-first-game-hints` at §10.2; L75-06 updates `first-game-hint-host` and `table-tutorial-board-tour`). **`docs/technical_spec_v7_parity.md` is stale after L75-01 and L75-06.** `table-first-game-hints` still says "Copy is normative and locked … typos included". `first-game-hint-host` Notes say base-Spied seats count as spied, and `table-tutorial-board-tour` Notes say kits stay hidden "until Spy". §10.2 and Q66 supersede all three.
4. **No issue (kept for the record).** **No conflict found with the code** on the `HintOverlayProps` shape, `ArenaIntent` (14 kinds), `CardSheetSource` (8 kinds), the `ResolveSubChoicePayload.kind` discriminant, `SeatModel.kitHidden`, `strip.pov` / `strip.isActive`, `useSheetStackState()`, `SUB_CHOICE_COPY`, `LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME`, `MENU_LABEL`, `SHOP_ACTION_LABEL`, the five thief catalog names (`formatCardLabel`), or `isIncomingThiefCardId`'s callers (only its own module).

##### Cross-task notes

- **Copy constants (every domain, after L75-01).** Read hint and tutorial copy only through `HINT_COPY[id]`, `tutorialStepAt(n)?.coach`, `TUTORIAL_TOUR_STEPS`, `TUTORIAL_LOOK_COACH` and, after L75-07, `INCOMING_THIEF_HINT_BODY`. Never use a literal. The CoachPanel renders bodies through `parseCoachBody`, so compare rendered text against a `CoachBody` render of the same constant, not the raw string.
- **`coach.gallery.test.tsx` is untouched by L75-01.** Steps 1 and 21 and the Draw heading do not change.
- **From L75-06 on, a hint card appears in Classic arena renders.** (Settled by S4: header › Conventions.)
  - It shows on the POV's turn with fresh hint storage ('your-turn' first), and off turn whenever an opponent's kit is hidden ('hidden-kit').
  - It is an `aside` named by the hint title (e.g. 'Your turn', 'Draw', 'Shop', 'Elimination reward', the same words as some chrome) with the buttons 'Hide coach', 'Got it' and 'Skip all'. It is portaled to `document.body` (`[data-hint-layer]` > `[data-hint-slot]` > `[data-zone="first-game-hint"]`).
  - It is hidden under every read-only sheet and every non-reward sub-choice.
  - Tests written earlier (L75-02 … L75-05) must not query document-wide by those words or count every button or landmark in `document`. L75-06 runs the whole client suite and scopes any such query.
  - Tutorial views (L75-04 / L75-05) never show a hint.
- **Hint storage persists across the tests of one file** (happy-dom `localStorage`; settled by S4). From L75-06, Draw, plays and card-sheet opens write `card-battle.v6.hints`. A test that asserts a hint must call `resetHelpStorage()` (or `localStorage.clear()`) in `beforeEach`, as `arena-hints.test.tsx` and `top-bar.test.tsx` do.
- **Shell order (L75-04 → L75-06).** Settled by S2 and S3; the final order is written once in contract A.9.2 (openers) and A.8.2 (hooks).
  - In `onOpenShop`, `onOpenSeat` and `openCard`: the tap guard (none in `openCard`), then L75-04's tutorial lock check (or `tutorial.onSeatOpened`), then `hints.note(…)`, then the open.
  - L75-06 adds the hint cause to the commit's `onSent` after the own-intent FX and `setLastSent`, and never touches `allow`.

   - The full strip's height depends on pill wrapping, that is on font metrics. So the strip part of `bottomBandSavingsPx` is a constant: the L74-26 measured 160 px minus the compact 61 px.
   - It is exact at 360–375 px with no extra pill row, and an over-estimate from about 385 px.
   - An upgraded-shield pill can add a fourth pill row to the full strip at 360 px. The model then under-estimates the saving by about 44 px, and a band within 44 px under the threshold could flap. Check 1 probes it.

### Draft questions and their dispositions

Resolved in the 2026-10-21 backlog review unless listed under "Designer questions still open" in `docs/backlog_v7.md`; dispositions are recorded in the **Review** lines above and in the amended spec.

- §6.4 does not cover a landscape box that is at least 500 px tall but not desktop, for example 900×600 or a 1280×650 laptop window. None of the three modes matches it. Which mode applies? This blocks L75-02 and L75-03 (and possibly L73 `seats()`).
- §6.8, tutorial Shop: does 'a step that highlights a shop item opens the Shop' mean the Shop opens by itself? Today the player taps the highlighted Shop button, and the parity keep says 'The Shop is never auto-opened'. Separately, 'Cards with Absorber preselected' seems to contradict 'keeps select-then-Buy (shop-absorber → shop-absorber-buy)'. Should Absorber be preselected, which skips the select step, or only shown and highlighted on the Cards tab? L75-05 currently assumes the player taps Shop and selects Absorber.
- §10.2 says 'HINT_COPY and coach copy typos are fixed', but every listed typo is in `HINT_COPY`. Five `TUTORIAL_STEPS` coach bodies have a space before '!' ('Incoming attack !', 'A Spy is incoming now !', 'A Strong attack is incoming now !', '**3 lives** !', 'a Strong attack !'). L75-01 fixes them as the same typo class as 'opponent !'. Confirm, or strike them.
- Per-card `incoming-thief` hint (L75-07): when several thief effects target the POV at once, which card's copy should show? The proposal is the earliest-queued effect, the one that resolves first under golden rule 3.
- If L76 has not merged when L75-11 deletes `screens/table/`, the old light-theme lobby and kit picker would import the V7 `ChatLog` / `KitInspectDetails` rebuilds, which use dark tokens (§4.4 contrast concern). Is that acceptable, or should L75-11 keep those two files, or wait for L76?
- Desktop mode (L75-02): does the collapsible right log panel replace the §6.9 one-line ticker, or do both show? Do the table's card, shop and seat sheets stay bottom sheets on desktop, or become centered dialogs? §7.7 covers only the shared dialogs.
- What's new: if `lot-72` was promoted to `main` before L72 merged and no V7 entry is open when L75-01 lands, is the new entry id `lot-75`, following the lot-number convention? And should the opt-in L74 arena changes be listed only at the L75-09 flip, when they become the default?
- Q63: seeding `eliminationBeat` on mount is in §8.1 (L78). After the L75 flip, the arena finished board and every reconnect would replay past elimination beats until L78 lands. Should the seed move into L75-08?
- Q66 / §6.3 (base-Spied seats count as kit hidden for the `hidden-kit` hint) names no lot. L75-06 does it together with the hint re-anchor and the L75-01 copy fix. Confirm, unless L73 already changed the selector input.

#### L75-02 — Render the arena desktop layout

**Cx / risk:** M / Medium · **Depends on:** Lot 74 · **Spec:** §6.4, §6.1, §6.9, §5.8

Seats sit on the full ellipse around a larger plate, with the same turn order and POV bottom anchor as portrait (a walk-in spectator has no anchor), and the grid fallback is unchanged. The log is a collapsible right panel with the §6.9 grouped content, and the dock sits beside the hand fan. The top bar, self strip and every other region still render (§6.1), sheets keep the visual-viewport sizing (L53-02/07), and the page never scrolls. Uses `ui/` only. `DESIGN.md` and the `frontend.md` arena section gain the desktop rules.

The dock sits in the hand row. A 768×1024 box renders portrait. Browser check at 1440×900 with 2, 4 and 8 seats: no page scroll, nothing cropped. `pnpm verify` green.

Browser check on a landscape phone with 8 seats: no page scroll, hand faces uncropped with readable chips. `pnpm verify` green.

#### L75-04 — Host tutorial coach, callouts, tour and Look gate

**Cx / risk:** L / High · **Depends on:** L75-02, L75-03 · **Spec:** §6.12, §6.6.3, §6.6.5, §6.10, §6.1, §5.6, §14.3

**Files:** `apps/client/src/screens/arena/`, `apps/client/src/ui/`, `apps/client/src/view-model/tutorial-coach-copy.ts`, `docs/backlog_v7.md`, `DESIGN.md`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

Host the tutorial presentation on the arena (§6.12, Lot 45). The coach uses the `ui/` coach chrome: it is not a dialog and stays visible over open sheets. × hides it; it reopens from the top-bar menu with a coach icon distinct from How to play (Q45), reopens automatically on new copy, retitles to 'Play' after 20 s idle, and is hidden on the finished board. Guide callouts are orange with the arrow outside the highlight. Real incoming threats on the plate are ringed without an arrow, in `threatToneFor` tone (L51-07), and the rings are static under reduced motion. The 11-step board tour and the Look gate keep their copy verbatim. Record the §6.12 id map in `docs/backlog_v7.md`: `your-zone` = self strip + hand fan + dock · `hand` / `specials` = the two hand-fan groups · `resources` = self-strip resource row · `incoming` = plate · `shop` = dock Shop · `opponent` = every opponent seat · `action-log` = log ticker (on desktop, the log panel or its toggle) · `timer` = top-bar banner + clock · `kit` = self-strip kit portrait · `flag` = top-bar flag · Look `opponent-portrait` = the Spy+-revealed seat avatar, which opens the seat sheet. The tour and the Look gate lock the table (no drag, Shop and Unspy closed, sends blocked; §6.6.3). Tutorial seats hide the bot difficulty, the clock mode is `none`, and the flag stays Skip tutorial (Stay / Skip, not a forfeit, no Game over).

**Acceptance:** Behavior tests use a tutorial view at index 0 in portrait, landscape-compact and desktop boxes. Each tour step marks exactly its mapped element, which exists even when empty (Q60), and Got it advances without sending an intent. Drag, Use and Draw send nothing during the tour. At index 9 every send stays blocked until the revealed opponent's seat sheet opens, then never again in that mount. The coach hides, reopens from the menu and reopens on new copy, and fake timers retitle it 'Play' after 20 s. The incoming Basic at index 3 is ringed in attack tone. The finished tutorial board has no coach. The flag opens 'Skip the tutorial?' and Skip only leaves. `pnpm verify` green.

#### L75-05 — Gate every arena commit path in the tutorial

**Cx / risk:** L / High · **Depends on:** L75-04 · **Spec:** §6.12, §6.6.4, §6.6.3, §6.8, §4.4, §11
**Parity (Appendix A):** `table-tutorial-script-spotlight`, `shop-dialog`, `table-shop-double-click-buy`, `card-actions-dialog`, `table-fx-card-ghosts`
**Files:** `apps/client/src/screens/arena/`, `apps/client/src/view-model/tutorial-coach-copy.ts`, `apps/client/src/App.tsx`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

Map each `TUTORIAL_STEPS` highlight to its arena control: Draw; the scripted hand card, matched by card id and upgrade state; the card sheet's `use` / `upgrade` / `sell` buttons; and the Shop button until the Shop opens, then `shop-upgrade-point` on the Upgrade points tab, or `shop-absorber` on the Cards tab followed by `shop-absorber-buy` once it is selected (§6.8). Route every commit path through `allowTutorialSend` (§6.6.4): sheet buttons, target tap, drop on a seat, the plate or Sell, shop Buy and double-tap, Unspy and duplication. An illegal attempt sends nothing and shows the follow-coach copy, and own-play ghosts fire only when the send passes. Stop ignoring tutorial rooms under `?ui=next` (§4.4), and remove the dead helpers `tutorialIncomingThreatIds`, `isTutorialIncomingThreatCard` and `tutorialShopSpotlight` (§11, Q62). Update the `frontend.md` tutorial lines for the arena.

**Acceptance:** A table-driven behavior test covers every human index (0 and odd 1–29). At each index the highlighted control carries `data-tutorial-highlight` and the scripted intent reaches a mocked connection with the right payload. A wrong path (another card, Basic+ at index 3, buying Basic, a drop on Sell) sends nothing and shows 'This tutorial step asks for a different action.' A tutorial room under `?ui=next` renders the arena. The full tutorial (indices 0–30) is played to the win in the browser at 375×812. `pnpm verify` green.

#### L75-06 — Re-anchor first-game hints on the arena

**Cx / risk:** M / Medium · **Depends on:** L75-01, L75-02, L75-03 · **Spec:** §6.12, §6.3, §6.7, §12.1
**Parity (Appendix A):** `first-game-hint-host`, `table-first-game-hints`, `global-help-storage`, `global-client-storage`
**Files:** `apps/client/src/help/hint-overlay.tsx`, `apps/client/src/help/hint-overlay.test.ts`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/`, `apps/client/src/view-model/`, `DESIGN.md`, `docs/agent/decisions.md`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

Host the L46 hint overlay on the arena (§6.12), with `data-hint-anchor` on the equivalent elements: `your-turn` = top-bar turn banner, `draw` / `shop` = dock buttons, `hand` / `specials` = the two hand-fan groups (the card is placed beside them), `resources` = self-strip resource row, `incoming` and `incoming-thief` = the plate, `hidden-kit` = each living opponent seat whose kit is hidden, `reward` = the reward sheet or its minimized bar. The selector order, the gates (Classic, live, not eliminated, not walk-in) and the auto-Got-it causes stay unchanged. Those causes map to arena events: a playing intent, Draw, the Shop opening, the seat sheet of an opponent whose kit is visible, a hand or special card sheet, a threat clearing, a confirmed reward. Hints hide while any sheet or a non-reward sub-choice is up. `hidden-kit` counts Base-Spied seats as kit hidden, in both the selector input and the anchor (§6.3, Q66; `decisions.md` entry). The panel is restyled on the `ui/` coach chrome (compact, translucent) and keeps the `place-hint-card` rules. Update the `frontend.md` hint lines.

**Acceptance:** Behavior tests with fake views: each id renders next to its anchor in all three layout modes. A table whose only opponent is base-Spied still selects `hidden-kit`; a Spy+ one does not. Opening the Shop sheet hides the card and dismisses `shop`. The reward hint stays over the reward sheet. Tutorial rooms and the finished board show no hint. Skipping or finishing the tutorial never writes `skipAll` (L46-03). The `hint-overlay.tsx` assertions in `hint-overlay.test.ts` are rewritten as these behavior tests. `pnpm verify` green.
